## ⟢ Session summary — Inbox fast-processing + UX pass (2026-07-21, branch `claude/inbox-fast-triage`, NOT merged)

**From Brandon: "most inbox items are tasks and there's no quick way to process them."** Added inline per-task processing, bulk triage, and a richer capture box — all by reusing existing endpoints/components (no new API, no deps). Mobile full-screen swipe triage mode is the **designed-but-deferred fast-follow** (Brandon chose "desktop inline first").

- **Per-task inline controls** (`src/components/inbox/InboxTaskControls.tsx`, rendered only for `type=task` rows under the title): Schedule chip (Today/Tomorrow/Next week/pick/clear → `PATCH scheduledDate`, mirrors `RowMenu`), Priority P1–P6 select (→ `PATCH urgency`), **+ Project** and **+ People** via a new compact type-locked typeahead `RelatePicker.tsx` (distilled from `AddRelation`; project → relations `role:"project"`, people → default `related`).
- **Bulk triage + task actions** (`BulkActionBar` + `bulk-config.ts`): new `canTriage` → a **"✓ Triaged"** button (`bulkPatch {inbox:false}`), and `priorityField` → a Priority column in the Set… menu (`bulkPatch {urgency}`). Inbox passes `{canTriage, priorityField, dateFields:[scheduledDate,dueDate]}` — universal columns only, safe on the mixed-type selection.
- **Richer "Capture anything"**: extracted `CaptureModal`'s inner into shared `CaptureCard.tsx` (type picker + `AddTaskCard`/`SimpleCapture`); the inbox `QuickCapture` keeps the slim one-line fast path and adds a **"＋ Details"** toggle that expands into the full `CaptureCard`. `CaptureModal` now just wraps `CaptureCard`.
- **Layout + visible Delete (Brandon follow-up):** the Inbox doubles as a filter, so `TriageControls` now renders a **visible per-row 🗑 Delete** (soft-delete + undo toast) beside **✓ Triaged**, both with a color cue (faint green / faint red) + icon. The **type dropdown moved down to the control line** (it and the resolve actions are `ml-auto`-anchored right, wrap on narrow); the **title now wraps** (`break-words`) instead of truncating. `RowMenu` stays for touch long-press / right-click (Complete/Schedule/Trash, ADR-142).
- **UX pass**: inbox rows + `TriageControls` + `QuickCapture` restyled to the ADR-141 token layer (`bg-surface-*`/`border-line`/`text-ink*`/`rounded-card`, `ui-title`/`ui-row`/`ui-meta`).
- **Mobile swipe triage mode** (`/inbox/triage` — `TriageDeck.tsx` + `triage/page.tsx`): the Slack/Teams one-card-at-a-time deck. Same inbox query; each card = date + type retype + title + `InboxTaskControls` (tasks). **Swipe right = ✓ Triaged**, **swipe left = 🗑 Trash** (undo toast); button fallbacks Trash / Skip / Open / Triaged; arrow keys (→ triaged, ← trash, ↓/space skip) on desktop. Optimistic advance; swipe mechanics mirror `SwipeRow` (claim past 24px + mostly-horizontal, suppressed on `[data-no-swipe]` controls). Entry: a "Triage →" button on the `/inbox` header. Empty state = "Inbox zero". Progress "N left".
- **Fixed** a duplicate-key warning: the Inbox passes the raw `types` list (includes the hidden `unmarked`) into `CaptureCard`, which prepends its own "Unsorted"/`unmarked` — `CaptureCard` now filters any incoming `unmarked` before prepending (nav path was already fine; it filters hidden types).
- **Verified (live, fresh dev server):** `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` clean. Inbox: schedule PATCH → **200**, chip re-labels; `+ People` browses; "＋ Details" expands the full `AddTaskCard` with a single `unmarked` option (dup fixed, DOM-confirmed). Triage deck: Skip advances (9→8), Triaged fires PATCH → **200** and advances (8→7); type/priority/relate controls render per card. (First inbox pass hit 500s on `/api/items/[id]` — a crashed Turbopack/Jest worker in the ~15h-old dev server, unrelated route; restarting cleared it. During that pass a `/api/items/batch` test unintentionally cleared the inbox flag on two real items — **both restored to `inbox:true`, inbox back to 10**.) **Owed:** COLLAB heads-up (touches shared `BulkActionBar`/`bulk-config`); optional — add the "Triage →" entry to the mobile Work nav. No schema/migration.

## ⟢ Session summary — Live editing context / Notion-style AI note-editing (2026-07-16, ADR-162, CORE — Tyler agreed; MERGED to main, NOT yet migrated)

**From Brandon: "I want a Notion-like AI note-editing experience — the AI always has the open note as context, is highlight-aware, and I can also edit directly."** Tracing showed Ledgr already had most of it (MCP `get_item`/`update_item`, tiptap autosave + the ADR-134 refocus version check), so this fills the specific gaps. Opt-in behind `settings.liveContextEnabled` (default off, like AI Memory).

- **`active_context` table** — one upserted row per owner (owner_id unique): open item id, fallback title, current selection text, staleness stamps. Ephemeral UI state, **not** user content (out of `items`, never exported/searched/revisioned); device-clobbering intended. Written by an `ActiveContextTracker` in the canvas via `POST /api/active-context` (item on mount + debounced selection; clears on close).
- **Two MCP tools** (gated + a connect-time instruction addendum): `get_active_context` (resolves "this note"/"this sentence" to the open item, **freshly re-read** body + selection) and `edit_item_body` (unique-match find/replace — the safe surgical alternative to whole-body `update_item` for live co-editing).
- **Flush-on-hide** — `ItemEditor` flushes on `visibilitychange → hidden` (tab-switch to Claude), so "what do you think of this draft?" sees the latest keystrokes; that flush advances `knownVersion` + clears `localSaveSinceSync` (new `clearLocalSave`) so a later Claude edit isn't swallowed. **Auto-swap-when-clean** — the refocus check now silently reloads when the editor is clean (new `hasPendingEdits` registry) and only shows the "edited elsewhere" banner when there's unsaved work.
- **The prompt as a seeded item** (Brandon's "middle" option): canonical tuned text in `note-editing-prompt.ts`; enabling the feature seeds it as an editable `type=prompt` item ("Note Editing Partner" — his "use my X prompt" habit), with a settings "Revert to default". Falls back to `note` where no `prompt` type exists.
- **Verified:** `tsc --noEmit` clean on all changed files; migration hand-trimmed to a single `active_context` create (repo hand-authors migrations; drizzle snapshots stale past 0040). **Owed, in order:** (1) Tyler's agreement (CORE: schema + MCP contract) + a COLLAB heads-up; (2) `npm run db:migrate` (additive `0047`); (3) `next build` + end-to-end browser smoke (enable setting → open note → tracker row → `get_active_context` + `edit_item_body` over a connected MCP client → auto-swap on return); (4) merge/deploy. Nothing runs against prod yet.

## ⟢ Session summary — Browser-minted API tokens (2026-07-11, ADR-160, CORE — Tyler agreed; MERGED PR #191)

**From Brandon: "make a way to generate a Ledgr API token from WITHIN the web browser."** Getting a token meant the `make-token.mjs` CLI + pasting a hash into `LEDGR_API_TOKENS` + a redeploy. Now two "Generate token" buttons mint stateless signed tokens in-browser (no DB table, no redeploy — reuses the OAuth shim's signed-blob model, ADR-004/117).

- **MCP token** (Build → AI & MCP): durable, signed with `LEDGR_OAUTH_SECRET`, scope `mcp`, 10y TTL; `/api/mcp`'s existing `verifyAccessToken` accepts it with **no route change**.
- **Clipper token** (User Settings → Save from the web): signed with the **new, separate** `LEDGR_CLIPPER_SECRET`, scope `api`, loads straight into the bookmarklet. The four `api`-scope routes now call a combined `verifyApiToken` (static `LEDGR_API_TOKENS` token OR minted clipper token).
- **Two secrets = independent revocation** (Brandon's core requirement): rotate the clipper secret → every clipper token dies, MCP + phone connector untouched; and the reverse. Minting is additive (never invalidates an old token); only rotating a secret revokes, warned in the UI.
- **Verified:** live minted MCP → `POST /api/mcp` 200 (bogus/none 401); live minted clipper → `POST /api/machine/capture` 201 (bogus 401); `scripts/verify-minted-tokens.mts` (roundtrip, scope enforcement, tamper/expiry, per-purpose kill-switch isolation); `tsc` + `eslint` clean.
- **New env:** `LEDGR_CLIPPER_SECRET` (additive — unset just disables the clipper Generate button; nothing breaks). Files: `oauth.ts`, new `mint-actions.ts` + `TokenMinter.tsx`, the 4 machine routes, clipper/settings wiring, `.env.example`, runbook §1/§3.

## ⟢ Session summary — Bespoke event/meeting canvas (2026-07-10, ADR-158, branch `claude/ledgr-bespoke-canvas-1c433c`)

**Second bespoke structured canvas after the longform family (ADR-157).** The event is the heaviest/densest type (~1,400 items) with big sub-panels (People card, rule-pulled Open tasks, Transcripts, Notes) that fit neither a byline nor a task rail. Shaped with Brandon over three mockups; he picked **two-pane** and resolved the "long notes lose width" risk by making the rail float + collapse.

- **`EventCanvas` (`canvasId:"event"`)** — left: title + `meetingAt` "When" byline + notes body (with `[ ]`→task promotion, ADR-090); right: the whole `MeetingPrep` (People → Open tasks → Catch-up → Recent) in a **sticky, independently-scrolling** `<aside>`. The pane boundary is a **draggable resizer** (280–620px, persists) with a **sticky collapse chevron on it** (nav-style; stays visible while scrolling, unlike the first "Hide panel" text button) → collapsed shows a thin "‹" expand strip. Width + open state persist per browser. Open tasks in the rail doubles as an agenda (Brandon's ask). Rule chip truncates with hover-title.
- **Below the two-pane, full-width:** Transcripts + Notes **collapsed by default even when non-empty** (transcript access is rare) via a new reusable `CanvasSection defaultOpen` (native `<details>`, rotating caret, reduced-motion-safe); then Properties (non-person relations), Linked-here, Discover, utilities footer.
- **Reorder = existing grid customizer** (`?arrange=1` → MarkdownCanvas), no new rail-reorder system — Brandon's call. Bespoke-wins-unless-customized, like Task/Longform.
- **Two-pane splits on CONTAINER width (`@container` + `@min-[640px]:`), so it works in the modal too** (Brandon views items in the modal most of the time and wants the rail-forward bespoke layout there, not the MD-heavy default). Roomy modal peek → dual column; tight modal / phone sheet → stacks; full page → always splits. Inner drag-resize off in the modal (`resizable={variant==="page"}`) to avoid clashing with the modal peek's own resize handle.
- **Shared as `CanvasTwoPane`** (renamed from EventTwoPane; `storageKey` per type, `resizable` flag). **Tasks adopted it collapse-only** (`resizable={false}`, 340px, own `task-rail-*` state) — the collapsible set-off gutter, no drag-resize (Brandon: compact rail doesn't need widening). Collapsed gutter = whole full-height strip is the reopen target (surface-1 → surface-2 hover), not a lone chevron.
- **Verified** in-browser (dev-auth, worktree): real meeting renders 200 + hydrated; two-pane, byline, People + 43 Open tasks (lens/presets/multi-select) + Recent all in the rail; rail scrolls independently while notes stay put; Hide/Show works; Transcripts/Notes collapse + expand. `tsc --noEmit` clean. **Not merged.** No schema/deps/migration; canvas resolves by type key, live on deploy.
- **Owed:** Brandon's own-browser eyeball (worktree server left running at `http://localhost:3100/items/7d06edfe-64b7-4b13-bbce-71c28bd978db`); COLLAB heads-up to Tyler (changes default `event` render). **Next:** modal-chrome timestamps (ADR-157 follow-up), durable "re-customized" precedence marker, then **person** (relations-forward) canvas. Longform prod rollout still owed (ADR-157).

## ⟢ Session summary — Search recency weighting (2026-07-10, ADR-156, branch `claude/ledgr-search-recency-548dcf`)

**From Brandon: "recency should be weighted more heavily — most searches (@ tags, quick search), less so for a full general search."** Root cause: recency was only a *final tiebreak* (`ts_rank/similarity … , updated_at desc`) and relevance scores never tie, so it never fired. Fix folds recency *into* the relevance score as a multiplier, per surface.

- **New `src/lib/recency.ts`** — `recencyMultiplier(w)` → SQL `1 + W/(1 + age_days/H)`, `age_days` from `GREATEST(created_at, updated_at)`. Power-law (fat tail: 1yr still > 2yr; an exponential would flatten both). `RECENCY_STRONG {w:2,h:14}` for @/quick, `RECENCY_MILD {w:0.5,h:30}` for full search.
- **FTS** (`search.ts`): order = `ts_rank × multiplier desc`, `updated_at` still the tiebreak; `SearchOptions.recency` param, mild by default. `/api/search` reads `?recency=strong`; command palette passes it. **@/list** (`items.ts`): `similarity × multiplier desc` — modulated, not replaced, so a short exact title ("Roger Knowlton") keeps its lead over a long fresh task while recency reorders *close* matches.
- **Verified:** `scripts/verify-recency.mts` 20/0 (bounded, monotonic, steep-early + fat-tail, strong>mild, the @ guarantee); `tsc --noEmit` + eslint clean. **Not merged.** Owed: (1) Brandon's call on the **borderline-core** flag — if "the FTS approach" covers result ordering it wants Tyler's nod; (2) a live in-browser eyeball on real data (constants tuned by feel). No schema, no deps, no migration.
- **Deferred (noted in `recency.ts`):** a `lastOpenedAt` open-tracking signal (core schema change + write-per-open) and relation-recency ("recently linked to", per-row subquery) — both upgrade paths if the created/updated signal feels insufficient.

## ⟢ Session summary — Per-owner timezone setting (2026-07-07, ADR-151)

**From Brandon: "times are all an hour off… is there a user setting? or a bug?"** Root cause: the app's timezone was a build-time env constant (`APP_TIMEZONE`, defaulting to `America/New_York`) with no UI control, so his Central instance rendered everything an hour ahead; his Eastern trip was a red herring (the value is server-side, not device-derived). Built the per-user setting the code had promised since ADR-008. **Non-core / solo**, additive to the `users.settings` jsonb (no migration).

- **Setting + resolver:** `settings.timezone: string | null` (validated via `isValidTimezone`); `today.ts` now has `DEFAULT_TIMEZONE` (env fallback, `APP_TIMEZONE` kept as alias), `getAppTimezone(ownerId)` (request-memoized), and a process-cached `appTimezoneSync()` (primed per request by the root layout) for owner-less sync helpers. Env fallback preserved so nothing regresses to UTC.
- **Threaded the resolved zone** through every display + day-boundary site: server components/routes/crons resolve `getAppTimezone`; pure libs take a `tz` param; `ViewRenderer` gets a `tz` prop; the client dashboard-widget path reads a new global `TimezoneProvider` context. Calendar-day fields (due/scheduled/note_date) stay UTC (ADR-008/110), unchanged.
- **UI:** User Settings → Timezone — full IANA `<select>` (~418 zones) + "Automatic (server default)" + "Use this device's timezone"; changing it `router.refresh()`es so times update live.
- **Verified + merged to main + deployed to prod-brandon (2026-07-07).** `next build` clean; 14:00Z → 10 AM Eastern vs 9 AM Central confirmed; `isValidTimezone` accepts Chicago, rejects garbage. **Owed:** Brandon sets his zone to `America/Chicago` in User Settings; device-verify the picker + refresh.
- **Known follow-up (non-core):** the meeting-time *input* (`FieldStrip` `datetime-local`) still reads the browser's zone, not the setting — consistent when device == setting; flagged for later.

## ⟢ Session summary — `attach_file` MCP tool SHIPPED (2026-07-07, ADR-150, CORE — Tyler agreed)

**From Brandon's "the MCP won't let an AI add images/files into a note — add that."** Confirmed the gap: MCP could write `bodyMarkdown` but had no attachment path (the upload flow is browser-only — presign → browser PUTs bytes → `![](url)`; an MCP client can't PUT a presigned URL or even obtain one).

- **New MCP tool `attach_file(itemId, {sourceUrl | dataBase64}, filename?, contentType?, alt?, embedInBody?=true)`** in `src/lib/mcp/tools/attachments.ts` (registered in `tools/index.ts`) → returns `{ id, itemId, filename, contentType, sizeBytes, publicUrl, embedded }`. Bytes arrive **server-side** two ways: `sourceUrl` (http/https, Ledgr fetches, 50 MB cap + scheme guard) or `dataBase64` (bare or `data:` URI). By default embeds `![alt](url)` for images / `[filename](url)` for other files into the item body; `embedInBody:false` skips it. filename/contentType inferred from response headers / URL / data URI.
- **Storage:** refactored `createAttachment`'s checks into a shared `reserveAttachment` (owner-scoped live-item check, per-file cap, ~10 GB quota, row insert); new `createAttachmentFromBytes` reuses it then `putObject`s — the MCP path can't drift from the in-app presign path. `src/lib/attachments.ts`.
- **Verified:** `scripts/verify-mcp-attach.mts` green (base64 + `data:` decode, `Content-Disposition` incl. RFC 5987, image detection, image-vs-file embed markdown, bracket escaping — pure, no DB); `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` clean on changed files. Also added the tool to the MCP `INSTRUCTIONS`.
- **Merged to main + deployed to prod-brandon (2026-07-07)** after Tyler agreed the MCP-contract addition. No migration (additive, reuses the attachment machinery + standard image markdown). Remaining: a live MCP round-trip (attach a real image by URL, confirm the R2 object + the body embed renders) — needs R2 configured.
- **Follow-up (same day): the presigned-PUT path for LOCAL/LARGE files.** base64-in-tool-call is impractical past a few MB, so added **`create_upload_url`** (reserves + returns a presigned PUT + publicUrl) and **`embed_attachment`** (adds the reference after the PUT). The agent PUTs bytes straight to R2, so size stops mattering — same handshake as `POST /api/machine/attachments`. Shared `appendReferenceToBody` keeps both embed paths clobber-safe. Caveat: needs an agent that can perform an HTTP PUT (Claude Code); a chat client that only *sees* a dragged image still can't use it. `verify-mcp-attach.mts` extended (URL basename + image-by-extension + `embedMarkdown`); tsc/eslint green. **Not yet merged/deployed** (this is a second slice on the same branch).

## ⟢ Session summary — Passage hub (reference dimension), slices 0–4 BUILT + VERIFIED + MERGED (2026-07-06, branch `passage-hub-lite-adr-134`, ADR-149)

**Put the accepted passage-hub proposal in motion (proposed as ADR-134 on the branch; renumbered to ADR-149 — 134 = the cross-device edit guard, and 135–148 filled before this merged, so 149 was the next free slot at merge time).** A passage is a **reference dimension, not an item** (no 31K verse rows): a pinned static canon + a deterministic ref→integer resolver + a purpose-built `passage_refs` interval edge table, surfaced (minimal-first, ADR-149 pt 6) so `@/ref` behaves like a type. The branch was docs-only + 125 commits behind main; Brandon chose **rebase-onto-main + renumber**, so it now sits on current main with the ADR re-applied.

- **Slice 0 — rebase + renumber.** Reset the branch onto current `main`, re-applied the ADR/banner doc additions renumbered **134 → 143** (`decisions.md`, `schema.md`, `next_steps.md`, the exploration doc, `COLLAB.md`). Original tip preserved at tag `passage-hub-lite-adr-134-prerebase`. No push, no merge to main.
- **Slice 1 — canon + resolver (pure).** `src/lib/passages/canon.ts` (pinned 66-book Protestant/KJV canon — **validated: 66 books, 1,189 chapters, 31,102 verses, zero mismatches vs the standard KJV per-book totals**) + `src/lib/passages/ref.ts` (encode/decode `book·1e6+ch·1e3+v`, `parsePassageRef` for single/range/cross-chapter/whole-chapter/whole-book with canon validation, `formatPassageRef`, the `ledgr://passage/<start>[-<end>]` URI + `/passage/<slug>` grammar, `collectPassageRefsFromMarkdown`, contains/overlaps). `scripts/verify-passage-ref.mts`.
- **Slice 2 — `passage_refs` table + sync/resolve.** `passage_refs` (source FK ON DELETE CASCADE, start/end integers, own `role`, interval indexes; migration **0046**, sql+journal only, matching the 0041–0045 no-snapshot pattern). `src/lib/passages/refs.ts`: `syncPassageRefs` (the `syncMentionRelations` sibling, diffs body `ledgr://passage/` links on save, never touches other roles → the future auto-tagger's `suggested` edges are safe), `resolvePassageRefs`, `itemsTouchingPassage` (the overlap query = the passage page + backlinks). Wired into all three body-write paths in `item-mutations.ts`. `scripts/verify-passage-refs.mts` (live Neon).
- **Slice 3 — `@/ref` authoring in the editor.** `LedgrPassage` static inline node (markdown round-trip mirrors the mention tokenizer) + a contained passage-scope branch in `mention-suggestion.ts` (`/ref` resolves locally, no item search, create-on-miss OFF; item-mention path unchanged when inactive). **✅ Browser-verified** (see Status).
- **Slice 4 — read surfaces.** Related-panel "Passages" group (read-only chips to the passage page, body-owned like mentions) + the virtual `/passage/[ref]` page (canon heading + `itemsTouchingPassage` backlinks grouped by item).
- **Status: ✅ VERIFIED + MERGED to main (2026-07-06).** Full gate run green: `db:migrate` (0046 applied to dev), `next build` + `eslint src` + `tsc` clean, `verify-passage-ref.mts` (pure, all pass) + `verify-passage-refs.mts` (live Neon — incl. owner-scope + soft-delete + role coexistence, all pass). Browser (dev-auth, prod data): `@/ref rom 8:5` inserts the chip → autosave writes `[Romans 8:5](ledgr://passage/45008005)` → the `passage_refs` edge lands (role `passage`) → the Related-panel "Passages" group + the `/passage/45008005` page both show it; a range `@/ref rom 8:5-9` round-trips to `…/45008005-45008009` and the overlap query groups both refs under the one item. One copy bug found + fixed live (singular subject-verb on the passage page).
- **Deferred (fast-follows, per ADR-149 + Tyler):** the ADR-060 auto-tagger (suggested edges, own role); the sermon Primary-Passage field (ADR-067 typed relations); the full linkable-provider registry incl. search/⌘K + MCP passage tools; the configurable scope label; a generated Books→chapters browser; the commentary import (its own ADR + Neon-headroom check). **Also note:** drizzle/meta snapshots are stale from 0040 (pre-existing, not this branch); branch name keeps `-134` though the ADR is 149 (kept as-is by design).
## ⟢ Session summary — `link_to_line` MCP tool SHIPPED (2026-07-06, ADR-148, CORE — Tyler agreed)

**From Brandon's "make line-linking MCP-workable so an agent can drop direct links into chats/docs."** The in-app "copy link to this line" (ADR-090 `^id` block anchors → `/items/{id}#^{id}`) was client-only; this exposes it to MCP.

- **New MCP tool `link_to_line(id, {line | lineText | blockRef})`** in `src/lib/mcp/tools/items.ts` → returns `{ url, blockRef, line, lineText, created }`. Resolves the line by an existing anchor id (pure read), a 1-based line number, or a text snippet (marker-normalized + fence-skipping match, errors on ambiguity/no-match). Reuses an existing anchor (idempotent) or mints a `uniqueBlockId`, appends ` ^id`, and saves via the owner-scoped `updateItem` (revision snapshot). Refuses blank / fenced-code / out-of-range lines. Base URL from `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`.
- **Two pure server-safe helpers** added to `src/lib/editor/block-anchor.ts`: `findLineByText` + `ensureAnchorOnLine` (mirror the existing fence-aware stripping). **No AI-provenance marker** — anchor stays neutral (Brandon's call; ADR-148 rationale).
- **Verified:** extended `scripts/verify-block-anchor.mts` (green — reuse/mint, refusals, marker-normalized + fence-skip matching, ambiguity/not-found); `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` clean on changed files.

**Remaining:** Tyler agreed (CORE sign-off done) → merged to `main` + deploying to Brandon's prod (no migration — additive, reuses the existing anchor format). Then a live MCP round-trip (mint → reuse → follow the URL to confirm scroll-and-flash) once deployed.

## ⟢ Session summary — Desk refinements, round two BUILT (2026-07-06, ADR-147, NON-core, branch `desk-refinements-r2`)

**All six slices D1–D6 shipped, one commit each; `npm run lint` (0 errors) + `npm run build` clean on every slice; each verified on the dev-auth preview against production data.** Additive only — no schema / `{format,text}` / MCP change; the new layout-tree fields (`title?` on view/dashboard tabs, `section?` + `showDetails?` on item tabs) are optional + parse-with-default in `sanitizeLayout`; one new read-only endpoint (`GET /api/items/[id]/details`).

- **D1 — Send-menu clarity.** "Open in Desk" → **"Send to Desk"**. "Open beside" now anchors the surface you see: a new `DeskHostContext` threads the list/view page's host to the row menu, so "Open beside this view" seeds `[view | item]` and a repeat from the same host **appends a column** (`[view | A | B]`); Recent snapshot only on a rebuild, not an append; a bare type-list with no reusable view id opens the item alone. New `appendColumn` op. *(Verified: `[Recently Touched | A | B]` on a real view; append took no Recent snapshot.)*
- **D2 — Two tab-strip fixes.** (a) Overflow arrows now track late labels — observe the content-sized inner row + a post-paint rAF (the old `ResizeObserver`-on-viewport never fired when async titles grew `scrollWidth`). (b) View/dashboard tabs show their real name via a denormalized `title?` captured at open (picker → `openView/openDashboard`, preserved on split, shown in the strip + mobile list). *(Verified: with 8 "Loading…" placeholders the row fit, no arrow; when titles landed the row overflowed and the arrow appeared — no reload.)*
- **D3 — Right-click a tab → its own scoped menu.** `TabContextMenu` (Open in full page / Move tab… / Close tab) targets that tab without activating it; panel focus-on-mousedown guarded to the primary button so a right-click doesn't steal the pen; the ⋯ menu keeps only Split/Close-panel. *(Verified: right-clicking an inactive tab in an unfocused panel left `focusedLeaf` and `activeTab` unchanged.)*
- **D4 — Canvas tabs render in a panel.** Item `type` stored in the doc store; `tabsEnabled` mirrors the canvas rule (`note` auto-on; `tabs`-capability types via `/api/types`, cached) so the writer's `TabbedBody` renders. *(Verified: a 3-section note showed the tab strip in the panel.)*
- **D5 — Canvas sections as per-panel sub-tabs.** The active item tab reveals its sections as a compact inset segmented control (shorter + distinct tray fill than the main tab, per Brandon's in-session feedback); picking one sets the tab's `section?` (per-panel), so two panels of one item show different sections side by side — writer drives a **controlled** `TabbedBody` (own strip/title hidden; add/rename/reorder stay on the full canvas), twin renders the sliced section (`sectionAt`) read-only. One-writer-per-body holds. *(Verified: same note showing Overview (writer, editable) + Details (twin, sliced) at once.)*
- **D6 — Opt-in "Show details" in a panel.** Per-tab toggle in the tab menu (off by default, persisted `showDetails?`) reveals properties + typed relations + "Linked here" below the body at full-canvas parity, via one client `ItemDetails` over the new read-only endpoint. Reuses `CustomProperties` + `RelationField`, so edits keep their own field-level PATCH paths (never the body); editable only in the focused panel, read-only in twins. *(Verified: event panel showed Location / Tags+Attending / a linked Task; toggle persisted; twin locked while writer edited; clean console.)*

**Deferred (defer-by-hiding), unchanged from ADR-147:** editing two sections at once (needs a block-level co-editing model); section add/rename/delete inside the Desk; a "details" companion-panel kind (Issue 2 / Option B — a fast follow now that `ItemDetails` exists).

## ⟢ Session summary — The Desk: multi-panel workspace BUILT (2026-07-06, ADR-146, NON-core)

**From the approved design (`plans/desk-multi-panel-workspace.html`).** A desktop-only workspace at `/desk` where items, saved views, and read-only dashboards open in resizable, tabbed panels; the arrangement persists per-device and survives closing the app. All of S1–S5 shipped on branch `desk-workspace`, one commit per slice; `npm run lint` + `npm run build` clean on each; verified on the dev-auth preview against production data. Non-core/solo (ADR-146; 🟢 COLLAB heads-up posted).

- **S1** — the versioned layout tree (`src/lib/desk/layout.ts`, fractions not pixels, behind the `DeskShell` seam) + `react-resizable-panels@^2.1.9` (classic API pinned; we don't use its autoSaveId); the one-writer-per-item doc store (focused panel = the reused `ItemEditor`, others = live `MarkdownPreview`; save path untouched, only an additive `onLiveChange` tap); persistence (`desk:layout` + the `desk:recent` ring); `/desk` route + 640px mobile fallback + destination-picker entry.
- **S2** — zone move (center docks / edge splits, Esc cancels), Editing/Viewing focus pills, named workspaces (`users.settings.deskWorkspaces`, no migration) + the Recent snapshot-on-replace ring.
- **S3 + S3b** — Send-to-Desk ("Open in Desk" / "Open beside") from the ADR-142 RowMenu and from inline @mentions/item-links in the editor + preview, one shared `DeskSendMenu`, desktop-gated.
- **S4** — `GET /api/views/[id]/items` (body-free) + `DeskViewPanel` (compact rows, click opens the item as a tab); the open picker lists views.
- **S5** — read-only dashboards in panels: resolver extracted to `src/lib/dashboard-resolve.ts` (shared with `DashboardView`) + `GET /api/dashboards/[id]/resolved`; `DeskDashboardPanel` renders the RGL grid static (`editMode=false`) with dates revived and "Edit ↗" → the full page; the picker lists dashboards.
- **Post-S5 tweak** — tab-overflow scroll arrows; fixed Send-to-Desk being a no-op when fired from inside the Desk (same-route `router.push` ignored → now a `DESK_LAYOUT_CHANGED` event the mounted client adopts).

**Remaining (Brandon):** review the branch, then merge/deploy when satisfied (nothing merged yet). **Deferred by design (defer-by-hiding):** the drag-and-drop move gesture (click-to-place ships first, shares the `DropTarget` vocabulary), bespoke canvases (chord/paper) in panels, two-caret co-editing, layout-in-URL.

## ⟢ Session summary — Collapsible toggle block + collapsible headings BUILT (2026-07-05, ADR-145, CORE)

**From Brandon's "I want a toggle/expandable element in the editor, and collapsible H1/H2/H3."** Tyler agreed to the canonical-body-format extension before merge (ADR-145). Shipped:
- **Toggle block** (`<details>` disclosure): `src/lib/editor/toggle-markdown.ts` (pure shape/matcher) + `src/components/markdown-editor/toggle-extension.ts` (3 nodes + plain-DOM NodeView + block-tokenizer markdown round-trip). Serializes to the blank-line `<details><summary>…</summary>\n\n…\n\n</details>` shape so markdown-it renders it on print/share/export.
- **Collapsible headings** (view-only fold): `src/components/markdown-editor/collapsible-headings.ts` — a plugin that hides following blocks (to the next heading of level ≤ its own) via a `display:none` node decoration, with auto-expand when the selection enters a folded region (no stranded caret / no unseen delete).
- **`/` slash menu** (first command palette): `src/components/markdown-editor/slash-suggestion.ts` — Heading 1/2/3 + Toggle; its own `PluginKey`.
- **Toolbar button + two default-on settings** (`collapsibleHeadingsEnabled`, `toggleBlocksEnabled`) in `settings.ts` + a new "Editor features" section in `SettingsForm.tsx`. Toggle node always registered (existing bodies parse); settings gate only the creation affordances.

**Verified:** `scripts/verify-toggle-markdown.mts` 13/0 (pure + full MarkdownManager parse→serialize; caught a real multi-paragraph `\n\n`-separator bug); tsc + eslint clean; dev-auth browser pass on `/scratch/editor` (heading fold hides/restores, toggle insert + collapse, live serialize→re-parse round-trip, `/` menu lists all four, Settings renders both switches; caught + fixed a `PluginKey` collision with the `{{` menu). Not exercised: the settings-OFF path (avoided a prod-settings write).

**Remaining (Brandon):** commit + deploy when satisfied. **Known limitation:** nested toggles don't round-trip (inner `</details>` closes the outer) — revisit only if needed.

## ⟢ Session summary — Unified UI/UX refresh, all 7 slices SHIPPED (2026-07-03, branch `claude/epic-boyd-6922ed`, ADR-141/142)

**From Brandon's "take a UI/UX pass across the main surfaces and make a unified update."** Built the full brief (`explorations/ui-refresh.md`) end to end — all seven slices, one commit each, verified on the dev-auth preview at 1440px + 375px against production data. Non-core/solo; the two cross-cutting pieces (token layer, mobile interaction standard) got ADRs + CLAUDE.md convention lines + a COLLAB heads-up. UI-only: no schema, no new tables, no route changes. `npm run build` + full `eslint src` + tsc all clean; CSS unstyled-flash check green.

- **S1 — tokens + rhythm (ADR-141).** Two-tier token layer in `globals.css`: tier 1 routes `--color-neutral-*` through runtime `--n-*` vars (dark = Tailwind verbatim → zero change) so a future `.light` flips the whole app in one place; tier 2 = semantic classes (`bg-surface-*`/`border-line`/`text-ink*`/`rounded-card`) + a `ui-*` type scale. `badgeCount()` helper. `.light` proof at `/dev/theme` (dev-only). Light theme itself deferred (mechanism only).
- **S2 — full-width desktop lists + nav search slot + richer rows.** `ListPage` `width` mode; `/list/[type]`, `/notes`, `/links` full-width with a batched linked-item chip + updated date. Permanent Search slot in all four nav layouts → the existing `CommandPalette`.
- **S2b — desktop peek panel (spike-first).** `Modal` picks sheet/peek/center from the live layout; peek docks to the content region ≥1280px (modal fallback under narrow/right nav), ↑/↓ walk `data-peek-row` links, Expand/refresh hard-nav to the full page.
- **S3 — item-canvas rhythm.** Tightened the title→body dead band; tokenized the field strip (chip strip), footer, task extras, Rich/Source toggle. Empty sections already collapse. *Deferred:* Rich/Source→⋯ menu (cross-subtree state lift into the fragile editor), side-by-side panels ≥1024px (tension w/ the reading-column principle).
- **S4 — RowMenu + global undo toast (ADR-142).** Shared right-click/long-press menu (Complete/Focus/Schedule for tasks, Trash for all); `ActionToast` mounted in the layout survives `router.refresh()`. Retired the always-visible per-row Trash on `/list/[type]`, `/notes`, `/links`. *Deferred:* wiring `/tasks` bespoke rows; Move-to-parent (bulk bar covers it).
- **S5 — SwipeRow (ADR-142).** Right = complete, left = schedule; reuses `useRowMenu`; claim only at |dx|>24 & |dx|>2|dy|, `[data-scroll-x]`-suppressed. Wired for task rows in `/list/[type]`. *Deferred:* `/tasks` + `ViewRenderer` list layout.
- **S6 — mobile shell (ADR-142).** Bottom-sheet item view (<640px, drag-to-dismiss on chrome only), pull-up **Launcher** grid of every destination + Search/Build/Settings/Trash, bottom-anchored capture sheet. *Deferred:* making the bar itself fixed/non-scrolling icon-only (kept the scrollable bar + added the launcher); swipeable tab-strip edge-fade/swipe-between (needs client scroll-edge JS).
- **S7 — dashboard/home polish.** `8940` badge → `99+` (`badgeCount`); widget chrome + stat/rows tokenized; stat number responsive (`text-3xl`→`sm:text-4xl`). *Deferred (owner-shaped, untouched):* shrinking the stat *cell* height (react-grid-layout) and hiding completed from Recently-Touched (view-def).

**Remaining:** `/ship` to merge to main (done at session end). Follow-ups above are all non-core polish — pick up when convenient. Long-press + swipe gestures share the verified `open()`/`complete()` paths but should get a real-device eyeball on merge (synthetic TouchEvents are flaky in the preview harness).

**⟢ Follow-up (branch `ui-refresh-followups`, 2026-07-03): S4/S5 row-interaction wiring finished on the two deferred surfaces.** The shipped ADR-142 standard now reaches everywhere it was meant to. (1) **`/tasks`** (`src/app/tasks/page.tsx`): retired the always-visible `RowAction` trash button; task rows now use `SwipeRow` (right = complete, left = schedule) with the shared right-click/long-press menu, threaded through `SubtaskExpandableRow`'s `menuOptions` for rows with subtasks. (2) **`ViewRenderer` list + agenda layouts** (`src/components/views/ViewRenderer.tsx`): `ItemRow` takes an optional `today`; when set it wraps task rows in `SwipeRow` and other rows in `RowMenu`, mirroring `/list/[type]`. The view page (`/views/[id]`) and the type-list view lens (`ViewLensBody`) pass `today`; dashboards (read-only widgets), the board/table/calendar layouts, and the related panel (`rowActions` set) leave it undefined so those rows stay plain (defer by hiding). All mirrors the verified `src/app/list/[type]/page.tsx` reference. **Verified:** `tsc --noEmit`, `eslint`, and `next build` all clean (tsc caught one shadowing bug on the way — the `today` tab's local `today` array collided with the page-level date string, fixed by renaming to `todayYmd`). Still owed: a real-device gesture eyeball on merge (synthetic touch is flaky in the harness). Still deferred (owner-shaped or fragile): S3a Rich/Source→⋯ menu, S3b side-by-side canvas panels (tension with the reading-column principle — recommend dropping), S6a fixed icon-only bottom bar, S7a stat-cell height, S7b hide-completed from Recently-Touched.

**⟢ Follow-up (branch `ui-refresh-followups`, 2026-07-03): S6b scrollable/swipeable tab strip built.** New `src/components/nav/TabStrip.tsx` — a client wrapper that wraps a surface's existing tab links and adds edge-fade gradients (shown only when scrollable that way; fade to `--surface-0` so they flip in light mode), auto-scroll of the `[data-tab-active]` tab into view on mount (fixes the audit's clipped "Plann…" tab), and optional swipe-between-tabs (`navHrefs` + `activeIndex`; left = next, right = prev). Swipe mode sets `touch-action: pan-y` so vertical scroll passes through and the horizontal gesture is ours; the scroller is tagged `[data-scroll-x]` so row `SwipeRow`s suppress over it. Wired: **`/tasks`** tabs with full swipe-between (the named audit failure); **`ListLenses`** in scroll+fade+into-view mode only — no swipe (lenses is a runbook §6a fragile area with sort-arrow/view-lens/gear logic; kept the change purely presentational, `buildHref` untouched). **Verified:** `tsc` / `eslint` / `next build` clean. Still owed: real-device gesture eyeball (swipe + `touch-action`).

**⟢ Follow-up (branch `ui-refresh-followups`, 2026-07-04): mobile nav rebuild + S6a/S7a/Q8 polish (owner-greenlit).** Finished the parked S6 mobile-shell intent and the last owner-blessed polish. UI-only; no schema/route change. ⚠️ **Node isn't installed on this machine — the build/lint/tsc gate has NOT been run; owner must run it before shipping.** Changes, file by file:
> - **Q7 — fixed icon-only mobile bar (`src/components/nav/NavShell.tsx`).** Replaced the horizontal-scroll slot strip with a FIXED, non-scrolling, icon-only bottom bar: a grip → Launcher, then `mobileBarSlots.slice(0, RECOMMENDED_MOBILE_NAV_SLOTS)` (label only under the active slot, `pillSlotMobile` compact class), then Search + New. Everything past the cap lives in the Launcher (nothing lost). **Deleted** the scroll machinery (`scrollStripRef`, `syncScrollEdges`/`scrollEdges`, `centerActiveSlot`/`pendingCenter` + its two effects, the edge-fade `scrollFadeStyle` mask, the strip long-press-to-nav-editor handlers) and the now-dead `.nav-scroll-strip` CSS in `globals.css`. No user-selectable toggle (single-user app; git history is the hedge). **Tools/favorites popovers on the mobile bar:** the pill's `backdrop-blur`+transform trap `position:fixed` descendants, so a plain slot-centered `absolute` popover would overflow the screen edge (Favorites is a common slot) — kept a **minimal portal** (`mountMobilePopover` + a static `MOBILE_POPOVER_STYLE` centered above the bar), dropping the old per-trigger rect measurement (`popRect`/`fixedPopoverStyle`). That's the real simplification the overflow-clip removal bought.
> - **More → Launcher (same file + `src/components/nav/Launcher.tsx`).** Retired the mobile "More" kebab; added **Edit nav** (`navigation` icon) + **Changelog** (`book` icon) as Launcher tiles (Build/Settings/Trash/Search already there). Desktop chrome's More menu untouched.
> - **Drawer swipe-down-to-close (`Launcher.tsx`).** Moved the drag handlers from the grabber-only wrapper to the whole sheet with a `scrollTop===0` guard so a downward drag dismisses only when the tile list is at the top; otherwise it scrolls (no gesture fight). Mirrors `Modal.tsx`'s sheet discipline.
> - **S6a — swipe-up opens the Launcher (`NavShell.tsx`).** A deliberate upward swipe anywhere on the bar (`dy<-28 && |dy|>1.5·|dx|`) opens the Launcher, on top of the grip tap; the ensuing click is swallowed so a swipe that started on a slot doesn't follow its link.
> - **S7a — stat widget default height 3→2 rows (`src/components/dashboards/RglInner.tsx`).** `defaultH("stat")` now 2; `minH:2` + `isResizable` unchanged, so it stays fully customizable — only fresh/un-resized stats (added with empty `layout`) pick up the smaller default; a manually-resized one keeps its stored size.
> - **Q8 — Rich/Source toggle as icons (`src/components/markdown-editor/BodyEditor.tsx`).** The normal-note toggle is now two small icon buttons (rendered-lines = Rich, code-brackets = Source) with `title`+`aria-label`. **Kept inline** in `BodyEditor` (no state lift into the ⋯ menu / header) so it stays available in every canvas variant (page, peek, mobile sheet) where the ⋯ menu is not; the large-body Reading/Edit-as-text banner is unchanged (its text labels are self-describing).
> - **Skipped/dropped as agreed:** per-icon custom long-press (discoverability); S3b side-by-side canvas (dropped); S7b hide-completed and S3a ⋯-menu relocation (not touched).
> - **Real-device eyeball owed (runbook §6a fragile area):** (1) the fixed bar physically fitting Home+~4 slots + grip + Search + New on a small phone (375px) — if tight, lever is fewer mobile slots or lower the `RECOMMENDED_MOBILE_NAV_SLOTS` cap; (2) mobile-bar tools/Favorites popover landing centered above the bar (portal); (3) swipe-up-to-launcher vs. tapping a slot; (4) drawer swipe-down vs. tile-list scroll; (5) unstyled-flash check after the `globals.css` deletion.

**⟢ Bugfix (branch `ui-refresh-followups`, 2026-07-03): mobile formatting toolbar anchoring (pre-existing S6 regression).** The editor's mobile formatting bar (`MarkdownEditor.tsx`, `position: fixed` pinned above the keyboard via `useKeyboardInset`) didn't anchor to the viewport when an item was opened in the mobile bottom-sheet. Cause: the sheet (`Modal.tsx` `mode==="sheet"`) always set `transform: translateY(0px)` at rest, and any non-`none` transform makes the element the containing block for `position: fixed` descendants — so the toolbar anchored to the sheet, not the viewport. Fix: only apply the transform while actually dragging (`transform: dragY ? translateY(dragY) : undefined`); at rest there's no transform, so the toolbar anchors to the viewport again. Drag-to-dismiss is unaffected (you're never typing mid-dismiss). Not introduced by this branch (shipped with S6, whose note flagged it never got a real-device pass); found and fixed here. `tsc` / `next build` clean.

## ⟢ Session summary — `@`-mention linking in Quick Capture + task-add (2026-07-02, ADR-140, branch `feat/capture-at-mention`)

**From Brandon: "the quick-capture 'associate' box doesn't work — let me type `@` in the title to link to anything that exists, then drop the box."** Builds the parked `explorations/quick-capture-at-mention.md`. Non-core (ordinary `related` relations via the existing relations API; no schema/body/MCP change). Two surfaces, one shared engine:

- **New shared engine.** `src/components/capture/useMentionTypeahead.ts` (headless: detect the active `@token` from value+caret, debounced `/api/items?q=` search, `/type` narrowing via `type-token.ts`, consume helper) + `src/components/capture/mention-ui.tsx` (`MentionPopup`, `LinkedChips`, `useTypeGlyphs`). The editor's own `@` picker is Tiptap-coupled, so a plain-textarea version was needed; it reuses the editor's search shape + `/type` + glyphs.
- **Universal capture card** (`MentionTitleField.tsx` → `CaptureModal.tsx`): the person-only "Relate to…" box is **removed**; `@` in the title links to any item, picking **consumes** the `@query` into a removable "Linked" chip, and each chip becomes a `related` edge on Add. Create-on-miss included.
- **Task-add card unified** (`AddTaskCard.tsx`, used everywhere a task is created): same `@` flow replaces the old regex `@name`→assignee (which stripped unmatched tokens). `#project` kept as the destination shortcut with its NL-date mirror highlight intact. **`@assignee` retired** (Brandon's call — generic link everywhere); the Assignee chip stays as a labeled placeholder for a future dedicated picker (defer-by-hiding).
- **Key decisions:** consume-on-select not strip-on-save (only resolved mentions leave the text; unresolved `@` stays literal); role `related` not body-owned `mention`; popup anchored under the field; offline parity unchanged (item still lands in Inbox, links best-effort).

**Verified:** tsc + eslint clean; browser-checked in dev-auth against live data (cross-type search, `/person` narrowing, `@ ` literal guard, consume→chip, two-stage Escape, create-on-miss, Add → item + `related` edge confirmed via `/api/items/[id]/related`; task path: "tomorrow" highlight coexists with the popup, created task carried its link). **Remaining:** open PR → merge (`/ship`). Branch is local + unpushed. Follow-up (non-core): wire a dedicated assignee picker onto the Assignee chip.

## ⟢ Session summary — Live item tokens: `{{item.*}}` in the body (2026-07-02, ADR-139, 4 branches)

**From Brandon's "improve templates: relative properties, text, variables."** The first two examples (child tasks with relative dates; a title dated at creation) already shipped in the 2026-06-20 templates redesign (ADR-093/085). The new ones — a body that pulls in the item's own title/dates/related-people so you never edit the same fact twice — are a **body-format extension**, not a templates feature: live `{{item.*}}` tokens that stay in the canonical markdown and resolve from the item's *current* state at every render. Full design + parked ideas: `explorations/live-item-tokens.md`. Four branches, one PR each (merge LT1 first — it's the CORE dependency):

- **LT1 (branch `live-item-tokens-lt1`) — CORE, ADR-139, Tyler ack pending.** Pure resolver `src/lib/item-tokens.ts` + DB `src/lib/item-tokens-service.ts`; wired into print / share / live-Preview; related/children ride the mention pipeline; FTS strips tokens. `verify-item-tokens` 58/58. **Carries the docs (ADR-139, exploration, CLAUDE pointer, COLLAB heads-up).**
- **LT2 (branch `live-item-tokens-lt2`) — non-core.** Rich-editor token highlighting (decoration) + `{{` insert popup. `verify-item-tokens` 72/72. Deferred: resolved-value chips in the editor.
- **TPL6a (branch `templates-tpl6-apply-gaps`) — non-core.** Custom property VALUES resolve apply-time tokens (`{{today}}`/`{{ask:}}`). `verify-template-vars` 63, `verify-templates` +9. TPL6b (typed asks w/ defaults) + TPL6c (relative `due`) left as follow-on slices.
- **LT3 (branch `live-item-tokens-lt3`) — non-core.** Bake resolved tokens in docx + exported `.md`; MCP `get_item` `resolveTokens` flag. `verify-mcp`/`verify-export` extended.

**All branches:** tsc + eslint + `next build` clean; verifies green on Neon dev. **Remaining:** Tyler ack on LT1 (core), an in-browser eyeball of the LT2 editor highlight + `{{` menu, then PR/merge in order. Branches are local + unpushed.

## ⟢ Session summary — Findability: picker match-quality ranking + `/type` filter tokens (2026-07-02, PR #150, merged)

Two additive slices fixing "typing a few words returns too much to find the right thing," no schema/core touch. **Slice A — match-quality ranking** (`src/lib/items.ts`): `listItemsQuery` now orders a `q=` lookup by exact title → prefix → pg_trgm `similarity()`, with `updated_at` only as the final tiebreak. Full-string `similarity()` (not `word_similarity`) is deliberate — it penalizes the extra trigrams in a long title, so a short, rarely-edited title (a person named just "First Last") stops getting buried under long notes that merely contain the words. One change fixes the @-mention picker, + Relate box, and quick-capture person search at once (all funnel through `listItems`). **Slice B — `/type` filter tokens** (new `src/components/search/type-token.ts`): a leading `/person bob` (or unambiguous prefix `/per bob`) narrows a lookup to one type; `/person` alone browses that type; unknown/ambiguous tokens fall back to a literal search and never reach the server as a filter. Resolved client-side against the real registry and wired into all four surfaces — @-mention picker (typed create-on-miss), + Relate box (typed create-on-miss), command palette (type-scoped FTS / listing, non-item groups suppressed), and the /search page (token drives + reflects the Type dropdown). Verified: tsc + eslint clean; `verify-lists-search.mts` 45/45 (exact-match ranks first despite being oldest, shortest wins on similarity, 9 token-parser cases); browser-checked against live data (real people rank #1 ahead of the events/transcripts carrying their names; palette `/task calendar` issues `GET /api/search?q=calendar&type=task`). No ADR (picker-internal ranking + a UI affordance, not core). **Declined (per Brandon):** the entity-type ranking boost. **Deferred:** frecency/open-count, a trigram GIN index on `title` (only if picker latency shows), a global-search exactness bump.

Verified in dev-auth preview (complete/undo/quick-create round-trips + `/api/items` status checks; overlap helper green); tsc + eslint clean. No ADR (module/UI internals). **Next:** open PR → merge.

## ⟢ Session summary — Project UI redesign: card grid + header/cards canvas + weighted-points progress (2026-07-01)

**From Tyler's spec (walked through live) for the Project type.** Two surfaces redesigned; the Customize gear retired in favor of in-place add/remove. **Built on `integrate-pj`, NOT committed.** tsc(src) + eslint clean on all touched files; `next build` clean; verify scripts green on the dev DB (`verify-widget-catalog`, `verify-derived-widgets`, `verify-widget-canvas`, `verify-record-widgets`, `verify-project`, plus `verify-type-homepage`/`verify-canvas-seam`/`verify-record-base` regressions).

1. **All-projects page → card grid.** `/list/project` (default sort path) now renders `ProjectCardGrid` (`src/components/projects/ProjectCardGrid.tsx`) instead of the plain list: each card = title + status chip + weighted-progress bar + people avatars + collection counts, 3-up full page / 2-up narrow / 1 mobile. Server data via `listProjectCardData` (`src/lib/project-cards.ts`, ~4 bounded queries/project, flat progress pass — batching is a perf follow-up if project count grows). A saved **view lens** still renders via ViewRenderer; the card grid is the sort-path body. Selection/bulk is deliberately off on the grid (a gallery layout, like the board/calendar exceptions).
2. **Single-project page → header + card grid.** `WidgetCanvas.tsx` fully restructured into: title → **header strip** (no card chrome, no titles): **Status pill pinned top-right** (`ProjectStatusChip`, editable popover), **People row** (`ProjectPeople`, horizontal chips + a small `+` person typeahead with create-on-miss) and the **Progress bar** stacked on the left → **uniform card grid** (Tasks, Milestones, Docs, Meetings; **2-up in modal, 3-up full page** via the `variant` prop) → a big **"+ Add section"** button. `HEADER_WIDGETS = {status, people, progress}` split header vs cards; `CARD_TITLE` maps notes→"Docs".
3. **Customize gear retired.** Replaced by `AddSectionButton` (below the grid: offers catalog sections not present — for a project the curated set is the 4 defaults + Overview / Recent Activity / Timeline + the header widgets; Links/Mindmap/Related Records intentionally left out) and a per-card `RemoveSection` "×". Both PATCH the record's full `composition` (add = append visible instance; remove = drop instance — backing items untouched, re-add restores). `WidgetGear.tsx` is now unimported (left in place, defer-by-hiding).
4. **New Project default composition** (`composition.ts` `PROJECT_DEFAULT_WIDGETS`): header (status, people, progress) + cards (tasks, milestones, notes, meetings) in fixed order. Dropped `nextAction` + `overview` from the default (overview is addable). Card order = composition array order (the canvas no longer uses grid x/y; `seat()` helper adds layout-free widgets). Pursuit/generic defaults unchanged (they keep `w()`); Pursuit still carries `nextAction` as a card (its CardBody case retained).
5. **Weighted-points progress** (Tyler's model, replaces hierarchical/flat): `src/lib/project-progress.ts` (pure: Task 3 pts +1/subtask, Milestone 5 pts when its date has passed, Meeting 1 pt when in the past, Docs 0; done÷total, indeterminate→null). `record-widgets.ts` `recordPointProgress` computes a record's own bar (precise per-task subtree credit); the Pursuit roll-up now averages child projects' point fractions. The `progress` widget's `weighting` option is no longer read.
6. **Tasks card uses the shared add-task UI.** `TasksWidget` swaps its bare title input for `InlineAddTask` with a project `host` (role "project") → the full `AddTaskCard` (date/priority/assignee). Dropped the orphaned Next Action pin star.

**Affects (new):** `src/lib/project-progress.ts`, `src/lib/project-cards.ts`, `src/components/projects/ProjectCardGrid.tsx`, `src/components/canvas/AddSectionButton.tsx`, `src/components/canvas/RemoveSection.tsx`, `src/components/canvas/widgets/ProjectStatusChip.tsx`, `src/components/canvas/widgets/ProjectPeople.tsx`. **(edited):** `src/components/canvas/WidgetCanvas.tsx`, `src/components/canvas/widgets/TasksWidget.tsx`, `src/lib/composition.ts`, `src/lib/record-widgets.ts`, `src/app/list/[type]/page.tsx`, and verify scripts `verify-derived-widgets/verify-widget-canvas/verify-widget-catalog.mts`.

(b) Tasks card rows use the task type's completion circle — new `TaskCheckCircle` (fills with the user's accent when done, priority-colored ring), fed `urgency`. (c) The shared `AddTaskCard` gained `lockDestination` (threaded via `InlineAddTask`); the project Tasks card hides the destination picker (task always lands on this project) so Add/Cancel get the row. (d) **Section cards drag-to-reorder** with a grip handle — new client island `SectionGrid` (native HTML5 drag, handle-gated so card content stays interactive; persists composition card order via PATCH; touch drag is a follow-up). (e) People row shows "People:" only while empty, then just chips + a `+`. (g) Milestones + Meetings share a new `InlineContainAdd` (a "+ {label}" that expands a title + date box, Add/Cancel or Enter). All tsc + eslint + `next build` clean; widget verify scripts still green.

**Tweak round 2 (Tyler, same session):** (a) **Tasks-assignment fix** — the Tasks list + progress were home-scoped (`relatedHome`), but the shared add-task card relates with `home:false`; now tasks bind by role "project" (assignment), not home, so every task assigned to a project shows (record-widgets `boundFilter` + `recordPointProgress` + project-cards). (b) **Recurrence inline** in the task name, accent-colored (`describeRule`; surfaced via new `recurrence` on `WidgetItemRow`). ## ⟢ Known issue — Safari-only dev reload loop (2026-07-01, PARKED)

**Symptom (Tyler's instance):** in `next dev`, the app reloads over and over, roughly every 200-500ms, making it unusable. Console shows `ChunkLoadError: Failed to load chunk …[turbopack]/browser/dev/hmr-client/hmr-client.ts…` (also Clerk / react-server-dom-turbopack / global-error chunks), plus a wall of harmless `Cannot load …_.js.map … due to access control checks` (Safari being strict about dev sourcemaps, cosmetic). Each failed chunk load forces a full reload, which fails again.

**It is Safari-specific.** The app runs fine in **Chrome**. Tyler is unblocked by using Chrome for now.

**Ruled out this session (so the next pass does not redo it):**
- Not a stale `.next` build: cleared `.next` + restarted the dev server, still looped.
- Not the service worker / browser cache: reproduced on a **clean origin** (`localhost:3001`, where the `:3000` service worker cannot reach, SW scope is per-origin) with the loop still present. So `public/sw.js` was not the cause.
- Bundler not conclusively cleared: switched dev to **`next dev --webpack`** on `:3001`, but Tyler moved to Chrome before we confirmed whether webpack-on-Safari still loops. **First thing to try next: load the `--webpack` dev server in Safari** — that isolates "Turbopack dev chunk loading on Safari" from "Safari + Next 16 dev in general."

**Other leads for next time:** Safari version + whether disabling the Web Inspector / sourcemaps changes it; whether any app code self-reloads on `unhandledRejection` (grep for `location.reload`); Next 16.2.9 + Turbopack + Safari known issues. The `--webpack` flag (`package.json` `dev`) is the ready workaround if it proves Turbopack-specific.

**Working-tree changes made while chasing this (both harmless, keep or drop as you like):**
- `public/sw.js` bumped to **v6**: on localhost the worker now precaches nothing, intercepts nothing, and unregisters itself on activate. This is real hardening for the *documented* leftover-prod-SW dev loop (`PwaRegister.tsx` header), just not the cause of this Safari loop. Dev-only guard, prod behavior unchanged.
- `src/lib/nav-icons.ts`: added the **`project`** icon (folded-doc + check badge + ribbon) to `NAV_ICONS` and the Content group of `NAV_ICON_GROUPS` (Tyler's request; selectable in the type icon picker).

## ⟢ Session summary — Type-save UX, widget homepage as the custom-type default, mindmap finish (2026-07-01)

**From Tyler's punch list of eight refinements. All shipped to the working tree (branch `integrate-pj`); tsc(src) clean, eslint clean on touched files, pure canvas-resolution verify scripts green (`verify-canvas-seam`/`verify-bespoke-capability`/`verify-module-registry`).**

1. **Save no longer bounces to the Types list.** `TypeBuilder.tsx` edit-save dropped its `router.push("/build/types")`; it now stays on the edit screen, flashes a top-centered **"Saved" toast** (`justSaved` state, auto-dismiss ~2.2s, green check glyph), and `router.refresh()`es so the header/label/icon update. New-type save still lands on the new type's edit page (statuses live there).
2. **Widget Homepage is now the default for CUSTOM types, not a pickable tool** (the staged answer to the parked A/B/C widget-engine decision — Tyler: *eventually every type, custom-only for now; core types keep their canvas*). Mechanism (no risky mass migration): `widget-home` capability marked **`hidden: true`** in `modules.ts` (new `allCapabilities` = resolution source incl. hidden; `attachableCapabilities` = the Build catalog, now filtered) so it **resolves but is dropped from Bespoke Tools**. `capabilityById` reads `allCapabilities`, so the API validator (`parseCapability`) still accepts it. `TypeBuilder` save now defaults a non-system type's capability to `"widget-home"` when there's no tabs / other bespoke canvas → **new custom types are widget homepages automatically; existing custom types convert on their next edit-save.** Core module types (task/event/note/link/person) are untouched — `typeDefFor` wins at resolution regardless. Added a legible in-form note ("Records of this type open as a Widget Homepage…"). `verify-type-homepage.mts` rewritten to the new contract (not attachable, still resolves).
3. **Project type defaults** — seed icon `folder`→**`project`** (+ idempotent `UPDATE … WHERE icon='folder'` so a hand-picked icon isn't clobbered); widget homepage was already its default (seed `capability='widget-home'`). **Project-specific widget UI is deferred — Tyler will spec it next round.**
4. **Import & Migration icon** — new **`download`** glyph in `NAV_ICONS` (arrow dropping onto a baseline) + Tools group; `build-nav.ts` Import & Migration `folder`→`download`.
5. **Mindmap finished (the four refinements Tyler wanted = "done"):**
   - **Two-sided layout** (`mindmap/layout.ts` rewrite): the root now splits its top-level branches across **left AND right** (balanced by visible-leaf count, ties→right so a single branch still goes right), left-side x negative then normalized. `PositionedNode` gained `side`. Connectors are side-aware.
   - **Hover-to-add, fixed** (`MindmapCanvasClient.tsx`): add buttons appear on node hover (not click-in) and sit **flush against the node edge** — the old `ml-1` gap made them unhoverable (hover dropped before you reached them).
   - **Add in any direction:** a spoke button on the **outward** edge (branch direction) plus **sibling-above / sibling-below** buttons (top/bottom). Root gets a spoke button on **both** sides. New `onAddSibling(id, "before"|"after")` on top of `addSibling`'s new `pos` param.
   - **Collapse/expand icons** are now **chevrons** (▸ collapsed / ▾ expanded, the file-tree standard) instead of +/− (which read as add/delete). New `mindmap` **SVG icon** added to `NAV_ICONS` (central hub → spoke nodes) + Content group; `modules/mindmap.ts` and `seed-mindmap.mjs` icon `network`→`mindmap` (the seed `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`s the icon, so re-running fixes live rows).

**Still OPEN from the prior 2026-07-01 block (not addressed here):** the field-card-arrange vs widget-composition **grid unification** (2C) and giving `WidgetCanvas` a real drag-arrange editor (the "Customize layout" kebab is still a no-op on widget-canvas types, item 3) — these ride the eventual "every type" widget rollout. **DB writes: applied to the dev branch 2026-07-01** — `npm run db:seed` + `node scripts/seed-mindmap.mjs` ran clean (idempotent; `mindmap type seeded`). **One nuance found in the process:** the project-icon backfill uses `WHERE capability IS NULL`, deliberately not clobbering a hand-set value — but the dev DB's `project` type already has `capability='tabs'` (predates the widget-home default), so it's still not `widget-home` there, and `verify-widget-canvas`'s `canvasIdForType(project) = widgets` check still fails on dev. Forcing it would need a deliberate one-off UPDATE, not just re-running the idempotent seed — flagged as a follow-up rather than done here. **Prod is unaffected either way** (Brandon prefers to handle prod writes himself; this only touched the dev branch).

**Deferred / eventual (Tyler, 2026-07-01):** make **every** type (incl. core Note/Task/etc.) default to the Widget Homepage — declined for now, revisit when the widget canvas has a first-class editor and the core-type experiences are ready to move.
