## ⟢ Session summary — AI Memory: `memory` type + always-on stumps + `remember`, over MCP, behind a setting (2026-07-01, ADR-137)

**From Brandon: he wanted his AI's memory to live in Ledgr (portable across AI vendors, unified with everything else) instead of a provider's private store — designed in-session as tiny always-on "stumps" over deep, linked memories pulled on demand, with Ledgr's relation graph as the serendipity engine.** Built five reuse-first pieces, opt-in so a vanilla MCP client is unchanged. **(1) A `memory` type** (migration `0040_memory_type.sql` + `seed.mjs`, additive): bespoke, `hidden=true` (out of Work surfaces + destination picker + generic `list_types`, like `unmarked`, but `getType` still renders `/list/memory` and Build → Types can add props); properties `kind` (user|feedback|project|reference), `horizon` (evergreen|seasonal|episodic), `pinned`. "About" uses **universal** relations, not a typed field. **(2) Two gated MCP tools** — `get_memory_stumps` (body-free index + linked neighbours via batched `relatedSummaryFor`; always-on set by default, `includeAll` for the whole store) and `remember` (create + facets + link in one call); `getMemoryStumps` lives in new `src/lib/memory.ts`. **(3) A `memory-protocol` resource** (`ledgr://guide/memory-protocol`) carrying the **rising-bar recall rule** (follow a link only if it may change the answer; the bar rises per hop; relevance beats distance; stop at diminishing returns) + write conventions. **(4) `aiMemoryEnabled` setting** (default off): gates the tools (`listToolDefs`/`callTool`), the resource (`resources/list`+`read`), and the Build entry. **(5) Build → AI Memory page** (`/build/memory`, MAINTAIN, gated) showing the live stump index (always-on vs pull-only) + link to `/list/memory`. Also `docs/ai-memory.md` with the per-surface instruction stanza (the user's global `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` + claude.ai box) so a client actually calls `get_memory_stumps` each session. **CORE** (extends the machine/MCP contract) → ADR-137, Tyler pre-cleared (COLLAB heads-up to post).
- **Status:** SHIPPED + MERGED — **PR #138** to `main` (off a clean `main`; prior uncommitted Clerk/push WIP archived to branch `archive/clerk-push-wip`). Migration `0040` **applied** to the prod DB (no migrate-on-deploy, so applied before merge). `tsc` + eslint clean; `verify-mcp.mts` green (fixed a stale count assertion `move_item_type`/ADR-132 had left broken); new **`verify-memory.mts`** ALL PASS end-to-end on live Neon (throwaway owner: gating both ways, `remember`+link, stumps, horizon aging); dev-auth preview confirmed the enabled/disabled Build page + sidebar gating + the explainer render (the Settings "Learn more" modal is a trivial open/close toggle whose click couldn't be exercised in the preview's non-hydrating `/settings` loading-shell — code is tsc/lint-clean, no errors). **Off by default:** nothing changes for the owner until the Settings toggle is flipped.
- **Explainer (follow-on ask):** a shared `AiMemoryGuide` (the thinking/strategy + how to use it in any LLM, with the copy-paste instruction stanza) rendered by a **"Learn more"** modal beside the Settings toggle and in a collapsible on the Build → AI Memory page.
- **Affects:** new `drizzle/0040_memory_type.sql`, `scripts/seed.mjs`; new `src/lib/memory.ts`; `src/lib/settings.ts` (`aiMemoryEnabled`), `src/components/settings/SettingsForm.tsx` (toggle + Learn more); `src/lib/mcp/tools.ts` (tools + owner-aware gating), `src/lib/mcp/server.ts`, `src/lib/mcp/guide.ts` (resource); `src/lib/build-nav.ts` (`gatedBy` + entry), `src/components/nav/{BuildSidebar,NavShell,Nav}.tsx`; new `src/app/build/memory/page.tsx`; new `src/components/memory/AiMemoryGuide.tsx` + `src/components/settings/AiMemoryLearnMore.tsx`; `scripts/verify-mcp.mts` + new `scripts/verify-memory.mts`; new `docs/ai-memory.md`; decisions.md (ADR-137). COLLAB.md heads-up to post.

## ⟢ Session summary — Calendar feed + event timeline as bespoke lenses; events unified onto `/list/event` (2026-07-01, ADR-136)

**From Brandon: he'd lost the ability to browse upcoming Outlook meetings and click each to add it to his events for note-taking, and wanted the feed back — as a special lens on the events type, default/leftmost, with other lenses to the right, hideable.** Diagnosed a **routing split**, not a deletion: the calendar feed + Upcoming/Past timeline lived only on the bespoke `/events` page (no lens strip), while the destination picker sent `event` → the generic `/list/event` (lenses, no feed), so navigating to Events lost the feed. **Fix (Brandon's chosen shape): unify onto `/list/event` and make both surfaces lenses.** The shared `Lens` union (`list-lenses.ts`) gains two bespoke *kinds* — `{kind:"calendar"}` (the external un-promoted feed + one-click Add) and `{kind:"timeline"}` (the `meetingAt` Upcoming/Past/No-date grouping). `defaultLenses(typeKey)` is now type-aware: `event` → `[Calendar, Timeline, Recent, Newest, A→Z, Most linked]` (Calendar default/leftmost); every other type keeps the four generic sorts. The generic list page branches on `active.kind` (fetches `listCalendarFeed` for calendar with an empty state; the meeting-time split for timeline). Feed/timeline rendering extracted to `CalendarFeed`/`EventTimeline` + shared `event-format.ts`; bespoke `/events` → `redirect("/list/event")` (defer-by-hiding); `ListTabs` events tab → `/list/event`. Build → Types tabs editor round-trips/reorders/hides them and offers an **"Event views"** re-add group. Related panels filter bespoke lenses out (`relatedLensCandidates`) since a feed/timeline inside "Linked here" is meaningless. **Core-adjacent** (shared `Lens` union, extends ADR-105) → ADR-136, COLLAB heads-up posted.
- **Status:** SHIPPED to the working tree on branch **`feat/events-calendar-lens`** — tsc + eslint clean; verified in dev-auth preview against the prod DB: `/list/event` strip = Calendar·Timeline·Recent·Newest·A→Z·Most linked (Calendar active by default, 12 real upcoming events each with **Add**); Timeline renders Upcoming/Past; `/events` redirects to `/list/event`; Build → Types → Event shows the six event defaults + the re-addable "Event views" options; `/list/person` shows only the four generic sorts (no leak); no console/server errors. **Not yet committed/pushed** (awaiting Brandon's go on branch/PR).
- **Affects:** `src/lib/list-lenses.ts` (union + `defaultLenses(typeKey)` + `relatedLensCandidates` + parse), `src/app/list/[type]/page.tsx` (kind branches), new `src/components/calendar/CalendarFeed.tsx` + `src/components/events/EventTimeline.tsx` + `src/lib/event-format.ts`, `src/app/events/page.tsx` (→ redirect), `src/components/lists/ListTabs.tsx`, `src/components/build/ListTabsEditor.tsx`, `src/components/relations/RelatedPanel.tsx`, `src/lib/related-views.ts`; decisions.md (ADR-136), COLLAB.md. No migration.

## ⟢ Session summary — Related-panel multi-select + meeting "Open tasks" on the lens system (2026-06-30, ADR-135)

**From Brandon: the related-items lenses (PR #129) weren't reaching a meeting's tasks, and there was no multi-select on "tasks associated with a meeting."** Traced it: a meeting's tasks mostly live in the **rule-pulled "Open tasks"** panel (`MeetingPrep`, ADR-094 E4 — tasks of the event's people / by tag), which rendered through a bespoke `PrepTaskList` + old lens bar, bypassing the lens path; and even "Linked here" (`RelatedGroupView`) called `ViewRenderer` **without** `selectable`, an unintentional ADR-118 gap. Fixed reuse-first, **no view-engine/schema change** (solo, not core): (1) `ViewLensBody` gained an optional `rowActions` pass-through, becoming the single selectable view-body. (2) `RelatedGroupView` now routes through `ViewLensBody` and takes a `bulkConfig`; `RelatedPanel` hands each single-type group that type's **full** `bulkConfigForType` (status/date/select + Move + Delete); the select checkbox and the un-relate/@-marker controls coexist. (3) New `resolveProvidedGroup` (`related-views.ts`) renders an **already-resolved** row set through the lens machinery with an **in-memory** sort (`sortInMemory`), so "Open tasks" gets the real lens picker + `ViewRenderer` + multi-select; `MeetingPrep` puts the picker in the `CanvasSection` action slot. **Open tasks offers SORT lenses only** (a view lens's filter can't apply to a rule-chosen set). `PrepTaskList` deleted.
- **Status:** SHIPPED to the working tree on branch **`feat/related-multiselect-meeting-lenses`** — tsc + eslint clean; verified in dev-auth preview against the prod DB on the "Brandon/Roger Check-in" meeting (21 pulled tasks): Open-tasks Lens picker (Recent/Newest/A→Z/Most linked, view lenses excluded) re-sorts + persists; Select toggle (off by default) → 21 row checkboxes + floating bar (Set… Status/Due/Scheduled, Move, Delete, Clear); the Linked-here Person group has its own toggle and a row carrying **both** the checkbox and the ✕ un-relate control; clean reload = exactly two toggles, both off; no console/server errors. **Not yet committed/pushed** (awaiting Brandon's go on branch/PR).
- **Affects:** `src/components/lists/ViewLensBody.tsx`, `src/components/relations/{RelatedGroupView,RelatedPanel}.tsx`, `src/lib/related-views.ts`, `src/components/meetings/MeetingPrep.tsx`; deletes `src/components/meetings/PrepTaskList.tsx`; decisions.md (ADR-135). No migration.

## ⟢ Session summary — Cross-device edit guard (2026-06-30, ADR-134)

**From Brandon's question: leave a meeting open on device 1, edit it on device 2, then type on device 1 without reloading — the stale full-body PATCH silently clobbered device 2's edits, with no indication.** Confirmed the cause (body autosave sends the whole body; `updateItem` was unconditional last-write-wins, no version check; properties already merge per-key, so body/title were the exposed wholesale fields). Built two additive, complementary pieces. **(1) Optimistic body-conflict guard:** the editor sends an optional **`expectedBodyDigest`** (pure, dependency-free `bodyDigest` in `src/lib/body.ts`, of the body it last *synced* with) and `updateItem` returns a new **409 `ItemError("conflict")`** when a real body write's digest no longer matches the stored body. **Content-based on the body, deliberately not an `updated_at` token**, so sibling status/property writes on the same item never cause a false body conflict. The editor surfaces 409 as a **"Keep mine / Reload"** banner (Keep mine = resend without the token; the clobbered version stays in revision history). **(2) Refresh-on-focus:** when a backgrounded canvas tab refocuses, `SaveStatusIndicator` reads a new lightweight **`GET /api/items/:id/version`** (`getItemVersion` — one `updated_at`, no body) and raises a dismissible "Updated on another device" banner. **CORE** (machine-REST contract: a new optional field + a 409 + a read route) but **fully additive/back-compat** — a caller that sends no token keeps last-write-wins → ADR-134, COLLAB heads-up posted.
- **Status:** SHIPPED to the working tree on branch **`feat/concurrent-edit-guard`** — `scripts/verify-concurrent-edit.mts` ALL PASS on Neon (digest determinism/sensitivity; matching digest writes; stale digest → 409 with stored body untouched; resynced digest writes; no-token = last-write-wins; identical-body no-op; `getItemVersion` owner-scoped); the full HTTP 409 round-trip eyeballed in dev-auth preview against a scratch note (device-2 write 200 → device-1 stale guarded write 409, body preserved → "Keep mine" 200 overwrite), then the note soft-deleted; `/version` route 200; tsc + eslint clean. **Scope boundary (defer-by-hiding):** hard guard is on the markdown `ItemEditor` (notes/tasks/meetings); chord/paper bodies advance the focus baseline but don't send the token yet; title clobber stays last-write-wins under the focus banner. **Not yet committed/pushed** (awaiting Brandon's go on branch/PR; merge after Tyler heads-up since core).
- **Affects:** `src/lib/items.ts` (`ItemError` +`"conflict"`, `ItemPatch.expectedBodyDigest`, the `updateItem` guard, `getItemVersion`), `src/lib/api.ts` (409 map), `src/lib/body.ts` (`bodyDigest`), `src/lib/item-input.ts` (parse), `src/lib/save-status.ts` (conflict state + force-save registry + focus baseline), `src/components/markdown-editor/ItemEditor.tsx`, `src/components/canvas/{SaveStatusIndicator,ItemCanvas}.tsx`, `src/components/chord-editor/useItemAutosave.ts`; new `src/app/api/items/[id]/version/route.ts`; new `scripts/verify-concurrent-edit.mts`; decisions.md (ADR-134), COLLAB.md. No migration (uses existing `items.updated_at`).

## ⟢ Session summary — Type cue on the item view + move-between-types (2026-06-29, ADR-132)

**From Brandon's two asks: (1) a gentle visual cue of an item's type on the item view, and (2) the ability to move an item from one type to another without losing its properties.** Planned via `/visual-plan` (rendered locally — hosted Plan publish was down), four design Qs decided. **Part A — type cue:** a quiet, non-interactive `TypeCue` (`src/components/canvas/TypeCue.tsx`, the type's `NavGlyph` icon + label, muted) rides existing chrome with **zero extra vertical space** — the full-page breadcrumb row (after the trash button, `·` before any ancestors) and the modal header beside "Trash". Chose this top-line placement (Option A) over a row-above-title or a title-line adornment. **Part B — move between types:** one server helper `moveItemType(ownerId, id, targetType, {dryRun})` (`items.ts`) behind the dialog preview, a REST endpoint, and a new MCP tool, so preview == commit. Reconciles **only the source type's declared properties**: shared keys carry over; keys the target lacks are **surfaced into the body as a fenced YAML block** (`# carried over from <Type>`) **and retained in jsonb** (recoverable). System keys (`locked`, `recurrence`, sync ids) and relation edges are never touched (relations survive and keep showing). `ChangeTypeDialog` + a "Change type…" item in `ItemActionsMenu`; `move_item_type` MCP tool. **CORE** (move-semantics + MCP contract; no migration) → ADR-132, **needs a Tyler heads-up before merge.**
- **Status:** SHIPPED to the working tree on branch **`feat/type-cue-and-move-type`** — tsc clean; verified in dev-auth preview + prod DB: cue renders ("Trash · Note", no console errors); menu shows "Change type…"; dialog opens (current type excluded) with a working dry-run preview; a throwaway **Event→Note** move surfaced `location: Test Hall` as a YAML block atop the body, retained `location` in jsonb, set `type=note`, then cleaned up (soft-deleted). **Not yet committed/pushed** (awaiting Brandon's go on branch/PR; merge after Tyler heads-up since core).
- **Affects:** new `src/components/canvas/TypeCue.tsx`, `src/components/canvas/ChangeTypeDialog.tsx`, `src/app/api/items/[id]/move-type/route.ts`; edits to `src/components/canvas/{ItemCanvas,Modal,ItemActionsMenu}.tsx`, `src/app/@modal/(.)items/[id]/page.tsx`, `src/lib/items.ts` (`moveItemType`/`MoveTypeSummary`/YAML helpers; dynamic `getType` import to avoid the items↔types cycle), `src/lib/mcp/tools.ts` (`move_item_type`); decisions.md (ADR-132). **Follow-up:** eyeball the modal-variant cue and the dialog commit on the live site; consider a `schema.md`/`runbook` note only if needed (no schema change).

## ⟢ Session summary — Planner: drag-to-schedule calendar (2026-06-29, ADR-131) — IN PROGRESS

**From Brandon's "I need a calendar-based way to plan and schedule my tasks" — brainstormed via `/visual-plan` (rendered locally; hosted Plan publish was down), all six design Qs decided, Tyler agreed the one core touch.** The Planner is the existing `calendar` view layout made interactive: drag tasks to plan *when* you'll do them. Two modes — a **month grid** (all-day chips, drag chip→day) and a **multi-day time-grid** (days as columns, 15/30/60-min rows, drag-to-time + resize→duration, all-day band) — plus an **Unscheduled rail**. Dragging writes `scheduled_date` (+ optional `properties.scheduledTime`), never the deadline (per-view "Place by Due" toggle to flip). Reuses the view engine, `scheduled-time.ts`, the touch-drag primitives (`useBoardTouchDrag`/`board-touch-drag.ts`), the `updateItem`/`PATCH /api/items` write path, and the ICS feed (drags reflect to Outlook for free). No new dependency. Surfaces (Brandon: more, not fewer): a layout option on any view + a default Planner Work-nav destination + a default (removable) Tasks tab. Design = the Ledgr theme + the built-in accent from `users.settings` (ADR-053). Full decided plan: `planner-plan.html` (scratchpad) + memory `planner-feature-plan`.
- **Slice 1 (data foundation) — ✅ SHIPPED + COMMITTED** (branch `planner-phase-1`, commit b1a33d7). Additive **`views.display` jsonb** (migration **0038**, applied to Neon) holding the calendar layout's interactive options (`mode` month|timegrid, `dayCount` 1–7, `slotMinutes` 15|30|60, `placeBy` scheduled|due, work-hours window, `showWeekends`); tolerant `parseDisplay` + `DISPLAY_DEFAULTS` (`views.ts`), threaded through `ViewDefinition`/`ViewInput`/`parseViewInput`/`rowToDefinition`/CRUD. **CORE** (views table) → ADR-131, Tyler agreed. `verify-planner.mts` 21/21 on Neon; `verify-views` regression green; tsc(src)+eslint clean. schema.md + decisions.md updated. **No readers yet** (reversible).
- **Slice 2 (interactive month grid) — ✅ SHIPPED + COMMITTED** (branch `planner-phase-1`). `ViewRenderer`'s `calendar` branch now mounts an interactive **`PlannerMonth`** client island for *writable-date* views (dateProperty scheduled/due/plan/unset); meeting calendars (`meetingAt`) keep the static grid. Drag a chip between days, drag from the **Unscheduled rail** onto a day, or drag back to the rail to clear — optimistic local override → `PATCH /api/items/[id]` ({scheduledDate|dueDate}) → `router.refresh`, revert on fail. Writes scheduled by default, due when the view places by due. Desktop = native HTML5 drag; touch = `usePlannerTouchDrag` (new) reusing the board's long-press/ghost/auto-scroll over a new pure 2D `cellAtPoint` in `board-touch-drag.ts` (kanban `columnAtPoint` untouched). Today highlighted in the User-Settings accent (`var(--accent)`), P1/P2 left-border. `verify-planner.mts` 29/29 (adds cellAtPoint + a live drop-write round-trip); `verify-views` green; tsc + eslint + **`next build`** clean; eyeballed in dev-auth (June grid, rail of 191, scheduled tasks on the right, today pill, no console errors). `ViewInput.display` made optional so hand-built view literals are unaffected.
- **Slice 3 (multi-day time-grid) — ✅ SHIPPED + COMMITTED.** `PlannerCalendar` wrapper toggles **Month ⇄ Multi-day** (init from `display.mode`). `PlannerTimeGrid`: days as columns × `slotMinutes` rows, all-day band, Unscheduled rail; drag into a slot (sets day + start time), onto the band (day-only), to the rail (clear); drag a block's bottom edge to **resize→duration**. Pure geometry in `src/lib/planner-grid.ts` (unit-tested); reuses the touch hook via composite `data-day` keys; floating time in `properties.scheduledTime`. `verify-planner` 38/38; build clean; eyeballed (5-day grid, 120 slot cells, timed block, no errors).
- **Slice 4 (reachable destination) — ✅ SHIPPED + COMMITTED.** New **`/planner`** route (month default + full `?month` nav) over all active tasks, and a **Planner tab on `/tasks`** (7-day time-grid; self-navigating so no `?month`/`?tab` clash). Added a **Planner** option to the nav picker (`nav-slot-options`) + to `DEFAULT_NAV_SLOTS` (fresh instances), and inserted it into Brandon's existing nav after Tasks (one-time settings write). Eyeballed: Planner in the left rail, `/planner` + `/tasks?tab=planner` both render, no console errors. tsc + eslint + `next build` clean.
- **Polish pass (from Brandon's first real use, 2026-06-29) — ✅ SHIPPED + COMMITTED (branch `planner-polish`).** (1) **Unscheduled rail** now has a filter box + sort (default **smart**: priority → recently edited → recently created; `src/lib/planner-rail.ts`, pure/tested) + **Show 25 more** paging, extracted to a shared `UnscheduledRail`. (2) **Width** — `ListPage` gained a `wide` prop (`max-w-[110rem]`); `/planner` and the Tasks Planner tab use it, so the calendar fills a large monitor. (3) **Drag-on-first-try** — chips/blocks are now `<div>`s, not `<Link>`s, so the browser's native anchor-drag no longer fights ours (the "click twice / goes gray" bug); click still navigates. (4) **Month** fills complete weeks with muted leading/trailing **spillover days** (no blank cells). (5) **Time-grid horizontal scroll** — 28 days in one scroller, columns sized to fit `dayCount`, with **drag edge auto-scroll** (both axes, rAF loop on the scroll container). (6) **Resize handle** — `justResized`/`handleResizing` guards + `stopPropagation` so grabbing/releasing the edge resizes and never opens the task. (7) **Vertical time scroll** — the grid now renders the full 24h in a scroll viewport that **opens at the work-hours window** (initial `scrollTop = workStart·HOUR_PX`) but scrolls to any hour; sticky day headers + all-day band + time gutter. `verify-planner` **45/45** (adds rail-sort cases); tsc + eslint + `next build` clean; eyeballed at 1680px (month fills width + spillover; time-grid fills + scrolls H/V, scrolled to 7am, no console errors). **Touch horizontal auto-scroll** left as a follow-up (desktop mouse path done). **Follow-up fix (Brandon, 2026-06-29):** the "drag twice" bug persisted on the **month** view only — root cause was the month chip being an *inner component* (`function Chip()` declared in `PlannerMonth`'s render body), so its type changed each render and `setDragId` in `onDragStart` remounted the dragged node mid-drag, cancelling the native drag. Converted to a plain `chip(item)` render function (inline elements, stable DOM node) — matching how the time-grid already renders chips, which is why it was unaffected. Verified in-browser: the chip node survives the drag-start re-render (same ref). **Two more (Brandon, 2026-06-29):** (a) a long Unscheduled rail squished its rows infinitely thin instead of scrolling — classic flex-shrink trap; chips now `sm:shrink-0` so the column scrolls past 60vh. (b) New **"Show unscheduled"** toggle in the planner header (PlannerCalendar), **off by default**, persisted in `localStorage` (`planner:showUnscheduled`), passed to PlannerMonth/PlannerTimeGrid to hide/show the rail (tasks with no due/scheduled date). Default-off + toggle-present verified in dev-auth; the toggle's runtime flip + rail scroll couldn't be exercised in the preview (the Next-dev `loading.tsx` shell blocks client effects/clicks there) — straightforward controlled-checkbox + CSS, build/lint clean.
- **Slice 5a (read-only calendar overlay) — ✅ SHIPPED to working tree (2026-06-29, ADR-133).** A **"Show calendar"** toggle (beside "Show unscheduled", off by default, `localStorage` `planner:showCalendar`, seeded from a new additive `ViewDisplay.showCalendar`) overlays the synced calendar as **read-only** context so you can schedule tasks around it. Sourced from the **`calendar_events` cache** (all non-cancelled events in range, promoted *or* not — the whole calendar, not just added items) via new `listCalendarEventsForRange` + `OverlayEvent`/`overlayWindow` (`src/lib/calendar/{feed,overlay}.ts`), day+time resolved to `APP_TIMEZONE` server-side. Month grid → muted sky-bordered event badges (+"N events" overflow) above task chips; multi-day grid → muted, **`pointer-events-none`** timed blocks behind the task blocks (so a task still drops onto the slot under an event) + all-day band chips. Available on **any writable calendar view** (`/planner`, `/tasks?tab=planner`, `/views/[id]`). Sync window bumped 14 → **28 days** (`DEFAULT_WINDOW_DAYS`) for a 4-week planning horizon; the meeting-import feed stays 2 weeks via a separate `FEED_WINDOW_DAYS` bound (decoupled). **Not core** (cache read + UI + additive jsonb flag; the calendar provider seam is untouched — no new Graph fetch/scope) → ADR-133, solo. tsc + eslint clean; verified in dev-auth preview against the prod DB (month + multi-day overlays render, no console/server errors). **Not yet committed** (awaiting Brandon's go on branch/PR).
- **Phase 1 essentially complete.** Remaining (**slice 5b, later, optional**): a deterministic **"find a slot"** / overdue-reschedule assist over the same overlay data. Also deferred: capturing Graph's `isAllDay` into the synced `meta` (today all-day is inferred from a midnight + whole-day-duration heuristic); persisting the in-session mode toggle per-view (system views can't be edited); and a true mobile bottom-sheet rail (today the rail stacks above the grid on small screens). **Branch `planner-phase-1` not pushed** — ready for a PR on your go.

## ⟢ Session summary — Reminder options + time-aware date box; notification center PAUSED (2026-06-29, ADR-130)

**From Brandon's two asks on the task Schedule popover.** (1) **Richer reminder options.** The reminder dropdown drove the ICS feed's alarm but only offered all-day-framed leads ("9 AM", "Midnight", "N days before") — wrong/confusing once a task has a time. Now **context-aware**: an all-day task shows *9 AM (default) / At start of day / 1 day / 2 days / 1 week before*; a **timed** task shows ***At time of event (default)** / 10 min / 30 min / 1 hour before / 1 day / 1 week before*, with a fallback so any stored value always renders. No ICS math change — the existing `alarm()` already triggers off DTSTART (midnight all-day, block start when timed); "at time of event" = the timed default (`PT0M`). Reminders already anchor scheduled-else-due, so "either due or scheduled" was already satisfied. (2) **Time-aware date box.** The Schedule free-text box ("e.g. next fri") now parses an optional time — `5am today`, `next fri 2:30pm`, `tomorrow at 9am`, `17:00`, bare `9am` — via new pure `parseNaturalWhen` (12h/24h token stripped, remainder fed to the unchanged `parseNaturalDate`; bare ambiguous `5` never guessed, Principle 3). On a hit it sets the scheduled date **and** `properties.scheduledTime` in one PATCH; `ScheduledTimeControl` got the adjust-during-render re-adopt so the Time control reflects it without a remount. Opt-in via a `parseTime` prop so the Due popover (no clock time) is untouched. **Not core** (feature/UI; no schema/seam/contract change).
- **Status:** SHIPPED to the working tree — tsc(src) + eslint clean; `parseNaturalWhen` runtime-checked across 18 phrases; verified end-to-end in dev-auth preview on a throwaway task (`5am today` → date Jun 29 + 5:00 AM, summary "Mon, Jun 29 · 5:00 AM", reminder flipped to the timed set), throwaway soft-deleted. **Not yet committed** (awaiting Brandon's go on branch/PR).
- **Affects (reminders + time box):** `src/lib/nl-date.ts` (`parseNaturalWhen`), `src/components/canvas/rail/DayField.tsx` (`parseTime`), `src/components/canvas/rail/SchedulePopover.tsx` (combined date+time PATCH; `hasTime` to reminder), `src/components/canvas/ReminderControl.tsx` (context-aware option sets + fallback), `src/components/canvas/ScheduledTimeControl.tsx` (re-adopt).

**Notification center PAUSED (ADR-130, detach not delete).** Brandon's reasoning: the ICS feed now gives precise, offline, on-device reminders **and** a per-device history on whatever calendar he subscribes, so the recently-built in-app center (ADR-129) is redundant for him. Per the **defer-by-hiding-not-deleting** rule, detached behind one flag rather than removed; scope confirmed **all off** (in-app center hidden + both push crons stopped — morning-agenda + meeting-prep phone pushes go quiet too). One db-free flag `NOTIFICATION_CENTER_ENABLED = false` (`src/lib/notifications-enabled.ts`, full re-enable runbook in its header) gates: the two `push/notify.ts` senders (early-return; manual dispatch is a no-op), the `/notifications` nav destination, the More-menu link + `AppBadgeSync` mount, the `Nav.tsx` unread query, the Settings "Notifications" section, and a `/notifications` → `/` redirect. Crons disabled in config (`notify-agenda` removed from `vercel.json`; `notify-prep.yml` `schedule:` commented, `workflow_dispatch` kept). **Kept fully intact + recoverable:** the `notifications` table + migration 0037, `notifications.ts`, the API routes, the page + `NotificationList`, the SW badge handlers, the whole Web Push transport (subscriptions, VAPID, `PushToggle`). **Per-instance / solo** — no schema/seam/contract moves (the table stays), so not a core reversal; Tyler's instance is unaffected (**COLLAB heads-up to Tyler suggested**).
- **Status:** SHIPPED to the working tree — tsc(src) + eslint clean; `vercel.json` valid JSON; verified in dev-auth preview (`/notifications` redirects to `/`; no nav/menu notification links; no `AppBadgeSync`; Settings "Notifications" section gone, rest of Settings intact; no console errors). **Not yet committed.**
- **Affects (detach):** new `src/lib/notifications-enabled.ts`; `src/lib/notifications.ts` (re-export), `src/lib/push/notify.ts` (guards), `src/lib/nav-slot-options.ts`, `src/components/nav/{NavShell,Nav}.tsx`, `src/components/settings/SettingsForm.tsx`, `src/app/notifications/page.tsx`, `vercel.json`, `.github/workflows/notify-prep.yml`; decisions.md (ADR-130).

Graduates `explorations/notification-center.md`.

## ⟢ Session summary — Discover: deterministic related-item suggestions (2026-06-27, ADR-127)

**From Brandon's "have Ledgr deterministically suggest items related to the current one" ask — graduates `explorations/related-items-discovery.md` (parked 2026-06-19) into Phase 1.** Planned via `/visual-plan` (plan-84b5d536cd50439c), Brandon answered the open questions, Tyler acked → built in one push. A collapsible **Discover related** section under "Linked here" surfaces items worth linking but not linked yet, deterministically ranked (no model, Principle 3), each with a reason chip + one-click **Link**. **CORE** (relations substrate, RelatedPanel, FTS) → ADR-127. Built: a pure scorer (`src/lib/discovery/score.ts` — bounded indexed probes: FTS OR-query + `pg_trgm` titles → **text**, IDF-damped co-citation → **cocitation**, same parent / shared select value → **sharedAttr**, recency → **temporal** booster; `WEIGHTS` + `SCORE_FLOOR` in one constant; `includeLinked` flag for the future explorer); an `item_relatedness` cache (**migration 0036**, applied to Neon) refreshed by a bounded, dirty-driven + rolling nightly job (`refresh.ts` → `/api/machine/relatedness` + daily `vercel.json` cron; `/health` `lastRelatednessRunAt`); the stable endpoint `GET /api/items/[id]/suggested-relations` (cache read, live-compute fallback on miss, read-time re-filter of now-linked items); and `DiscoverPanel` wired under Linked here in both canvas layouts (auto-hides when nothing clears the floor, Link writes a generic `related` edge and the row graduates up; "Search everything about this" prefills `/search?q=`). Never writes `suggested` edges (that stays the matchers'); no dismiss in v1.
- **Status:** SHIPPED — PR #107 merged to main; prod deploy in flight. `scripts/verify-discover.mts` **19/19** on Neon (signals, floor, template/deleted/self exclusions, body-free, score-sorted, cache populate + read, read-time re-filter of a now-linked candidate, owner-scoping); regressions green (`verify-canvas-layout` updated for the new `discover` card, `verify-relations`, `verify-lists-search`, `verify-health-check`); tsc + lint + `next build` clean. **Remaining:** in-browser eyeball on the live site (Discover under an item; expand → ranked rows + chips; Link → moves into Linked here). The nightly cache trickles in a `BATCH` of ~200/run; the live fallback covers anything uncached, so it works day one.
- **Phase 2 — Related Explorer ✅ SHIPPED (2026-06-27, PR #109).** `/items/[id]/explore` (server page; breadcrumb trail via `?trail`; one score-sorted list unifying existing links + discovered candidates, linked rows badged; `+ Link` and `Explore →` to re-anchor on any row; search tail). `exploreNeighborhood` (`src/lib/discovery/explore.ts`) unions `listRelatedItems` + `scoreRelated({includeLinked:true})` — computed live per anchor (re-anchor = a bounded recompute), so it never needs the unlinked-only panel cache. Entry points: the item `⋯` menu (always available) + the Discover panel footer. `verify-explore.mts` 9/9; tsc/lint/build green.
- **Phase 3 — Loose Ends ✅ SHIPPED (2026-06-27, PR #110).** Build › MAINTAIN page `/build/loose-ends` + sidebar entry: the relatedness engine inverted across the corpus — `findLooseEnds` (`src/lib/discovery/loose-ends.ts`) aggregates confirmed non-mention edge degree once, takes the least-connected items (≤3 edges), scores the top ~40 concurrently, and shows ~20 that have a real suggestion (skips true orphans with nothing to link); `LooseEndCard` renders each with its top suggestions inline + one-click `+ Link`. `verify-loose-ends.mts` 7/7; tsc/lint/build green. **Deferred:** the **dashboard widget** (a bespoke widget kind — heavier; the page is the home for now), and an on-demand **AI "Find connections"** (Layer 2). Discover (ADR-127) is complete bar those two opt-in extras.
- **Affects:** new `src/lib/discovery/{types,score,refresh}.ts`, `src/app/api/items/[id]/suggested-relations/route.ts`, `src/app/api/machine/relatedness/route.ts`, `src/components/relations/DiscoverPanel.tsx`, `scripts/verify-discover.mts`; `src/db/schema.ts` (+`itemRelatedness`) + migration `0036`; `MarkdownCanvas.tsx` + `canvas-layout.ts` (a `discover` card after `related`), `health.ts` (canary), `vercel.json` (cron), `search/page.tsx` + `SearchClient.tsx` (`q` prefill); decisions.md (ADR-127).

## ⟢ Session summary — Large-body paging on the MCP get_item read path (2026-06-27, ADR-126)

**The follow-up ADR-125 flagged (next_steps line 13).** ADR-125 fixed the *browser* freeze on huge notes but left the MCP read path: `get_item` (the only MCP tool that returns a body — search/list/run_view are body-free via `rowView`) returned the body whole, so reading a 2.4M-char import (~600k tokens) flooded the model's context in one tool result. Built **`windowBody(text, {offset, limit})` + `BODY_WINDOW_CHARS`** in `body.ts` (pure substring math, **no markdown parsing** — keeps the module client/server-portable; reuses the ADR-125 `LARGE_BODY_THRESHOLD`/`isLargeBody`, window pinned to the threshold). `get_item` now **windows only large bodies** (or when the caller explicitly pages): a normal body still returns **whole and byte-identical** (contract untouched, existing `verify-mcp` holds), a large one returns one window + a truncation marker + a structured **`bodyInfo {totalChars, offset, returnedChars, truncated, nextOffset}`**; the caller pages by passing the previous `nextOffset` as new args **`bodyOffset`**/**`bodyLimit`** (≤ window). **CORE** (machine/MCP contract) but **additive** — optional args + one extra output field on one read tool, no write-path or `{format,text}` change.
- **Status:** built; tsc + eslint clean; new **`scripts/verify-mcp-large-body.mts` 25/25 on live Neon** (windowBody unit cases incl. clamping/reassembly; get_item small-body-verbatim + no bodyInfo, large-body auto-window, exact reassembly walking nextOffset over 3 reads, bodyLimit, final-window/null-nextOffset, owner scoping); existing `verify-mcp.mts` still ALL PASS (no regression). **Remaining:** PR (no migration, fully additive/reversible).
- **Section windowing deferred** (the marker's "section/offset" hint): needs markdown parsing `body.ts` excludes; char offsets are a robust, dependency-free paging primitive that round-trips exactly. Could layer a server-side section param later if a real need appears.
- **Affects:** `src/lib/body.ts` (`BODY_WINDOW_CHARS`, `BodyWindow`, `windowBody`), `src/lib/mcp/tools.ts` (`get_item` schema + handler), new `scripts/verify-mcp-large-body.mts`; decisions.md (ADR-126).

## ⟢ Session summary — Body modes (rich / source / preview) + large-body size gate (2026-06-27, ADR-125)

**From Brandon's "very long notes freeze/crash on open" report (e.g. ECC Insurance, 955K chars).** Measured against prod data: the cause is entirely client-side — the body always mounts the Tiptap/ProseMirror WYSIWYG (one contenteditable tree of tens of thousands of nodes + a full markdown re-serialize on every keystroke). Postgres returns 1MB+ in ms and the FTS tsvector stays ~13% of its 1 MiB limit even for the 2.4M-char outlier, so neither DB, search, Neon, nor Vercel is the bottleneck. Brandon wants long read-only notes (PDFs-as-text) as a permanent use, and reframed the fix to the long-intended **source/preview toggle**, with the size gate just selecting it. Built: a new client **`BodyEditor`** (owns rich/source/preview mode, rendered by `ItemEditor` so every canvas inherits it), **`RawMarkdownEditor`** (a plain `<textarea>` — no contenteditable, holds megabytes), **`MarkdownPreview`** (read-only HTML via new **`POST /api/render-markdown`**, markdown-it stays server-only). **Size gate** `LARGE_BODY_THRESHOLD = 100_000` chars (`isLargeBody`, in `body.ts`): at/above it the rich editor is never mounted — opens in Preview with a banner, Source is the edit path; below it, rich default + a quiet Rich/Source toggle on any note. **Size-aware revisions** (`snapshotRevision`): large bodies throttle to 60-min / 10-snapshot (vs 5-min / 50) so a multi-MB doc can't balloon Neon storage. **CORE** (type+canvas model, body contract) → **Tyler agreed before build**. Full design: **ADR-125**.
- **Status:** built; tsc + eslint clean; `verify-body-modes.mts` ALL PASS (threshold/isLargeBody boundaries + the markdownToHtml preview path: title-shift, tab-marker flatten, anchor strip, ~520K render); live `/api/render-markdown` exercised in dev-auth preview (200, tabs flattened, anchors stripped, 200K-char doc renders). The `/items/[id]` UI itself couldn't be eyeballed in-preview — the headless preview won't swap in this route's Suspense content (the server returns 200 + 2.1MB; documented Next-dev streaming quirk, upstream of the change). **Remaining:** in-browser eyeball at deploy (open ECC Insurance → reading mode; toggle Edit-as-text; confirm a normal note still rich + Rich/Source toggle), then PR (no migration, fully additive/reversible).
- **Follow-ups noted (out of scope):** bound the FTS input with `left(body_text, N)` if a tsvector ever nears the 1 MiB ceiling (distant — measured ~13%); ~~MCP `get_item` should truncate/section a huge body so reading a 2.4M-char note doesn't flood Claude context~~ **→ done, ADR-126**; in-app Preview doesn't carry sermon highlight-color CSS (`mark.hl-*`), so highlights show browser-default in preview (rich mode unaffected).
- **Affects:** new `src/components/markdown-editor/BodyEditor.tsx`, `RawMarkdownEditor.tsx`, `MarkdownPreview.tsx`, `src/app/api/render-markdown/route.ts`, `scripts/verify-body-modes.mts`; `src/lib/body.ts` (`LARGE_BODY_THRESHOLD`, `isLargeBody`), `src/components/markdown-editor/ItemEditor.tsx` (renders `BodyEditor`), `src/lib/items.ts` (size-aware `snapshotRevision`); decisions.md (ADR-125).

## ⟢ Session summary — Item-canvas redesign: CanvasSection + skins, Properties/Linked-here merge (2026-06-27, ADR-124)

**From Brandon's "this event view is bland and hard to glance; also trim the vestigial 'Promote action item to task'" ask, designed via before/build mockups he approved.** The flat wall of gray sections is now a standardized **`CanvasSection`** primitive (icon · label · count pill · right-aligned action, accent glyph) used by every item view, with three owner-selectable weights — **heavy** (bordered cards), **light** (divider rule, the default), **unified** (flat) — driven by one `data-section-style` body attribute (new `sectionStyle` setting, same per-owner rail as accent/textSize; toggle in User Settings, live). The confusing **"Relations" + "Related"** pair is resolved per Brandon's reframing: typed relation fields fold into **Properties** (marked with a blue link glyph), and the connected web becomes **"Linked here,"** de-duped so a field value never shows twice. Event prep refined: suggested people inline as dashed sparkle chips (hover to confirm), Open tasks reuse the actionable `RelatedRow` (check off + edit in place), long titles wrap (mobile). Vestigial `PromoteTask` button deleted (the `[ ]`-line "→ task" path replaces it; its route stays). **CORE** for the relations/related presentation (ADR-055/067) → **Tyler acked**; the primitive + skins are solo UI/settings. Full design: **ADR-124**.
- **Status:** SHIPPED — PR #103 merged + live (ledgr.app), Brandon's `sectionStyle` set to **heavy** (default ships light). **Consistency pass (follow-up, Brandon flagged the task rail + popovers weren't skinned):** swept the surfaces the opt-in `CanvasSection` had missed — `HistoryPanel`, the Fields footer, `Subtasks`, and the song/paper canvases now skin-responsive; the `TaskCanvas` rail merges relations+scalars under one bare "Properties" header and `SchedulePopover` labels adopt the shared header style (compact/transient surfaces get matching headers but NOT card chrome, Brandon's call). tsc/eslint/`next build` clean; dev-auth preview confirmed. ADR-124 addendum recorded.
- **Affects:** new `src/components/canvas/CanvasSection.tsx`; `settings.ts`/`layout.tsx`/`globals.css`/`SettingsForm.tsx` (skins), `nav-icons.ts` (+`properties`,`affiliate`); `MarkdownCanvas.tsx`, `RelatedPanel.tsx`, `RelationProperties.tsx`, `RelatedRow.tsx`, `InlineTitle.tsx`, `MeetingPrep.tsx`, `SuggestedPeople.tsx`, `MeetingTranscripts.tsx`, `PaperCanvas.tsx`; deleted `PromoteTask.tsx`; decisions.md (ADR-124).

## ⟢ NOW BUILDING — Auto-match events to people via templates (EM1–EM4, ADR-123, 2026-06-27)

**From Brandon's "events should arrive knowing who they're about, so tasks/notes/prayer connect."** The slice-23 matcher engine only linked people through hand-authored `attendeeEmail` rules (0 in prod) and nothing resolved an attendee email to a person already storing it, nor matched "Roger/Brandon 1:1" → the Roger person. Brandon reshaped the design (this session): a **guessing** layer (email + fuzzy title/details → suggestions), **confirm → pin**, and the rule **routed through a template** (a pinned match = an `event` template with the people pre-related + recurring content; future matching events apply it). Promotion stays **manual** (no firehose). **CORE** (matching, `match_state`, relations, templates schema) → both-agree + ADR-123; COLLAB heads-up posted, **Tyler ack pending before merge**. Four slices, one PR each (E1–E4 precedent). Full design: **ADR-123**.

- [x] **EM1 — Schema + rule source (no behavior change). ✅ DONE (2026-06-27; CORE; branch `events-em1-template-rules`).** New nullable **`templates.match_config` jsonb** (`{condition: MatcherCondition; autoApply: boolean}`) + partial index `templates_match_idx`, **migration 0035** (additive, applied to Neon). The calendar match-rule SOURCE moves from the empty `matchers` table onto templates: the matcher engine's condition vocabulary (`MatcherCondition`, `CONDITION_RANK`, `validateCondition`) is reused verbatim; new `src/lib/calendar/event-rules.ts` (`listEventRules`, `matchEventToTemplate` — kind precedence, fuzzy gated, oldest-template tie-break); `matchConfig` threaded through `templates.ts` CRUD + new strict `src/lib/templates/match-config.ts`. `null` = plain content template (unchanged). `verify-event-rules.mts` **32/32** on Neon; tsc/eslint/`next build` clean.
- [x] **EM2 — Person suggester + Tier-B suggestions on Add. ✅ DONE (2026-06-27; CORE; branch `events-em2-people-suggester`).** New `src/lib/calendar/suggest-people.ts` (`suggestPeopleForEvent`: Signal 1 attendee/organizer email → person by stored `email`; Signal 2 title/details token containment over person-name tokens, owner id + name tokens excluded, meeting stopwords dropped, multi-token names ranked above single-token; Signal 3 full-title `word_similarity` fallback, gated to when 1+2 miss; arrays passed as `jsonb` params). New `src/lib/calendar/intake.ts` (`applyEventIntake`: Tier A recognized → record-only in EM2; Tier B → `addMatchEdge(…, "suggested")` + `properties.match.suggestions`; Tier C → nothing; create-only). `promoteCalendarEvent` (`feed.ts`) repointed from `applyMatchersToMeeting` to it. Result: adding "Roger/Brandon 1:1" attaches Roger as a **suggested** person with ✓/✕ in the existing relations panel; confirming flows it into prep; re-intake never downgrades a confirmed edge. `verify-people-suggest.mts` **21/21** + calendar-feed/sync regressions green; tsc/eslint/`next build` clean. Works with zero templates.
- [x] **EM3 — Templates as rules + Tier-A auto-apply. ✅ DONE (2026-06-27; CORE; branch `events-em3-template-apply`).** `applyEventIntake` Tier A now calls `applyTemplateToExisting(…, {mode:"fill"})`, so a pinned template's pre-related people land as **confirmed** edges (flow into prep/task-pull) and its body/subtasks copy on; `properties.match` records `{templateId, templateName, condition}` (back-compat: `getMeetingPrep` still reads `templateName` for the display chip). Both sync routes (`/api/calendar/sync` user + `/api/machine/calendar-sync` cron) **dropped `matchEvent`/`applyMatchersToMeeting`** and auto-promote (promotion is manual; events wait in the feed, intake runs on Add). The matchers engine + `/api/matchers` route are marked **DORMANT** (defer-by-hiding; `MatcherCondition`/`CONDITION_RANK`/`validateCondition` stay as shared vocab). A dormant (`autoApply:false`) rule does not apply — intake falls through to suggestions. `verify-event-template-apply.mts` **10/10** + `verify-calendar-sync` (promoted:0) green; tsc/eslint/`next build` clean.
- [x] **EM4 — Pin-as-rule UX + suggestion surfacing. ✅ DONE (2026-06-27; CORE; branch `events-em4-pin-rule`).** `src/lib/templates/pin.ts`: `derivePinCondition` (non-owner attendee email → series → title fuzzy) + `pinEventAsTemplate` (creates/updates an `event` template carrying ONLY the event's **confirmed** people — via `getMeetingPeople`, not `createTemplateFromItem`, since `carryRelations` would copy suggested edges as confirmed — with `matchConfig` autoApply on; create-or-update guard by equal condition). `POST /api/events/[id]/pin-rule` (derive or accept an explicit condition). `MeetingPrep.tsx`: a labeled **"Pin as rule"** button + hover tooltip (shown once people are confirmed, hidden when the event already came from a rule), a "Suggested (confirm below): …" line (from new `getMeetingPrep.suggestedPeople`), and clearer empty states. `getMeetingPeople` gained a `matchState` arg. `verify-pin-rule.mts` **18/18** (incl. the full loop: pin → a new matching event auto-applies) + `verify-meeting-prep` regression green; tsc/eslint/`next build` clean. **Remaining:** in-browser eyeball at deploy (local = prod DB, so not exercised against live data here).

**🎉 Auto-match events to people via templates (EM1–EM4, ADR-123) — MERGED + LIVE 2026-06-27 (PRs #96–#100, Tyler-approved).** Calendar events arrive with the right people suggested (email + fuzzy title), confirming flows them into prep/task-pull, and pinning a confirmed match creates a reusable `event` template (people pre-related + a rule) that future matching events auto-apply.

**Post-merge fix — suggestions are LIVE on the canvas (branch `fix/event-suggestions-live-on-canvas`).** First live test surfaced the gap: suggestions had been written only at feed-Add intake, so opening an existing/imported/hand-made event showed nothing (the People section was empty). Fixed: `getMeetingPrep` now runs `suggestPeopleForEvent` over the event item every render (minus confirmed people), so **any** event shows guesses in the People section, each with a one-click **"+ add"** (`SuggestedPeople.tsx` → `POST /api/items/[id]/relations`). `applyEventIntake` is now Tier A (apply a pinned template) or none — the suggested-edge writing is removed (ADR-123 addendum). `verify-people-suggest` + `verify-event-template-apply` updated; tsc/eslint/`next build` clean. (Also: Ledgr is a PWA, so the old UI can be served from the service-worker cache after a deploy — a hard refresh / reopening the installed app pulls the new build.)

**Rule management UX (same branch / follow-up).** From Brandon's "how do I remove a pin?" — there was no way to see/remove a rule short of deleting the template. Added to the **TemplateBanner** (the template's canvas): a plain-language line "⚡ Auto-applies to {events where X is invited / titled like \"Y\" / in this series}" + an **"Unpin rule"** button (`PATCH /api/templates/[id]` `{matchConfig:null}` → keeps the template, drops the rule). The event's "auto-filled from rule: {name}" chip now **links to the template** (`getMeetingPrep.templatePrototypeId`) so it's reachable from an auto-filled event; templates are also listed under Build → Templates. `verify-event-template-apply` asserts the chip-link resolves. Editing a rule's condition in place (vs unpin + re-pin) is a noted future follow-up.

## ⟢ Session summary — Multi-select is opt-in via a "Select" toggle (2026-06-27, ADR-121)

**From Brandon's "the always-present checkboxes waste row space on desktop and are ugly/always-on on mobile" ask.** Refined the ADR-118 selection layer so it's **off by default**. `SelectionProvider` gained a `selectMode` flag (+ `total`); `SelectCheckbox` now renders **nothing** until select mode is on (a null flex child reserves no gap, so an idle row is back to its pre-multi-select layout — space reclaimed on desktop, no checkboxes on touch). New **`SelectModeToggle`** is a small right-aligned "Select" / "Done" button placed just above the list inside each provider (Brandon's chosen placement: "inline above the list"); it shows the live count ("Done · 3") and hides when nothing's selectable. New client cells **`SelectHeaderCell`/`SelectBodyCell`** collapse the table layout's checkbox column when off (server `ViewRenderer` can't read the client mode). Wired into all eight ADR-118 surfaces; suppressed on board/calendar (still the selection exceptions). UI/UX, **not core**; no change to the batch endpoint or bulk-config.
- **Status:** built; tsc + eslint + `next build` clean; in-browser dev-auth pass on `/notes` + `/tasks` (default = no selection boxes, titles/check-off flush-left, "Select" present; toggle → boxes + "Done"; select → bulk bar; "Done" clears). **Remaining:** PR (no migration, pure UI).
- **Affects:** `src/components/selection/{SelectionProvider,SelectCheckbox,SelectModeToggle,SelectTableCell}.tsx`, `src/components/views/ViewRenderer.tsx`, `src/components/lists/ViewLensBody.tsx`, the 7 list pages, CLAUDE.md (convention bullet), decisions.md (ADR-121).

## ⟢ Session summary — Multi-select + bulk item operations (2026-06-26, ADR-118, branch `feat/multi-select-bulk`)

**From Brandon's "I want to edit 50 tasks at once and can't" ask.** Every list rendered one independent row at a time (no selection), so multi-item edits meant opening items one by one. Built a **reusable selection layer** wired onto every list, and made "every list includes multi-select" a CLAUDE.md convention. Core-adjacent but additive (no new item contract; reuses `updateItem`/`softDeleteItem`); COLLAB heads-up for Tyler.
- **Endpoint (`src/app/api/items/batch/route.ts`, new):** user-auth `PATCH { ids, patch }` (one validated patch → each id via `updateItem`) and `DELETE { ids }` (soft-delete each, cascades to children). Per-id `errors` collected, one bad id never fails the rest. Cap 200/request; client chunks larger selections. Mirrors the machine batch route (ADR-113) under Clerk auth.
- **Selection layer (`src/components/selection/`, new):** `SelectionProvider` (context: selected set + ordered in-view ids → shift-click range + select-all), `SelectCheckbox` (per-row island; renders nothing without a provider), `BulkActionBar` (floating bar), `bulk-actions.ts` (chunked batch calls). Config from `src/lib/bulk-config.ts` (`bulkConfigForType`).
- **Actions:** **Delete** (Trash, `ConfirmButton`, notes the child cascade), **Set…** (status / any select·multi_select property / due·scheduled date, via `propertyPatch`), **Move…** (typeahead → `parentId`, like AddRelation, + "top level"). Config-driven: status-less types hide status/date; mixed-type Inbox shows only Move + Delete. **Archive deliberately not built** (Brandon scoped it for the future archive feature; the Set… field list is the seam).
- **Wired:** `/tasks` (all tabs), `/notes`, `/links`, `/events`, `/inbox`, `/list/[type]` (= People + every custom type), and the shared `ViewRenderer` list/table/agenda (so `ViewLensBody` lenses + `/views/[id]` get it via a `selectable` prop). **Deferred (defer-by-hiding):** board/calendar layouts, dashboard widgets, Trash.
- **Status:** built; tsc + eslint clean. **Remaining:** in-browser eyeball (select incl. shift-range, Set, Move, Delete) and a `verify-items-batch.mts`; then PR.

## ⟢ Session summary — MCP OAuth shim: connect Ledgr's MCP from claude.ai + the phone (2026-06-26, ADR-117, branch `feat/mcp-oauth-shim`)

**From Brandon's "I want to use my Ledgr MCP from anywhere, including my phone" question.** The consumer Claude apps add a remote MCP only as an OAuth custom connector (no bearer/header field; Anthropic #112 "not planned" — confirmed by research this session), so the live static-token `/api/mcp` couldn't be added on web/mobile. Added a minimal, **stateless** OAuth 2.1 shim that **wraps, not replaces**, the static bearer model (CORE/machine-MCP contract; **Tyler agreed 2026-06-26**; COLLAB heads-up posted).
- **Lib (`src/lib/auth/oauth.ts`, new):** dependency-free HMAC-signed tokens (node:crypto, Principle 5) for client_id / authorization code / access / refresh, each kind-pinned + expiry-checked; PKCE S256 verify; discovery-metadata builders; `originFromRequest`. Signed with new `LEDGR_OAUTH_SECRET` — **no DB tables, no dependency**, which is what keeps it off the shared schema.
- **Routes:** `/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource` + `…-authorization-server` (RFC 9728/8414, via `next.config.ts` rewrites onto `/api/oauth/*`), stateless DCR `POST /api/oauth/register` (RFC 7591), the Clerk-gated `GET /api/oauth/authorize` (owner login = consent), PKCE `POST /api/oauth/token`. `/api/mcp` now accepts an OAuth access token **in addition to** the static `LEDGR_API_TOKENS` bearer and emits a `WWW-Authenticate` discovery hint on its 401. `proxy.ts` public-set updated (discovery/register/token public; authorize stays gated).
- **Verify:** `verify-oauth-mcp.mts` **37/37** (sign/verify, kind-pinning, expiry, tamper, PKCE, scope, metadata, secret-rotation isolation, configured gate). **Live HTTP round-trip** on the dev server (full discovery → register → authorize → token → authenticated `/api/mcp` returning all 20 tools; 401+WWW-Authenticate; refresh; bad-code→invalid_grant — all green). tsc + eslint clean. Docs: AI & MCP page (new "Phone & claude.ai" section + status dot), runbook §1f/§3a + env table, `.env.example`.
- **Status:** built + verified locally. **Remaining:** set `LEDGR_OAUTH_SECRET` on Vercel + redeploy, then a real claude.ai-mobile connect. PR next.

## ⟢ Session summary — List paging: Load-more past the 200-row cap (2026-06-26, ADR-116, branch `fix/list-paging-load-more`)

**From Brandon's "I only see 200 notes, no way to load more" question.** Every plain list rendered through the view engine's `VIEW_LIMIT = 200` hard cap with no paging UI, and the subtitle counted the capped array, so a 743-note type read "200 notes" and stopped. The Notion import (ADR-115, same day) made the long-invisible cap bite. List UX in spirit (move-fast) but it touches the shared `views.ts` engine, so logged as **core-adjacent** with a Tyler ack flag (additive, all list invariants preserved).
- **Engine (`src/lib/views.ts`):** `VIEW_LIMIT` stays the 200 default page size; new `VIEW_MAX = 2000` hard ceiling; `viewItemsQuery` clamps to `[1, VIEW_MAX]` so a render can grow its window. New pure `parseListWindow(?show=)` (floor one page, clamp ceiling).
- **UI (`src/components/lists/LoadMore.tsx`, new):** "Showing X of Y" + a `scroll={false}` Load-more link that bumps `?show=` a page at a time, preserving lens/rev/filter params; renders nothing when all shown; a "narrow with a filter or search" note at the ceiling (no client JS, Principle 5). Wired into `/notes`, `/links`, `/list/[type]`, `/events`, each now fetching the true `countViewItems` total so the subtitle is honest.
- **Verify:** `verify-list-paging.mts` ALL PASS on Neon (clamp params; default returns one page; grown window returns all 205; count = true total; throwaway owner torn down). LoadMore static-render check, no DB (href growth, param preservation, last-page remainder, empty-when-done, ceiling message). tsc + eslint clean.
- **Deferred:** user-defined page size (Brandon's second ask) onto the view/lens surface where `listTabs` lives; `/items` browse + `/tasks`/dashboard widgets share the cap (less likely to hit it) and can page later. **Status:** built + verified; PR'd + merged to main this session.

- [x] **Blocks become buckets — a drill-down page per collection. ✅** Card shows a capped preview + **"Showing N of M →"** linking to `/items/[id]/collection/[key]` (full list, same `boundFilter` uncapped, multi-select + bulk per ADR-118). Each collection card (Tasks/Docs/Meetings/Milestones/Links) shows a capped preview and, when there's more, a **"Showing 5/20"** affordance; clicking the card (header/count) opens a dedicated **`/list/project/[id]/[collection]`** page (e.g. `…/docs`) listing *all* items of that collection for that project as a clickable list. Same for every box. **Why:** a project with 20 docs / 30 tasks stays manageable on the home page but nothing is buried. Reuses the same `boundFilter` set the card already pulls (just uncapped + full list layout — likely a `ViewRenderer` list with the project+type scope). **Open — confirm the click routing:** clicking a *project card* in `/list/project` opens the project home (current behavior); clicking a *collection block* opens its bucket page. Tyler's note also implies the bucket page is where a task/sub-record "card click" should land — nail down exact click targets before building.

On hover, each card shows a **gear** next to the existing `RemoveSection` ×; behind it, a per-widget option for the preview count. **Default 5 for every block.** This is the cap that feeds the "Showing 5/20 → click to see all" behavior above (fills in the stubbed per-widget options / Behaviors gear noted in [[project-type-widget-model]]). New **Notes** card on the event canvas (`MeetingNotes.tsx`); the contain route bubbles a note jotted on a meeting up to the meeting's project (home stays the meeting, adds a `contains` edge to the project via `homeParentRecord`), so it shows in the project's Docs. Transcripts unchanged. When a user takes notes tied to a meeting on a project, those notes should surface in the project's **Docs** block (not only under Meetings). Needs a routing convention: a note created in a meeting context gets contained by the project (home/role edge) AND typed/tagged so `boundFilter` sorts it into Docs. Confirm how "meeting notes" are modeled today (a `note` related to the `event`? a body section?) before wiring the binding.

(Confirmed the intent: done pinned to bottom, date within each group — not date-primary.) Completed tasks should never sit above open ones. **Recommended sort: completion status primary (open before done), date secondary within each group** — that's what guarantees "done always at the bottom." *(Tyler's phrasing said "date first, then completion," which would interleave done tasks by date and NOT keep them at the bottom — flagged to confirm which he means before implementing.)* Applies to the `TasksWidget` ordering first; consider making it the default task sort everywhere.

### Overview = a living after-action report (Tyler, 2026-07-01) — designed, not built

Full write-up: `explorations/overview-after-action.md`. The project Overview becomes a two-zone after-action report that accrues as the project runs: a readable human summary (Head) on top, the detailed time-stamped record (the PJ1 activity log) on the bottom. **Deterministic code captures the substrate; AI narrates it** — Ledgr never embeds a model or an API key. Design decisions locked with Tyler: (a) AI on a cron is fine **but must run on Tyler's Claude subscription, not a metered API** → the schedule + model live on the Claude side (a claude.ai/Desktop scheduled automation over the ADR-117 MCP connector now; local Claude Code headless in Phase 4), Ledgr only exposes the work queue over MCP; (b) **milestones are BOTH dated deadlines AND stampable** — date optional (a hard target when present), completion is a manual stamp (`reached_at`), a stamp doubles as a chapter boundary for the weave.

- [ ] **Shippable now (non-core):** one-line `recap` field on meetings/notes + deterministic render; the activity-log timeline as the Overview's bottom zone (period-grouped) + richer payloads + a "minutes recorded" event; a deterministic digest header (interim top) via `composeDigest`; the "awaiting summary" flag surfaced (view + Notification Center badge) so a pasted transcript can't be forgotten.
- [ ] **Core (needs joint ADR + Brandon):** the milestone model change (optional date + manual stamp; progress reads the stamp; stamp = chapter boundary — `milestone` is a shared system type); the MCP tool to list pending summary work + the scheduled-Claude drain (reconcile with ADR-052 scheduled Claude tasks).
- **Handles, not summaries** (how to show "what the note said" without dumping it): first heading/first line, a designated `## Recap`/`## Decisions` section, or the recap field — surface what the human already marked; the nuanced "how did it go" is the AI/MCP layer's job.

## ⟢ Session summary — Floating table of contents (2026-06-26, ADR-114, branch `claude/floating-toc`)

**A Notion-style floating outline, built automatically from a document's headings, per-type and synced across devices.** Non-core (UI/UX + per-owner prefs, move-fast-solo); **no schema/migration** — the per-type config rides the existing owner-scoped `users.settings` blob, the same posture as `listTabs`.
- **Storage:** `users.settings.tocByType: Record<typeKey, {enabled, levels}>` with a tolerant `parseTocByType`, a `tocForType(settings, key)` resolver, and a focused per-type write route `PATCH /api/types/[key]/toc` (`{config}` saves, `{config:null}` resets) — mirrors the list-tabs route. **Default = auto-on, all levels, gated to ≥2 headings** (turn off per type in Build → Types via the new `TocSettingsEditor` panel).
- **One component, two presentations (`src/components/canvas/FloatingToc.tsx`), mounted once in `ItemCanvas`** (universal across every canvas type) inside a `[data-toc-scope]` wrapper: a desktop **right-edge rail** (CSS `group-hover` expand) on `lg`+, a **floating button → bottom sheet** on phone/narrow and in the intercept **modal**. Reads the live `.ledgr-prose` DOM (tracks edits via MutationObserver), drives scroll + active-section tracking against `getScrollParent` (window on the page, the modal's scroll div in the modal), never mutates ProseMirror's heading nodes, self-gates below 2 headings.
- **Verify:** tsc + eslint clean. In-browser on Neon (dev-auth, a 12-heading "Record stores" note): desktop rail (12 marks, H1/H2/H3 widths, hover labels), jump lands at the 72px offset, active mark follows scroll; mobile (375px) rail hidden + button + sheet, tap jumps to 16px offset + closes; intercept modal reads its own body and scrolls the modal container (page behind unmoved); Build panel save persists `{enabled,levels}`, H3-off drops H3 from the outline (12→9), disable removes it, reset restores default; 1-heading note shows nothing. Test notes soft-deleted after. Logged as **ADR-114**.
- **Deferred (in ADR-114):** per-item override (Notion's "This page" vs "New pages"); levels beyond H3; width-based (vs viewport-based) rail/sheet switch. **Status:** built + verified on branch `claude/floating-toc`, PR pending.

## ⟢ Session summary — Effective plan date default + task canvas arrange restored (2026-06-24, ADR-109, branch `fix/effective-plan-date-and-task-canvas-arrange`)

**Two fixes from Brandon's "due vs scheduled" question.** (1) The whole app now defaults to the **effective plan date** (`scheduled ?? due`) for tasks, scheduled-primary and due-secondary, and never breaks when due is unset. (2) The **DnD "Customize layout"** editor works again on the task type (it regressed when ADR-108 moved tasks onto the bespoke rail).
- **Part A (CORE, additive, Tyler-flagged):** a first-class **`plan`** date option = `COALESCE(scheduled_date, due_date)`, added to the view engine's `DATE_PROPERTIES`/`SORT_FIELDS`/`GROUP_FIELDS`/`COLUMN_FIELDS` and made the **default** date field for tasks (filter window, calendar/agenda placement, builder offers it first). One `PLAN_DATE` SQL expr; UTC calendar-day like due/scheduled. The two stragglers now agree: `/tasks` uses `scheduled ?? due` (was `min`) + sorts by `plan`; the "Tasks Today / Overdue / Upcoming" starter widgets filter+sort by `plan`. A both-dates user still names `dateField: dueDate` for a pure-deadline view (the *default* changed, not the columns). MCP `list_items` `dateField` doc updated; the enum addition is additive to the contract.
- **Part B (non-core):** `TaskCanvas` mirrors `MarkdownCanvas`'s dispatch — `arrange || saved canvas_layout` → delegate to `MarkdownCanvas`'s `react-grid-layout` cards (the `LinkCanvas` wrap pattern); else the ADR-108 compact rail (the default). Reset (layout → null) returns to the rail. No new grid code.
- **Verify:** `verify-views.mts` extended + green on Neon (plan accepted as field/sort/group; no-`dateField` default is `plan`; scheduled-past = overdue; future-scheduled-past-due = NOT overdue (scheduled wins); due-today & scheduled-today both land today; `dateField: dueDate` still flags past-due; sort-by-plan ordering; coalesce in SQL). `verify-canvas-seam` (fixed a stale "task falls back to default canvas" assertion — task has its own canvas) + `verify-canvas-layout` green. tsc/eslint/`next build` clean. Server-HTML eyeball (dev-auth on Neon): default task = rail (no grid), `?arrange=1` = grid + arrange UI, `/tasks` lists, builder shows "plan date (scheduled, else due)". (Preview browser's Suspense fallback didn't resolve under dev streaming — harness quirk; server returned 200, fetched HTML correct.) Logged as **ADR-109**.
- **Known limitation (deferred):** the grid card vocabulary has no focus-star / scheduled-time / reminder card, so a task *arranged into the grid* loses those rail-only controls (matched the pre-ADR-108 grid; add cards later). **Deferred earlier idea (not built):** a per-type "hide the due field / scheduled-only mode" toggle (status_mode-style) — unneeded since the effective-plan-date default already gives Brandon the behavior; revisit if the UI declutter is wanted.

## ⟢ Session summary — Task rail redesign: compact rows + Schedule popover (2026-06-24, ADR-108, branch TBD)

**The task canvas right rail went from ~7 always-expanded sections to a tidy list of one-line rows; date/time/repeat/reminder now collapse behind a single "Schedule" row's popover (Todoist's pattern, from Brandon's screenshots).** Non-core (UI/UX polish of the `task` canvas only); no schema, no migration, no dependency; the underlying scheduled/time/repeat/reminder writes + endpoints are untouched (reused verbatim in `bare` mode).
- **Rows:** Status (done-checkbox in checkbox mode, swatch-menu `StatusRow` in select mode, nothing in none — ADR-106) · **Schedule** (`SchedulePopover`, summary "Thu, Jun 25 · 8:18 PM · ↻ Daily" → popover with Date/Time/Repeat/Reminder) · **Due** (`DueRow`) · **Priority** (`PriorityRow`, None + P1–P6 swatch menu, replaces the bare select) · Relations + custom props (existing editors, same rhythm) · **Focus today** (kept a visible one-tap star, not tucked).
- **Completion moved to the title (Brandon's follow-up):** a Todoist-style circle next to the title, **colored by priority** (P1 red…P6/none neutral), fills with a check + **strikes through and dims the title** when done; the redundant rail Status row is dropped in checkbox mode. `TaskTitle` owns one optimistic `done` so circle + strike update instantly (then `/complete`, recurrence-aware, + refresh). The strike sits on the title `<textarea>` via a new `ItemEditor` `done` prop (text-decoration can't cross a textarea from a parent).
- **New reusable `Popover`** (`src/components/ui/Popover.tsx`): in-house (no dep), **portals to `<body>` with fixed positioning** (the item Modal is `overflow-hidden`, so an in-flow absolute popover would clip), flips up + caps height near edges, re-measures on scroll/resize. **Esc claimed in the CAPTURE phase** — fixes a real bug where Esc in the popover closed the whole modal (the Modal's bubble-phase listener ran before the popover's `preventDefault`). New `DayField` (date input + chips + NL) shared by Schedule/Due; new `formatDayLabel`.
- **Verify:** tsc + eslint clean; in-browser on Neon (dev-auth, "Review Kingdom Come outline with Tom"): rows render, Schedule popover opens un-clipped with all stored values correct, Priority pick PATCH→200 + optimistic (restored after), Esc closes popover but not modal, no console errors. Logged as **ADR-108**.
- **Follow-ups (not built):** apply the same rows to the classic `MarkdownCanvas` (event/note/link); fix `ConfirmButton`'s same latent bubble-phase Esc bug (task spawned). **Not yet committed** — on `main`, branch before committing.

## ⟢ Session summary — Per-type status display mode (2026-06-24, ADR-106, branch `per-type-status-mode`)

**Tasks default to a done/undone checkbox; multi-status is opt-in per type; the view builder stops offering Status for types that don't use it.** CORE (a `types` column + the `task` default), but **additive** and a **presentation layer over the ADR-082 category plumbing** — no boolean redesign, no change to `items.status`/`status_category` or the MCP contract. Tyler agreed up front.
- **The model:** new nullable **`types.status_mode`** = `none` | `checkbox` | `select`, resolved (never null) by `resolveStatusMode` (`src/lib/status.ts`). `status_category === 'done'` is still the one source of truth for "done"; the mode is only how a type *presents* it. `checkbox` reuses the recurrence-aware `toggleItemDone`/complete endpoint. **Defer-by-hiding:** switching to checkbox/none never rewrites the stored `status_schema`.
- **Defaults (status is opt-in):** migration **0032** + `seed.mjs` backfill `task` → checkbox, any type with a custom `status_schema` (`project` + user types) → select, **everything else → none**. ⚠️ Inherited-status user types with no custom schema (`hiring_candidate`/`paper`/`song`) defaulted to **none** — set their mode in Build if they want status (flagged in COLLAB). **`event` = none** (its default canvas has no status field today; checkbox would need canvas wiring, deferred).
- **Surfaces:** Build → Types "Completion tracking" three-way control (`StatusSchemaEditor`); the task canvas shows a done-checkbox (no select) in checkbox mode (`TaskCanvas`); the view builder gates the Status filter/group/column by the selected type's mode (`ViewBuilder`, the same rule already used for dates/urgency).
- **Verify:** `scripts/verify-statuses.mts` **35/35** (incl. 10 new mode checks); `verify-types`/`verify-views` green; tsc/`next build`/eslint clean; migration applied to Neon + backfill confirmed; **in-browser on Neon (dev-auth):** Build control (Person→None, schema editor hidden), view-builder gating (Status absent for person/note, present for any/task/project), task checkbox toggles done (non-recurring open→done→open; recurring advances), `PATCH …/statuses` round-trips + rejects a bad mode.
- **Deferred (in ADR-106):** thread `status_mode` through `ViewRenderer` so a `none`-type **table** view drops its default Status column (list/agenda/calendar already clean); a status control on the **default canvas** for non-task checkbox/select types; MCP `list_types` exposure of `status_mode`.

## ⟢ Session summary — Interface density (2026-06-24, ADR-107, branch `feat/interface-density`)

**A "Display density" setting (Compact / Default / Comfortable / Roomy) that scales the whole interface, not just the prose canvas, set separately for desktop and mobile.** Non-core (UI personalization on `users.settings`, no migration, the navSlots/favorites/textSize posture); `default` (1.0) is pixel-identical to before, so it is a safe opt-in and Tyler's instance is untouched.
- **One knob:** `--ui-scale` drives the root font-size (`html { font-size: calc(100% * var(--ui-scale)) }`). The app is rem-based, so scaling the root rem scales nav, menus, titles, buttons, and spacing together, and future UI inherits it for free. Levels map to 0.9 / 1.0 / 1.1 / 1.2 (`UI_SCALE`, `src/lib/settings.ts`).
- **Per surface:** `uiDensity` + `mobileUiDensity` (null mirrors desktop), emitted as a server-rendered `<style>` with a `max-width:639.98px` mobile override (the var must sit on `:root`, and a media query can't live in an inline style; 639.98px complements the nav's 640px `sm` line).
- **Nav geometry px → rem:** `RAIL_W`/`TOP_BAR_H`/`BUILD_SIDEBAR_W` (`src/lib/nav-layout.ts`) so the nav frame and the body clearance scale together and stay locked. Coexists with the prose `textSize`.
- **Verify:** tsc/eslint/`next build` clean (re-confirmed on apply; built + verified in the originating cloud session). Files: `settings.ts`, `globals.css`, `layout.tsx`, `SettingsForm.tsx`, `nav-layout.ts`, `NavShell.tsx`, `BuildSidebar.tsx`; logged as ADR-107.

## ✅ COMPLETE — Dashboard canvas (ADR-111 DC1–DC4) + nested-list widget (ADR-120, BUILT 2026-06-27; non-core/solo, branch `dashboard-canvas`)

**Brandon's ask:** make the dashboard MORE of a dashboard — a customizable *canvas*, not a uniform card grid — **plus** a widget that shows items with their subs/children ("N projects, plus N child tasks for each"). Built on one branch (Brandon's call). **Plan in `explorations/dashboard-canvas.md`**; decision record **ADR-120** (builds ADR-111). One additive column (`dashboards.appearance`, migration 0034); everything else rides the `widgets` jsonb or reuses shipped machinery (react-grid-layout, the autosaving `ItemEditor`, the view engine, the relations read path).

- [x] **DC1 — Per-widget appearance + collapse.** Cross-cutting optional `appearance` on `DashboardWidget` (rides `widgets` jsonb, no migration): header/border toggles, background color, accent edge, collapsible/collapsed. `WidgetFrame` branches on `effectiveAppearance` for ALL kinds (generalizes the old chrome-free `text` path); the gear popover gained an Appearance section; collapse is a **view-mode** chevron (the forced height-1 lives in `RglInner` view-mode-only, so the stored expanded height survives). Absent appearance = today's chrome (existing dashboards identical).
- [x] **DC2 — Dashboard stage.** New `dashboards.appearance` jsonb (migration 0034) + a `BackgroundPanel`/`StageBackground`: curated color/gradient swatches + image URL, scrim + blur sliders, title-visibility + density toggles. `null` = today's look; export/print ignore it (Sunday-proof). **Video + raw byte-upload deferred behind the parser seam** (curated tokens + URL ship; no no-item R2 plumbing yet).
- [x] **DC3 — Item-embed widget (any item).** New `embed` kind + `itemId`; renders the item's body via the autosaving `ItemEditor`, editable in place (title via the frame header, toggled by `showHeader`). Sticky note = embed + color + no header. `text` widget kept for labels.
- [x] **DC4 — Tab/section container.** New `container` kind (tabs/stack/section + activeTab) holding child widgets; one-level nesting; single-recursion fan-out; add/remove/configure children from inside the container.
- [x] **Nested-list (`tree`) widget — the new ask.** N parents from a backing view, each with its children listed under it; children sourced EITHER from the `parent_id` hierarchy OR a relation role (e.g. `project`, since project↔task is a relation, not parent_id); hide-completed default on; "+N more" overflow; batched body-free fetch in `src/lib/dashboard-tree.ts`.
- [x] **`image` widget** (Brandon's "header image / quote pic" ask, added after preview review). A chrome-free picture from a URL (`{url, alt, fit, link}`); for an uploaded image, embed a note and paste (uploads to R2).

**Post-preview-review fixes (PR #93):** relation role is now a **select** (from the relation fields across types), settings persist+refetch is **debounced** (was a per-keystroke `router.refresh()` racing the input), and all popovers render in a **portal** (`floating-menu.tsx`) so a short widget no longer clips the gear menu and it flips to stay in the viewport.
