MCP `add_widget` enum pinned to the original 4 kinds (contract held). COLLAB heads-up to Tyler posted (migration 0034).

## ⟢ NEXT SLICE — Archive axis (cross-cutting `items.archived_at`, own ADR)

**Brandon's decision (2026-06-24):** archive is a *different axis* from done/undone — "hide from most surfaces (search, sort, lenses, automations) but keep it recoverable," and it applies to **every type**, not just tasks. Today "archived" is only a task *status category* and doesn't even hide from search (`search.ts` excludes only `deleted_at`). Build a first-class lifecycle flag parallel to soft-delete:
- **`items.archived_at`** (hot column, partial index like inbox/`deleted_at`) → **excluded by default** from search, lists, lenses, automations; an `/archive` view + unarchive action. Active → Archived → Trash (30-day purge) as three tiers.
- **Reconcile** the existing `archived` *status category* — lean toward folding it into the flag (categories become not_started/in_progress/done), decide in the ADR.
- **Optional fast-follow:** deterministic auto-archive-after-N-days-done (cron, no model — Principle 3), opt-in per type/user.
- CORE (new column + a new "exclude archived by default" query invariant) → both-agree + its own ADR before building.

## ⟢ Session summary — List lenses: per-type tab strip (2026-06-24, ADR-105, branch `list-lenses`)

**Every type's list page now has a `/tasks`-style tab strip ("list lenses"), customizable in Build.** Non-core (per-instance config in `users.settings.listTabs`, the navSlots/favorites posture); no migration, no dependency. The one shared-file touch is additive sort modes in the view query builder (`views.ts`), flagged for Tyler in COLLAB; the `ViewSort`/MCP `create_view` contract is unchanged.
- **Two lens kinds:** a **sort** lens (orders the plain list) and a **view ("widget")** lens (renders a saved view via the existing `ViewRenderer` — board/calendar/agenda/table as a tab). Four virtual sort defaults every type gets free: Recent · Newest · A→Z · Most linked; the active sort tab has a direction toggle that inverts it in place (Brandon's call over duplicate per-direction tabs). URL-driven (`?lens=&rev=`), server-rendered, no client JS.
- **Customize in Build → Types → edit** (`ListTabsEditor`): reorder/rename/remove/add/reset; add a built-in field, a type property (text/number/date/select), or a saved view (a "＋ New view" link goes to `ViewBuilder`). Saves via `PATCH /api/types/[key]/list-tabs` (null/empty resets).
- **Engine:** internal `ListSort` superset adds `mostLinked` (correlated confirmed-relation count, served by `relations_source_idx`/`target_idx`) + property sort (`properties->>key`, guarded numeric cast). View-lens render reuses the `DashboardView` pattern in a self-contained `src/lib/view-render.ts` (`resolveViewLens`/`applyTypeScope`; no dashboard code touched); a missing/deleted view falls back to the default sorted list.
- **Scope:** generic `/list/[type]` + Notes + Links. **Events and Tasks keep their richer bespoke tabs.** Generalizes the older `types.default_view_id` (left untouched, defer-by-hiding).
- **Verify:** `scripts/verify-list-lenses.mts` (30+ checks: defaults, reverse, mostLinked both directions, property text vs numeric guard, view-lens scope + fallback, owner-scoping, body-free SQL, settings round-trip) green on Neon; tsc/eslint/`next build` clean; in-browser eyeball (strip + reverse + most-linked on Notes and `/list/person`, the Build panel listing real saved views as widget options, no console errors). **Remaining eyeball:** a view-lens rendering a board/calendar live (covered server-side + by `ViewRenderer` reuse; not exercised in-browser to avoid writing a lens to the real account).

## ⟢ Session summary — AI workspace shaping over MCP (2026-06-23, ADR-102, branch `mcp-workspace-shaping`)

**Claude can now SHAPE the workspace over MCP, not just file/read items** (the "the AI is FOR them" aim — a user says "make me a place to track sermons" / "set up my main toolbar" and the model makes Ledgr-correct moves). CORE (machine/MCP contract) but **additive-only** and **Tyler-approved direction**, built solo with Brandon's four design calls. Resolves the write/shape half of `explorations/ai-workspace-shaping-over-mcp.md`; ADR-071 was the read/apply half.

- **Eight new tools (12 → 20), all thin wrappers over the owner-scoped Build libs** so safety lives in the `parse*` validators (the model can't persist an illegal config): `describe_workspace`, `create_type`/`update_type`, `create_view`/`update_view`, `create_dashboard`/`add_widget`, `update_nav`. **No config delete tool** — shape by create/update only; destruction stays in the Build UI.
- **`describe_workspace` = summaries + drill-down** (Brandon's call): a compact cross-surface snapshot (types/views/dashboards/nav + the Build-tool catalog), full detail via the existing `list_types`/`list_views`. Index-backed, no `body` (Principle 8). Also closes a read-gap — dashboards + nav had no MCP read before.
- **Orientation guide as an MCP resource** (Brandon's call over a fat INSTRUCTIONS string): `server.ts` gains a `resources` capability + `resources/list`/`resources/read` (+ empty `resources/templates/list`); the stable doc lives in new pure `src/lib/mcp/guide.ts` (`ledgr://guide/workspace-shaping`). It's the unchanging "how it works"; `describe_workspace` is the live "what you have." `INSTRUCTIONS` now points at both.
- **Config-write safety** (Brandon's call): conversational confirm-before-calling + types soft-delete/edit + `describe_workspace` legibility + no delete tool. No new infra (Track-changes covers content, not config).
- **Verify:** `scripts/verify-mcp.mts` extended (20-tool list, the resources methods, and the shaping tools end-to-end on Neon with owner-scoping + parser-validation errors) — **101/101 on live Neon**. Plus a **live HTTP round-trip through `POST /api/mcp`** (both capabilities advertised, 20 tools, the guide resource reads, `describe_workspace` returns the real workspace). `tsc`/eslint/`next build` clean. No schema/migration, no protocol-version change, no new dependency. (Found + fixed a stale dev DB en route — it was 2 migrations behind main; ran `db:migrate`.)

## ⟢ Session summary — Web clipper slice 1 (2026-06-21, ADR-100, branch `claude/browser-plugin-ledgr-capture-cngt52`)

**Save a web page's readable content as Markdown into the Inbox, desktop + mobile, images stripped** (Brandon pulled `explorations/web-clipper.md` forward; "no images for now, build it later if I want"). Non-core — rides the existing machine-token API, the `link` type, and the `{format, text}` body; no schema/seam change.

- **`POST /api/machine/capture`** — `api`-scoped token (same door as `/api/machine/items`), **CORS-open** (`*` + OPTIONS preflight; token is the credential, no cookies), `{url, html?, title?}` → `link` item `inbox:true` with the markdown body; extraction failure still lands URL-only. Covered by the existing `/api/machine(.*)` Clerk bypass.
- **Desktop = bookmarklet:** Build → AI & MCP generator (`ClipperSetup.tsx`) bakes a pasted api token into a draggable `javascript:` link client-side (href set via ref to dodge React's sanitization); reads the live DOM (no injection → no CSP block), POSTs to the endpoint.

- **Verify:** `scripts/verify-clip-extract.mts` **12/12** (markdown structure, no image syntax/refs, relative→absolute links, empty page → null); `tsc`/eslint clean; `next build` clean (capture route present). **Remaining eyeball:** drag the live bookmarklet on a few real pages + a mobile share, once deployed.

## ✅ COMPLETE — Templates redesign (TPL1–TPL5 SHIPPED 2026-06-20, ADR-093)

**🎉 The whole chunk shipped 2026-06-20 (TPL1–TPL5).** Templates are real prototype items, applied by `cloneItemSubtree` with variable resolution + apply-to-existing; authored in the normal canvas; chooser + per-type default on "+ New"; full MCP parity; a `new-from-template` dashboard action widget. **The next build chunk is "Track changes" (below).** Original design record kept for context. Recorded as **ADR-093** (CORE — reverses ADR-045's "templates aren't items"; **Tyler ratified the direction**). Builds on the existing `templates` table + `TemplateBuilder` + relation-defaults (ADR-045/050) and, crucially, **`cloneItemSubtree`** (`src/lib/clone.ts`, ADR-076 — its header already names "the subtree-aware template apply" as a planned caller) + relative subtasks (ADR-085). **Supersedes `explorations/template-property-defaults.md`.**

**The four decisions (Brandon, 2026-06-20):**
1. **Templates are real items.** A template's content is a normal `items` row + subtree, authored/edited in the same canvas as any item (real subtasks/body/properties/relations — no second editor). Apply = a deep clone. (Reverses ADR-045; **CORE**.)
2. **Variables = dates + ask-on-apply prompts + computed (full tier).** `{{today}}`/`{{today+7d}}`/`{{now}}`/`{{nextSunday}}`/`{{title}}` tokens in title+body, each fixed-or-dynamic; relative due/scheduled offsets; `{{ask:Label}}` fill-in prompts collected on apply.
3. **Apply-to-existing = fill-blanks (default) + overwrite (explicit).** Apply to an already-started item, filling only empty fields + adding missing subtasks (Brandon's "change only the unchanged"); plus an explicit overwrite mode.
4. **Create UX = per-type default + chooser.** Each type may set a default template "+ New" uses; a chooser lists the rest + Blank.

**Industry grounding (research, 2026-06-20):** Notion (duplicate-a-prototype + `@today` "date when duplicated" toggle + default DB template), ClickUp (merge-on-apply-to-existing, per-list default, remap-subtask-dates, granular "what to import"), Obsidian Templater (`tp.date.now(fmt, offset)` + `tp.system.prompt`), Asana (subtasks + relative dates; notably *can't* do relative-to-parent-due, which Ledgr's S5 relative subtasks already nails).

**Slice plan (TPL1–TPL5; scope each finer when picked up — each lands with its own `verify-*` + an ADR/COLLAB note where it touches core):**
- [x] **TPL1 — Data model: templates-as-prototype-items (CORE). ✅ DONE (2026-06-20; Tyler ratified; branch `templates-tpl1-prototype-model`).** `items.is_template` (migration 0025) covers the whole prototype subtree via a `createItem` inheritance invariant (`isTemplate = explicit ?? parentIsTemplate ?? false`); `cloneItemSubtree` never copies it, so **apply yields real items**. Reshaped `templates` → thin registry `{prototype_item_id → items ON DELETE cascade, is_default, apply_config}` (migration 0026), content moved onto the prototype; **alpha reseed** (`DELETE FROM templates`, ADR-039); two-migration split sidesteps drizzle-kit's rename prompt. **Exclusion sweep** added `is_template = false` across every owner-scoped surface (`viewWhere`/`today.live`/`needsExportWhere`/`overdueWhere` helpers + list/inbox/Build-counts/search/relations/mentions/ICS/push/todoist/trash); by-id reads (getItem/subtree/clone) stay visible; the `deleteType` FK guard keeps templates counted. Apply rewired to `cloneItemSubtree(prototype)`. Minimal authoring: create = name+type → registry + empty prototype → opens the prototype canvas (old `TemplateBuilder` + edit page retired); index lists registry rows with delete; MCP `list_templates`/`apply_template` updated. `verify-templates.mts` **46/46** + `verify-mcp`/`verify-views`/`verify-recurrence` green; tsc/eslint/build clean. **Remaining eyeball:** the Templates UI loop (folded into TPL2).
- [x] **TPL2 — Authoring in the real canvas + Save-as-template + duplicate + delete. ✅ DONE (2026-06-20; non-core; branch `templates-tpl2-authoring`).** A prototype opens in the normal canvas under an amber **"Template" banner** (`TemplateBanner` in `ItemCanvas`, both variants) carrying inline rename + set-default + duplicate + delete + "All templates"; "← All items"/"Save as template" are suppressed for templates, and a template's modal hides the generic Trash (its delete is the registry-aware banner). **"Save as template"** (`SaveAsTemplateButton`) sits in the modal header + full-page chrome for real items → `POST /api/templates/from-item` → `createTemplateFromItem` clones the item's subtree into a hidden prototype + registry row (carries body/subtasks/properties/relations) and opens it. **Duplicate** (`POST /api/templates/[id]/duplicate` → `duplicateTemplate`, "Copy of …", never default) + **delete** + **set-default** as Build → Templates row actions (`TemplateRowActions`, replacing the single-purpose delete button). Rides an additive **`cloneItemSubtree({isTemplate})`** override (root-only; subtree inherits) — backward-compatible with the recurrence-materialize caller. `verify-templates.mts` **64/64** + `verify-mcp`/`verify-recurrence` green; tsc/eslint/build clean; **in-browser on Neon** (banner actions, save-as-template end-to-end, set-default uniqueness live, no console errors — also closes TPL1's deferred eyeball).
- [x] **TPL3a — Variable resolution: text tokens + `{{ask:}}` prompts. ✅ DONE (2026-06-20; non-core; branch `templates-tpl3-variables`).** New **pure, client-safe `src/lib/template-vars.ts`** — `resolveVars`/`scanAskLabels`/`hasVars` resolve `{{tokens}}` in titles + bodies: dates (`{{today}}`, `{{today+7d}}`/`-3d`/`+2w`/`+1m`/`+1y`, `{{tomorrow}}`/`{{yesterday}}`, `{{nextSunday}}`…`{{saturday}}`, `{{now}}`) with optional `:format` (iso/long/short/us/day/time), `{{title}}` echo, and `{{ask:Label}}` fill-ins. Reuses `nl-date.ts`/`recurrence.ts` (no dep). `createItemFromTemplate(ownerId, id, {answers, now})` deep-clones then resolves over the whole cloned subtree (root title first so `{{title}}` echoes the resolved title; the prototype keeps its raw tokens). New `GET /api/templates/[id]/vars`; `POST …/apply` + **MCP `apply_template`** accept `answers`; "+ New" shows `TemplateApplyDialog` when a template has `{{ask:…}}`, else applies straight through. `verify-template-vars.mts` **33/33** (pure) + `verify-templates.mts` extended (apply resolves dates/offsets/ask/title-echo over the tree; prototype untouched; unanswered→empty) all green on Neon; tsc/eslint/build clean; **in-browser end-to-end** ("+ New ▾" → pick → "Passage?" form → item "Sermon for Jun 28" with the answer + resolved date in the body).
- [x] **TPL3b — Structured due/scheduled date rules. ✅ DONE (2026-06-20; non-core; branch `templates-tpl3b-date-rules`).** Per-template apply rules in the registry `apply_config` — typed `ApplyConfig`/`DateRule` (`{ dueDate?, scheduledDate?: { mode:"none"|"offset"|"fixed", days?|date? } }`) + pure `resolveDateRule`/`parseApplyConfig` in `template-vars.ts`. On apply the clone clears the prototype's dates, then `applyDateRules` sets the root: `offset` = apply-date + N days (0 = apply day, negatives ok), `fixed` = absolute, `none`/absent = empty. **Setting the root's scheduled cascades to relative subtasks (ADR-085)** via `updateItem`. Authored from a "Dates" panel in the Template banner (`TemplateDatesControl` → PATCH `apply_config`); `updateTemplate`/`parseTemplateInput` persist it. No migration (column shipped in TPL1). `verify-template-vars.mts` **50/50** + `verify-templates.mts` extended (offset/fixed/none on apply, relative-subtask recompute apply+2+1, config round-trip) green on Neon; tsc/eslint/build clean; **in-browser on Neon** (banner "Dates" → Scheduled "Days after apply" 3 → apply → item scheduled = today+3, no console errors).
- [x] **TPL4a — Create UX: default-aware "+ New" + chooser with preview. ✅ DONE (2026-06-20; non-core; branch `templates-tpl4a-chooser`).** A per-type **default** template (registry `is_default`) is applied by the primary **"+ New"** click; a **▾** opens a chooser listing Blank + every template, the default marked **★**, each with a small **preview** (subtask count · starter body). With no default, "+ New ▾" just opens the chooser (prior behavior); with no templates, plain "+ New" (Blank). New `listTemplatesForPicker(ownerId, type)` (one query: default-first, live-prototype join, subtask count) behind `GET /api/templates?…&preview=1`; `NewItemButton` rewritten as a split button; the TPL3 `{{ask:…}}` prompt dialog rides every apply path. `verify-templates.mts` extended (picker default-first + preview fields + owner-scoping) green on Neon; tsc/eslint/build clean; **in-browser on Neon** (split "+ New"/▾; chooser shows ★ default "1 subtask · starter body" + the plain one; primary "+ New" applied the default → item with its body + subtask).
- [x] **TPL4b — Apply-to-existing (fill-blanks + overwrite). ✅ DONE (2026-06-20; non-core; branch `templates-tpl4b-apply-existing`).** `applyTemplateToExisting(ownerId, templateId, targetId, {mode, answers, now})` merges a template into an already-started item. **fill** ("change only the unchanged"): sets only empty scalars (title/urgency/url), skips a non-empty body, adds only properties the target lacks, sets due/scheduled only if empty, adds the template's subtasks the target doesn't have (deduped by resolved title), adds missing relations. **overwrite**: replaces scalars + body + shared properties + dates, still only ADDs subtasks/relations (never deletes the target's). Tokens + date-rules resolve into the merged content, `{{title}}` echoing the target's final title; new relative subtasks recompute off the target's scheduled date. Guards: same-type, not-a-template, not-trashed, owner-scoped. `POST /api/templates/[id]/apply` gained `{targetId, mode}` (else create-new); `ApplyTemplateButton` (picker of the item's type + fill/overwrite + the TPL3 `{{ask}}` fields) in the modal header + full-page chrome for real items. `verify-templates.mts` +18 (fill/overwrite field-by-field, token+relation merge, dedup, guards) green on Neon; tsc/eslint/build clean; **in-browser on Neon** (fill: kept title/body, added the template subtask beside the existing one, scheduled=today+1; dialog picker + mode radios; no console errors).
- [x] **TPL5 — MCP + surfaces parity + verify. ✅ DONE (2026-06-20; branch `templates-tpl5-mcp-widget`).** **MCP parity:** `list_templates` now reports `isDefault`, `prototypeItemId`, the template's **`askLabels`** (the `{{ask:Label}}` prompts, so a client knows which `answers` to pass), and `applyConfig` (the due/scheduled date rules, when set); **`apply_template` gained `targetId` + `mode`** — with `targetId` it merges onto an existing item via `applyTemplateToExisting` (fill | overwrite) instead of always creating, mirroring `POST /api/templates/[id]/apply`. The lib already supported both paths; the tool only needed the args (+ `templateAskLabels`/`applyTemplateToExisting` imports). **Dashboard widget:** the `new-from-template` action widget is now **addable** (a new "Actions" section in `AddWidgetMenu` → quick-capture / new-from-template / link + `handleAddAction` in `DashboardClient`, append-only, no refetch) and **configurable with real dropdowns** (the settings popover's raw text keys → a **template dropdown** from `/api/templates` for new-from-template, which also carries the chosen template's `type` into the widget's `targetType`, **and a type dropdown** from `/api/types` for quick-capture — closing Tyler's dashboards follow-up "richer action-widget pickers (type/template dropdowns vs text keys)"); an unconfigured new-from-template widget renders a "Pick a template in settings" guard rather than silently creating a blank item (`ActionWidgetBody`). The render/parse halves already existed (`ActionWidgetSettings`/`parseWidgetSettings`); TPL5 made it reachable + usable. Also fixed a latent `verify-templates` leak (its `vtmpl_other_*` type-mismatch fixture wasn't in the cleanup → it left "VOther" junk types in the registry; now hoisted to `typeKeyOther` + purged in `finally`). `verify-mcp` **71/71** (+askLabels/isDefault, apply-to-existing fill, owner-scope), `verify-dashboards` **21/21** (+templateId UUID round-trip/drop), `verify-templates` **105/105** (no longer leaks) all green on Neon; tsc/eslint/`next build` clean. **In-browser on Neon (dev-auth):** new-from-template → "Pick a template" guard → gear dropdown listed the template → picked it (guard cleared) → one click applied the template and opened the new item (title + resolved "Plan for …" body + cloned subtask); quick-capture → gear Type is now a dropdown of types (Task/Meeting/Note/…); no console errors. **🎉 Templates redesign (TPL1–TPL5, ADR-093) is complete.**

My Todoist usage (pulled 2026-06-20) is heavily recurring + checklist-style: weekly bulletin/comms workflows ("Finish Bulletin" every Thursday with ~7 subtasks), biweekly sermon edits ("every other friday" with subtasks), p1–p4 priorities, due *times* ("every thursday at 9am"). Maps well onto recurrence (RRULE incl. `INTERVAL 2` for "every other"), relative subtasks, urgency, and scheduled-time (ADR-091). **Both gaps now have a direction (2026-06-20):** (1) **cross-cutting labels → a built-in `labels`/tags field on the `task` type** (flagged to Brandon in COLLAB as a core-touch proposal, since every user wants it; shape fork: a free-tag property kind vs. `multi_select`-with-inline-add vs. tag-items-via-relations — leaning the latter two). (2) **projects → a `project` hub type** (see post-1.0 below), which also answers the project>section question; the loose GTD buckets (Waiting/Paused/Someday) become a status/select on the task or a saved view. Left here: validate the recurrence/subtask/time mechanics against my real weekly workflow (bulletin + comms + sermon edits).
- [ ] **Notes replace Apple Notes.** One `note` type, organized by relations (ADR-061), not separate "meeting note"/"song note" types. Set up the saved-view lenses ("Meeting notes" = notes related to a meeting, etc.) and confirm the in-line-body-vs-related-note split feels right in daily use. Quick-capture → `note`/`unmarked` is the Apple-Notes jot path.
- [ ] **Finish the Songs module** so I can author songs in Ledgr (chord canvas exists; song-import PDF→ChordPro spec'd in `explorations/song-import.md`).
- [ ] **Sermons bespoke type** (+ upload my existing sermons). Likely relates to `passage` (ADR-060) and to songs. Build together where it touches shared shape.
- [ ] **Projects bespoke type (1.0 — Tyler, 2026-06-20).** My Todoist "projects" are just flat Kanban task-buckets; I want a real project *hub* that ALSO gathers related notes, files, and meetings and tracks progress. A bespoke `project` type, openable, canvas = a board of its related tasks + the attached notes/files/meetings. My 5+ dev apps are the first use (status, repo, live URL, stack), but it's general (any tracked project). The *tracked* project case per ADR-061; loose ones stay emergent-from-relations. **Needs its own scoping/PRD before I'd call 1.0 done** (like the sermons PRD). Mostly my lane unless the canvas reuses core.
- *Post-1.0:* a **Books** bespoke type (build together) and promoting **ideas** to their own type *if* they earn a lifecycle (until then: quick-capture notes).

**v1.0 set:** native tasks · notes (Apple Notes replacement) · Papers (done) · Songs · Sermons · Projects (hub type). **Post-1.0 (in the list):** Books · an `idea` type.

Mostly plumbing over things already built/proposed: (a) **ingestion door = my parked API-keys work** — Atlas gets a key scoped to write `task` items and POSTs to `/api/machine/items` (ADR-066/067); this is a strong motivator to un-park that branch. (b) **auto-label = the built-in labels field** (deterministic Atlas-category → Ledgr-label map applied on ingest, no model — Principle 3); the integration basically needs the labels field. (c) register via `IntegrationDef` if I want it first-class. **Open decision: direction** — Atlas *pushes* to `/api/machine/items` (lighter, reuses everything) vs. Ledgr *pulls* from an Atlas API. Lean push. Pairs with the parked API-keys rescope above.

**CLAUDE.md working conventions — ✅ ADOPTED (2026-06-23).** Brandon's repeated norms from the call, now live in CLAUDE.md → Working conventions (Tyler heads-up in COLLAB to peek + flag):
- **"Scope the UI when you build something big."** A significant feature must be understandable to the other builder without a walkthrough, even if tucked behind *More*; don't leave unexplained bespoke controls (the cautionary case: an unlabeled event-canvas control).
- **"Defer by hiding, not deleting/commenting."** Over-built / not-ready features get hidden (scoped to a default), not ripped out.
- (Mobile-as-its-own-surface is captured as a roadmap stance in `explorations/mobile-swipe-navigation.md`, not a coding convention.)

State is an ordered id list in `users.settings` (`favorites: string[]`, no schema change, same posture as `navSlots`/ADR-056) — order = array order, membership = presence, validated/deduped/uuid-checked/capped (100) in `parseSettings`. New `src/lib/favorites.ts` (pure `isFavorited`/`addFavorite`/`removeFavorite`/`applyReorder` + DB-backed `getFavoriteItems`/`setFavorite`/`reorderFavorites`/`isItemFavorited`, body-free resolve that self-heals dead ids) + `GET/POST/PATCH /api/favorites`. **Favorites nav slot opens a flyout, not a page** (Brandon's call): `NavShell` special-cases `FAVORITES_HREF` into `FavoritesFlyout` (same hook as the `/search` palette slot, reusing the tools-popover open-state + outside-click closer) — fetch-on-open, each row links to its canvas, **pointer-event drag reorder** (mouse + touch, no DnD dep — Principle 5) → `PATCH /api/favorites`, unstar-in-place. Added to `DEFAULT_NAV_SLOTS` + the destination picker. **Star toggle** sits in the canvas `ItemActionsMenu` beside Lock (optimistic → `POST /api/favorites`); page + modal resolve membership via `isItemFavorited`. `tsc`/eslint clean; `verify-nav-and-deletes.mts` favorites pure checks all PASS. **Remaining:** in-browser eyeball (drag on a touch device; flyout in each nav layout).

Generalize it so **any nav slot/destination can opt into a trackable number**, with a configurable source: (a) the inbox/untriaged count for a given type, (b) the count of a saved view, or (c) an "in-process" count for a type (items not in a done status category). The config rides the existing owner-configurable nav slots (`settings.navSlots`, additive — same pattern as ADR-056, non-core nav chrome, no ADR needed). **Perf rule:** counts must be cheap — index-backed, owner-scoped, no `body` (Principle 8). Pairs with the existing `inbox` flag (a per-type "inbox" is just `type=X & inbox=true`). Raised by Tyler 2026-06-20; general enhancement both instances would want — worth a quick heads-up to Brandon, but solo-buildable.
- [ ] **Notes cleanup in Data Hygiene.** A feature on the Data Hygiene page (`/build/hygiene`, Build → MAINTAIN) that surfaces notes no longer needed, for either **cleanup** (→ Trash, soft-delete + 30-day recovery, never hard-delete) or a long-term **archive** (kept but out of the way). A **deterministic cron** computes the candidates — plain rule code, no model (Principle 3: no AI in a cron); it only *surfaces* a review list, the user confirms the action (matches soft-delete-only + the no-silent-failures / staged-for-review ethos). Scheduler = the Vercel daily cron or the GitHub-Actions authenticated-endpoint pattern (provider seam). **Candidate rules:** untouched for N days, orphan notes (no relations/links/mentions), empty or near-empty, never opened since creation, lingering `unmarked` Inbox stubs. **Archive shape:** `status_category` already has an `archived` value (ADR-082, currently task-side) — reuse it for notes, or a dedicated archive flag/tag; pairs with the labels/tags proposal. Raised by Tyler 2026-06-20; general (both instances).

Rides the Templates redesign (ADR-093) directly — each built-in is a **seeded prototype item + registry row** (like `seed.mjs` seeds types), so this is *content*, not new mechanism (non-core; worth curating the set with Brandon, but no ADR). Uses the variable + date-rule machinery already shipped (`{{today}}`, `{{nextSunday}}`, `{{ask:Label}}`, offset due/scheduled). Starter set (scope ~4–6 first, expand later):
  - **Meeting / Agenda** (`meeting`) — title `Meeting — {{today}}`; body sections *Agenda · Discussion · Decisions · Action Items*; `{{ask:Attendees}}`. Action-item lines promote to tasks via the existing block-anchor promote (ADR-090).
  - **1:1 Check-in** (`meeting`, recurring-friendly) — *Wins · Challenges · Priorities this week · Follow-ups from last time*; relate to the person. A repeatable cadence template.
  - **Weekly Review** (`task` with subtasks) — GTD-style subtask checklist: clear inbox · review calendar · review projects · pick next actions · someday/maybe; scheduled `{{nextSunday}}` (or set recurring weekly).
  - **Daily Note / Journal** (`note`) — title `{{today}}`; sections *Top 3 · Notes · Reflections*. Supports the Apple-Notes-replacement workflow.
  - **Sermon prep** (`sermon`, once the type exists) — the shape scaffold from `explorations/sermons-module-prd.md` (sticky statement + intro + 3 points). Ties the sermon-shape library to the template system.
  - **Project kickoff** (`project`, once the type exists) — *Goal/outcome · Milestones · Stakeholders (relations) · Initial tasks (subtasks) · Status*; a dev-app variant pre-fills repo/live-URL/stack properties.
  - *Stretch:* Event plan (VBS/Fall-Festival style task+subtasks), Reading/book notes (summary · key quotes · takeaways), Decision one-pager (problem · options · decision · rationale). Seed per-owner on setup (or a catalog to copy from); ADR-093 TPL4's "default + chooser" surfaces them at "+ New". Raised by Tyler 2026-06-20.
- [x] **Canvas tabs (Google-Docs-style, across the top). ✅ BUILT on branch `canvas-tabs` (ADR-095); in-browser eyeball pending.** CORE-ADJACENT (Brandon agreed before build). Marker-based (`<!-- tab: Title -->`) sections of the one body; pure codec `src/lib/editor/canvas-tabs.ts` (verify 19/19) + `TabbedBody.tsx` wrapper; auto-on for `note`, attachable elsewhere via the `tabs` capability (ADR-051); readers flatten markers to `## Title`. No migration. PRD: `explorations/canvas-tabs.md`. Original ask: Split one item's canvas into named tabs (a tab strip + "+ Add tab" across the top, a per-tab inline title), each a section of the *same* markdown body. Tyler's case: 5–6 lyric versions + notes on one song's note. **Tabs = named sections within the single body** (the canonical-body contract holds; all readers see the whole doc if the delimiter is render-safe), auto-on for `note` and attachable to any type via the ADR-051 capability seam. Encoding fork to settle: invisible tab marker (robust, lean this) vs `# H1`-as-tab (upload-friendly — use as the import path). Likely **no migration** (tabs live in the body; active-tab ephemeral or a tiny property). Raised by Tyler 2026-06-20.
- [ ] **Markdown editor chrome redesign + SVG toolbar icons (editor-wide).** Tyler doesn't like the current editor's **boxed black surface** (the `rounded border border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-950` wrapper in `MarkdownEditor.tsx`, Image #13). Redesign: a **clean, borderless typing surface** (no dark box), **auto-growing height** (no fixed min-height), and a subtle **bottom divider line** separating the text field from the footer actions (e.g. `+ Relate` / `+ Task` on the default canvas). The **toolbar** redesigned to feel integrated/"belonging," with a **consistent inline-SVG icon set replacing the current text/emoji labels** (B/I/S/H1/H2/lists/Tasks/quote/code/Table/Image/Link + the Color/Highlight dropdowns) — no icon-font, no dependency (Principle 5). **Editor-wide:** `MarkdownEditor.tsx` renders on every markdown surface (notes, the summoned task description, papers, the changelog notes, scratch), so this is general polish, not tasks-specific — both instances benefit; a heads-up to Brandon, but non-core (no ADR). Pairs with the tasks capture-card/row SVGs (those are separate, task-only icons). Raised by Tyler 2026-06-21.
- [ ] **Configurable editor toolbar (User Settings, app-wide).** A User Settings section — **same pattern as the tasks "Quick Add"** (a "Show toolbar items" list, drag-to-reorder, each − to hide; a "Hidden" pool, each + to show) — letting the user **show/hide (and order) every toolbar item above the canvas**: Bold · Italic · Strikethrough · H1 · H2 · Bullet list · Numbered list · Tasks · Quote · Code · Table · Image · Link · Color · Highlight (· the @-mention hint). **The choice applies to `MarkdownEditor` on every canvas across the app** (notes, task description, papers, changelog notes, scratch). Hidden items optionally live behind a **"…" overflow** in the toolbar (the capture-card kebab pattern) so they're still reachable. **Storage:** additive jsonb on `users.settings` (`editorToolbar: {shown: [...], showOverflow?: bool}`), tolerant parse — the `navSlots`/`quickAddActions`/`highlightGradient` pattern (ADR-056). Ideally a **shared reorderable show/hide settings component** reused by both Quick Add and this. Non-core UI; general (both instances); no ADR. Raised by Tyler 2026-06-21.
- [ ] **Drag-and-drop on board *widgets* (and any board surface).** The board *view* has full DnD (`src/components/views/BoardDnd.tsx`, ADR-072 desktop HTML5 + K1/K2 touch long-press), but a board rendered as a **dashboard widget** is read-only by design: `ViewRenderer` exposes a `boardDraggable` prop that defaults off and `WidgetBody` leaves it off (`src/components/views/ViewRenderer.tsx` ~line 685, "dashboards leave it false, so a board widget stays read-only"). So a tasks-as-board widget can't drag-to-re-bucket. Make DnD available on **any** board rendering, widget included: flip `boardDraggable` on for the widget path and reuse the existing `BoardDnd` drop logic (`boardDropPatch`, optimistic + revert). Confirm the optimistic patch + `router.refresh()` behave inside the dashboard grid (no view URL context the way the full view has). Non-core (one widget surface, no new dependency, matches ADR-072's no-DnD-lib stance); no ADR. Raised by Brandon 2026-06-23.
- [ ] **Calendar views/widgets default to full available width.** A calendar view renders inside the standard reading column (the view page wraps content in `mx-auto w-full max-w-5xl`, `src/app/views/[id]/page.tsx` ~line 92), which suits tables and lists but **squishes the month grid** (`CalendarLayout` / `grid grid-cols-7` in `src/components/views/ViewRenderer.tsx`). A calendar wants room. Default calendar (and likely board/timeline) layouts to **expand to the max width available** (full page / container) rather than the narrow reading column. Direction: let a layout declare a "wide" width preference the page honors (raise/drop the `max-w-5xl` cap for those layouts); widgets already fill their grid cell, so this is mostly the standalone view page. Non-core (view chrome/CSS); no ADR. Raised by Brandon 2026-06-23.

## ✅ COMPLETE — Track changes: version history + diff + undo (TC1 shipped 2026-06-20; TC2 source-tag dropped by decision)

**The next build chunk now that the Templates redesign (TPL1–TPL5) is complete.** Came out of an AI-friendly-editing exploration with Brandon (2026-06-20).

**Exploration outcome (so it isn't re-litigated):** **not** building an in-app AI sidebar / "the bar" right now. A bespoke embedded assistant can't ride Brandon's Claude subscription (Anthropic bans subscription OAuth for third-party apps; only Claude.ai / Claude Code / Claude in Chrome qualify), and paying per-token API on top of a plan he already pays for doesn't make sense for now. Instead the AI-editing workflow leans on surfaces that **do** run on his plan, all over the **existing Ledgr MCP server**: (a) **Claude in Chrome + Ledgr MCP** (sees the page, writes back via `update_item`); (b) **Claude Code / cowork + Ledgr MCP** (most capable today); (c) **local read + deliberate check-in**. Revisit an embedded sidebar later (likely the headless-Claude-Code path) once Phase 4 gives Ledgr a local runtime.

**Why track changes is the build, regardless of the above:** Brandon named undo as a general item-view gap, and it's the prerequisite for every editing round-trip we *did* keep — the "check-in = import" write-back should land as a labeled revision, and any future AI-apply flow rides the same rail (propose → confirm → snapshot → write → "show changes" → undo).

**What already exists:** Phase-1 **revision snapshots** — bodies snapshot to `revisions` on save (debounced, capped ~50 per item). So the data is being written; what's missing is the user-facing surface.

**Scope (slice finer when picked up; ADR/COLLAB note if any of it touches core):**
- [x] **TC1 — Version-history panel + "Show changes" diff + undo/restore. ✅ DONE (2026-06-20; non-core; solo).** The whole user-facing surface over the snapshots every save already writes (the panel "list snapshots, select any two, diff, restore" — all three original bullets). A pure, dependency-free **`src/lib/diff.ts`** (word-level: common prefix/suffix trim → LCS on the middle, size-guarded coarse fallback — Principle 5); a new **`GET /api/items/[id]/revisions/[revisionId]`** returning a snapshot's markdown text (`getRevision`, the by-id read path — the list route stays metadata-only per the no-`body`-in-list rule); and **`HistoryPanel`** (`src/components/canvas/HistoryPanel.tsx`) wired into both canvas paths (classic + the arrangeable grid via a new `history` card in `canvas-layout.ts`). The panel is **lazy** (fetches the list only when expanded, so opening an item costs no revisions query — Principle 8): lists snapshots newest-first with relative timestamps + a count; **"Show changes"** renders the word diff (added green / removed red-strikethrough, a `+N −M` word summary) between any version and the current body, with a `→` selector to repoint the comparison to any other version (covers current-vs-previous + any-two); **"Restore"** (the general item-view *undo*) goes through `ConfirmButton` → `POST …/restore`, which force-snapshots the current body first so the restore is itself undoable, then reloads to re-seed the editor. `scripts/verify-revisions.mts` **38/38** (the diff reconstruction invariant — drop `add`→old text, drop `del`→new text — across many shapes incl. the large-input coarse path; plus the create-snapshot/getRevision/restore-to-older/owner-scoping service paths on Neon); `verify-canvas-layout` updated (the `history` card) + green; tsc/eslint/`next build` clean. **In-browser on Neon (dev-auth):** expand → "· 2" → Show changes on the older version → inline diff (`*original*`→`**current**`, added third point, `+11 −4`) → switch target → no-change path → Restore confirm → body reverts + count 2→3, no console errors.
- [x] **TC2 — Tag each revision's source — DROPPED (Brandon, 2026-06-20).** Decision: *"I don't think it matters WHERE it came from, as long as we have an undo/revision history, I can undo a catastrophic change."* So no `revisions.source` column, no ADR, no core touch. The underlying goal still holds for free: **every write — manual, import, or AI-via-MCP — already routes through `updateItem`/`createItem` and snapshots a revision, so any of them is undoable in the panel** (just unlabeled). The "tie-in" need (a catastrophic AI/import edit is recoverable) is met by the existing snapshot + restore, not by labeling.

**🎉 Track changes chunk COMPLETE (TC1 shipped; TC2 dropped by decision).** Fully non-core (UI + a pure diff lib + a by-id revision read + a `history` canvas card); no schema change, no migration, no dependency, no ADR.

> **Note on granularity (existing behavior, not introduced here):** snapshots are debounced (~5 min, cap 50/item — `REVISION_DEBOUNCE_MS`), so not every intermediate keystroke-burst is its own version; restore recovers the nearest captured snapshot. A catastrophic edit is recoverable because the prior good snapshot is in history; restore force-snapshots the current body first, so the restore is itself undoable.

## ⚠️ TURNING POINT — the Markdown epoch (v0.18, ADR-037, 2026-06-13)

**Read this before anything below.** After a long Brandon↔Tyler conversation, two foundational decisions changed and Ledgr became a two-builder project:

1. **Canonical body format is now Markdown** (extended dialect: GFM + inline HTML for sermon colors + ChordPro per type), not BlockNote JSON. *(As written in 2026-06-13 this also claimed Pandoc footnotes/superscripts/citations; none were ever built outside Papers, and ADR-190 removed them from the dialect. See CLAUDE.md for the current contract.)* Markdown is the source of truth; Word/PDF/chord-chart/slides all render *from* it.
2. **Bespoke-first with per-type canvases** (a type may declare its own canvas), not "Notion-default / one editor canvas." One customizable catch-all type for the tail, with a promotion path.
3. **Two builders** (Brandon + Tyler), shared codebase, separate single-tenant deploys. Core changes need both-agree + an ADR (see CLAUDE.md "Building together"); `COLLAB.md` holds rotating plans.

Full record in **ADR-037**; PRD bumped to **v0.18 "Markdown epoch"**; git tag `v0.17-blocknote-canonical` marks the last pre-pivot state; a GitHub Release marks the turn. **The docs are now updated; the code is not.**

**The next build work is the foundation rework** (roadmap **Phase M**), which gates the new modules (Papers, Songs, etc.) and is more pressing than opening Phase 3.

> **🚧 Alpha / build phase (ADR-039, 2026-06-13; HISTORICAL, ended by ADR-115's v1.0 production flip on 2026-06-26: data is now real, reseed-freely no longer applies).** Ledgr was pre-production then with **no data to protect.** The original "each slice leaves the app working / careful staged migration" framing is dropped: get fully onto Tiptap/markdown the cleanest way, convert-or-reseed dev data freely, no DB-copy dry runs. **M3 and M4 are collapsed into one direct cutover.**

- **M1 — Editor selection (spike + ADR)** ✓ **decided (ADR-038, 2026-06-13, accepted — Brandon + Tyler agreed): Tiptap + first-party `@tiptap/markdown`.** Custom marks via `renderMarkdown`/`parseMarkdown` emit the exact `colors.ts` inline-HTML; official Mention/slash/drag extensions for the deferred Notion feel; Savor-proven in Tyler's prod. Milkdown documented as runner-up, Lexical rejected.
- **M2 — Markdown editor stood up in isolation** ✓ **built (2026-06-13).** Tiptap v3.26.1 + first-party `@tiptap/markdown` on `/scratch/editor`, with a WordPress-style **Visual / Markdown toggle** (WYSIWYG + Ctrl-shortcuts ⇄ editable raw source). New `src/components/markdown-editor/` (BlockNote's `editor/` dir still present until M3's cutover): `TextColor`/`Highlight` marks + `LedgrMention` node whose `renderMarkdown` emits the **exact** `colors.ts` inline-HTML and `[@Title](ledgr://item/<uuid>)`; hand-rolled `@`-mention popup (no popup dep) over `/api/items`; lazy-loaded. Pure bespoke logic in `src/lib/editor/mention-markdown.ts` + `colors.ts` reverse lookups. `scripts/verify-tiptap-markdown.mts` covers the color/mention encode↔decode round-trip; `tsc` clean. **(In-browser eyeball + `next build` were closed as part of M3.)**
- **M3 — The cutover (merged M3+M4, the flip)** ✓ **done (2026-06-13, ADR-040).** All of it landed: (a) renderers read markdown via **markdown-it** (new `src/lib/markdown-render.ts` — `markdownToHtml` for print/share + `markdownToText` for FTS, with heading-shift, mention-as-span, color-HTML passthrough); (b) Tiptap wired into the real canvas (`ItemEditor` moved to `src/components/markdown-editor/`); (c) body is `{format,text}` via new `src/lib/body.ts`; (d) `mentions.ts` scans `ledgr://item/<id>` out of markdown; email-in emits markdown; (e) **dev data wiped, not migrated** (Brandon's call — start fresh, alpha; the BlockNote serializer `src/lib/markdown.ts` + the migration script were deleted, not kept); (f) `@blocknote/*` uninstalled + `src/components/editor/` deleted. Markdown is now the only path. Bonus: fixed a pre-existing export-idempotency flake. `tsc` + `next build` clean; **24/24** verify scripts; in-browser editor + share render + write round-trip all confirmed.
- **M5 — Per-type canvas seam** ✓ **done (2026-06-13, ADR-041).** `ItemCanvas` split into a thin **shell** (owner check, item load, guards, breadcrumb → resolves + renders the type's canvas) and the default **`MarkdownCanvas`** (editor + panels + chrome). Pure policy `canvasIdForType` in `src/lib/canvas-registry.ts` (default `"markdown"`, no component imports → testable), wiring `Record` in the shell with a `?? MarkdownCanvas` fallback. Proof: `link` → `LinkCanvas` (URL chip over the default — the module compose-pattern). `footerFieldsFor` extracted pure (pins the ADR-018 invariant). `tsc` + `next build` clean; **16/16** in `verify-canvas-seam.mts`; in-browser confirmed.
- **M6 — Module registration boundary** ✓ **done (2026-06-13, ADR-043).** `src/lib/modules.ts` (pure policy/contract: `ModuleManifest`/`ModuleTypeDef`/`ExporterDef`/`IntegrationDef`, the registry + `isModuleEnabled` seam, and the resolvers core dispatches through — `canvasIdForType`/`canonicalFormatForType`/`exportersForType`/`moduleForType`, import-pure so it's node-testable) + `src/lib/module-wiring.tsx` (impure canvasId→component, `?? MarkdownCanvas` fallback). **Core is the first module** (`coreModule`), so the whole app resolves type behavior through one boundary, unchanged. Canonical-body-format-per-type resolves (Tyler decision #1); per-user enable is an owner-threaded, default-on seam. Proven by `referenceModule` (exported example) + `verify-module-registry.mts` **38/38**; `canvas-registry.ts` folded in + deleted, `verify-canvas-seam.mts` still **16/16**; `tsc` + `next build` clean; in-browser link-chip + default-note both route through the boundary, no errors. **The modules themselves (Papers, Songs, …) are explicitly out of the foundational build** (ADR-042) — they come after, on top of this capability (mostly Tyler's lane). Tyler's PR #1 specs were the design input.

**🎉 Phase M is complete.** The Markdown-epoch foundation rework is done end-to-end (M1–M3 markdown cutover, M5 canvas seam, M6 module boundary).

## ⟢ NOW BUILDING — Mobile kanban drag-and-drop (long-press to drag on touch, 2026-06-19)

Brandon: kanban DnD works on desktop but not mobile. Add long-press-and-drag on touch. The board (`BoardDnd.tsx`, ADR-072) uses native HTML5 drag, which is **mouse-only** — touch never fires `dragstart`/`drop`, and mobile browsers have no native drag for arbitrary elements. Fix = add an isolated **touch** input path beside the untouched desktop mouse path (no DnD library — Principle 5, matches the board's existing "no DnD dependency" stance). The drop logic (`drop(col)`/`moveCard`/`boardDropPatch`, optimistic + revert + `router.refresh()`, `dragId`/`overCol`) is fully reused — **no API/schema/persistence change.** Non-core (one view surface, no dependency added → no ADR); solo.

**Gesture:** `touchstart` → ~400ms long-press timer; finger moves >8px before it fires → scroll/tap, cancel (browser scrolls); timer fires (held still) → arm (haptic + lift), `preventDefault` from here; `touchmove` → hit-test column under finger (`elementFromPoint` → `[data-col]`), set `overCol`; `touchend` → `drop(overCol)` + suppress the click so the `<Link>` doesn't navigate; `touchcancel` → revert. **Key gotcha:** React's `onTouchMove` is passive, so the non-passive `touchmove` listener is attached imperatively via the container ref (`{passive:false}`), and preventDefault fires only while armed. Also tags the board `data-scroll-x` (the `mobile-swipe-navigation.md` conflict marker).

- [x] **K1 — Touch long-press drag. ✅ DONE (2026-06-19).** Pure helpers `src/lib/board-touch-drag.ts` (`exceedsMoveThreshold` scroll-vs-hold at 8px, `columnAtPoint` direct-hit + nearest-column fallback, `LONG_PRESS_MS`/`MOVE_CANCEL_PX`); the `useBoardTouchDrag(boardRef, callbacks)` hook (`src/components/views/useBoardTouchDrag.ts`) — delegated listeners on the container, **non-passive `touchmove`** attached imperatively (React's is passive, can't preventDefault), ~400ms long-press arm + `navigator.vibrate`, per-move rect-based column hit-test, click-suppression via touchend `preventDefault`, latest-callbacks ref so mid-drag re-renders don't stale the listeners. Wired into `BoardDnd` (`boardRef` + `data-card-id`/`data-col`/`data-scroll-x`), reusing `drop`/`overCol`/`moveCard` **unchanged** — desktop mouse HTML5 path untouched, no API/schema change. `verify-touch-drag.mts` **19/19**; `tsc`/eslint/`next build` clean. **Verified in a real browser** (keyless dev-auth): a synthetic long-press open→done re-buckets the card + fires `PATCH {status:"done"}`; sub-8px jitter doesn't cancel the arm; a >8px scroll and a quick tap each fire no drag/PATCH. **Remaining eyeball:** real-device feel (scroll arbitration under a thumb), which synthetic events can't reproduce.
- [x] **K2 — Floating ghost + edge auto-scroll. ✅ DONE (2026-06-19).** On arm the hook clones the lifted card into a fixed, `pointer-events-none` ghost (appended to `body`, lift scale/rotate/shadow) that tracks the finger imperatively (no per-move re-render); the source stays dimmed in place. A per-frame `requestAnimationFrame` loop auto-scrolls the board horizontally / the page vertically when the finger nears an edge, speed ramping with proximity (pure `edgeAutoScrollVelocity`, 56px zone / 18px-per-frame max), re-hit-testing as the columns slide under a held finger so off-screen columns are reachable on a narrow phone. All internal to `useBoardTouchDrag` — **no `BoardDnd`/API change**. `verify-touch-drag.mts` now **27/27**; tsc/eslint/`next build` clean. **Verified in a real browser** (dev-auth): the ghost spawns with the card's content + follows the finger (transform tracks + lift scale/rotate), holding near the right edge auto-scrolled the board 192px, the drop-target column highlights, and `touchcancel` removes the ghost with no mutation.

**🎉 Mobile kanban drag-and-drop complete (K1–K2).** Long-press a card to lift it (haptic + floating ghost), drag to a column (auto-scrolling to reach off-screen ones), release to drop — touch-only path layered beside the untouched desktop mouse drag, no DnD dependency, reusing the existing `drop`/`boardDropPatch` persistence. New: `src/lib/board-touch-drag.ts` (pure), `src/components/views/useBoardTouchDrag.ts` (hook), `scripts/verify-touch-drag.mts` (27/27). **Remaining:** the real-device feel eyeball (scroll arbitration under a thumb) — synthetic touch events verified the logic but can't reproduce that.

## ⟢ NEXT UP — Native Tasks (go fully native, ADR-073) + toward-1.0 decisions (2026-06-17)

> **🥇 #1 priority toward 1.0: getting native tasks good enough to fully replace Todoist.** This is the next thing to sort out, ahead of the rest of the 1.0 push (Notion migration, matcher UIs, the alpha→1.0 flip). Brandon, 2026-06-17.

**Toward-1.0 decisions Brandon settled 2026-06-17 (ADRs 073–075):**
- **Tasks go fully native (ADR-073, CORE).** Drop Todoist as the engine; Ledgr owns tasks end to end. Todoist stays an optional adapter behind the `tasks` seam (Tyler keeps it). Reverses PRD §4.6/§5.2. → the Native Tasks chunk below. **Flag Tyler in COLLAB to ratify the seam change before the engine merges.**
- **Microsoft-only for v1.0 (ADR-074, CORE).** Google/iCloud calendar/email adapters deferred post-1.0 (Tyler builds them in his instance when he adopts). Seam discipline stays; only the extra adapters wait. Answers PRD Q7 for v1.0.
- **No confidential tier (ADR-075, CORE).** The single-user + Clerk + owner-scoped posture is sufficient; declined for v1.0 (revisitable later / for Tyler's instance).
- **Audio capture → AI minutes is a candidate 1.0 item** (`explorations/meeting-recording.md`): **design converged + specced 2026-06-18.** Upload-only for v1, transcript-as-pivot (paste OR upload→transcribe), audio transient (compress-on-ingest + purge-after-confirm), and minutes + action-items via a **Claude-over-MCP workflow, not an in-app LLM call** (fires manually or scheduled; reuses the ADR-047/071 tools; staged as drafts / Inbox suggestions). v1a (paste-first) needs almost no new infra; v1b adds the `transcription` seam + storage. 3 CORE flags need Tyler-agree + ADR (transcription seam, audio-retention schema touch, Principle-3 interpretation). Not committed; sequence after Tasks Polish unless Brandon reprioritizes.

**✅ In-browser verification backlog cleared (Brandon, 2026-06-17).** The pending eyeballs across recent slices — customizable dashboards (ADR-064/065), typed relations (ADR-067), item-view inline editing + canvas layout (ADR-068/069), view/capture papercuts (ADR-072), AI & MCP page (ADR-066), nav v6 (ADR-056), and the editor-parity image-paste + GFM tables (ADR-040 carry-overs) — are all confirmed working. No open eyeball gates remain.

**THE NEXT CHUNK — Native Tasks (T1–T6).** Make Ledgr the task manager end to end (ADR-073). The exploration (`explorations/tasks-todoist-vs-native.md`) named what Todoist provides that native must replace; these slices replace each, plus Brandon's daily-planning asks (rescheduling, focus/Top-3). Tasks stay rows in `items` (Principle 2) — this is the recurrence/reminder/scheduling layer around them. Core (the `tasks` seam + a recurrence model); ADR-073 recorded, **flag Tyler in COLLAB to ratify before the engine merges.** Pairs with `explorations/calendar-time-blocking.md`.

- [x] **T1 — Native recurrence engine. ✅ DONE (2026-06-17, ADR-076; branch `native-tasks-t1-recurrence`).** Model (C): one task carries an RRULE + a per-date completion log; completing advances `scheduled` to the next uncompleted occurrence (deterministic, no model; no spawned clones, no overdue stacking). **`scheduled_date` is now a first-class column** (migration 0021), distinct from `due` (+ optional maintain-due-offset). **Hand-rolled RRULE subset** (`src/lib/recurrence.ts`, no `rrule` dep — Principle 5; `rrule` is the documented off-ramp) supporting daily/weekly+BYDAY/monthly/yearly + INTERVAL/COUNT/UNTIL, **fixed vs after-completion anchor**, calendar-day math (DST-safe, month-end clamp, leap year). Per-task **occurrence mode**: virtual (default) or materialized (one item per occurrence via create-next-after-completion, **deep-cloned from the series prototype** — fresh unchecked subtasks/body, carried relations — by the shared `cloneItemSubtree` primitive in `src/lib/clone.ts`, the one templates will adopt). Completion is recurrence-aware in `updateItem` (checkbox/MCP/REST all advance identically). UI: a `scheduled` field + a `RecurrenceControl` on the task canvas (both classic + grid paths). **`verify-recurrence.mts` 60+ checks** (pure engine + live-Neon service + in-browser HTTP round-trip) all PASS; `tsc`/`next build`/eslint clean; regression verifiers green. Un-defers ADR-026. (**Tyler flagged in COLLAB to ratify the `tasks`-seam direction.**) Resolves `explorations/recurrence-model.md`.
- [x] **T2 — Scheduling + rescheduling. ✅ DONE (2026-06-17, ADR-077).** Reschedule shortcuts (Today / Tomorrow / +1wk + a date picker) on the task's `scheduled` field; **natural-language date entry** (`src/lib/nl-date.ts`, hand-rolled, no dep — Principle 5: today/tomorrow/"in N days"/weekday/"next fri"/"jun 20"/m-d/ISO + year-rollover) on the scheduled field and on capture; a **deterministic overdue auto-roll** (`src/lib/scheduling.ts` — rolls open, non-recurring, effective-overdue (scheduled ?? due < today) tasks to `scheduled = today`, leaves the missed deadline; recurring excluded, they're completion-driven) as a one-click "Roll N overdue → today" on Today + `POST /api/tasks/roll-overdue` + an optional cron `GET /api/machine/roll-overdue`. **Defer/start = a future `scheduled` date keeps a task off Today until then** (Today now partitions on the effective plan date). `verify-scheduling.mts` (26 NL + live-Neon roll) all PASS; in-browser confirmed (roll button + reschedule chip persist). `tsc`/`next build`/eslint clean.
- [x] **T3 — Daily focus layer ("Top 3" / in-focus). ✅ DONE (2026-06-17, ADR-078).** A **day-stamped focus marker** (`properties.focus = {date, order?}`, no column — `src/lib/focus.ts` pure helpers; auto-clears overnight, no silent carryover) + a **"Today's Focus" zone** at the top of Today (the vital few, ordered, checkable) + a **star affordance** (`FocusStar`) on every Today task row (one tap to add/remove) + a **soft cap at 3** (a gentle nudge past 3, not a wall). Focused tasks show once (excluded from the due/Tasks list). `getTodayData.focusTasks` is an index-backed `properties @>` query. `verify-focus.mts` (pure + live-Neon focused-today query, order, owner scoping) all PASS; in-browser confirmed (star → zone, removed from Tasks). **Follow-ups (not built):** the star on the item canvas + list/board rows, a Top-3 dashboard widget (the engine exists), drag-reorder, the morning re-pick prompt (Tier-4 rhythm).
- [x] **T4 — Reminders without Todoist. ✅ DONE (2026-06-17, ADR-079).** A Ledgr-published read-only **ICS subscription feed** (`webcal://`/`https://` at `/api/ics/<token>.ics`): all-day VEVENTs for every open dated task, **recurring tasks emit the stored RRULE verbatim** (the calendar expands occurrences — why the rule is stored standards-shaped, ADR-076), a default 9am VALARM (overridable per task via `properties.reminder.minutesBefore`), CDN-cached (Sunday-proof, `s-maxage`). Per-owner token in `users.settings.icsToken` (the share-link posture — feed route is public, token is the credential; token route gated by `requireOwner`); generate/rotate/stop from **User Settings**. Hand-rolled ICS builder (`src/lib/ics.ts`, no dep — Principle 5). The web-push half is the existing morning-agenda push (ADR-034). `verify-ics.mts` (13 pure builder + live-Neon token/selection) all PASS; in-browser confirmed (feed serves 200 text/calendar with VEVENTs; unknown token 404s). **Follow-ups:** a per-task reminder *picker* UI (the data/feed already honor it), EXDATE for completed occurrences, a per-task push nudge cron.
- [x] **T5 — Offline / quick capture without Todoist. ✅ DONE (2026-06-17, ADR-080).** An **offline outbox** (`src/lib/outbox.ts`, localStorage — tiny payloads, no IndexedDB, Principle 5): Quick Capture + the capture modal **enqueue locally on a network failure** instead of erroring ("Saved offline · will sync"), and an app-wide **`OutboxSync`** (mounted in the layout) flushes on `online` / tab-visible / load, refreshing when it syncs and showing a "N queued offline" pill while pending. No Background Sync (iOS PWAs don't support it) — the flush-on-reconnect path is cross-platform. The PWA **share-target** (ADR-016) already exists. Replaces the Todoist inbox pull-in (ADR-010/026) as the offline-capture path. `verify-outbox.mts` (enqueue / flush-on-success / stop-on-offline / stop-on-error / double-flush safety) all PASS; **in-browser confirmed end-to-end** (captured while offline → queued → reconnect → drained → synced to the server).
- [x] **T6 — Seam + retire Todoist (Brandon's instance). ✅ DONE (2026-06-17, ADR-081).** A **`tasks` provider seam** (`src/lib/tasks/provider.ts`): `native` is the default adapter (Ledgr owns tasks, no sync), Todoist is opt-in via `TASKS_ADAPTER=todoist` (+`TODOIST_TOKEN`), falling back to native if unconfigured. The three Todoist sync entry points (machine cron, "sync now", webhook) **skip cleanly when native** (`{skipped:true, adapter:"native"}`, 200). `/health` gains `checks.tasksAdapter`. `.env.example` + runbook §1d + the **PRD §4.5/§5.2 reversal notes** updated. **Todoist code unchanged** (Tyler keeps it; Phase-4 packageable). `verify-tasks-seam.mts` PASS; `verify-health-check`/`verify-provider-seams` green; in-browser `/health` shows `native` + the sync skips. **Brandon's instance is now fully native (Todoist off).**

**🎉 The Native Tasks chunk (T1–T6, ADR-073) is complete — Ledgr replaces Todoist end to end on Brandon's instance.**

**Other committed 1.0 work (tracked elsewhere, not in this chunk):** selective Notion migration (Phase 3 Tier 3); the matcher setup-wizard + learn-by-confirmation UIs (Phase 2; §1c is live as of 2026-06-19, so these are now unblocked); the **alpha → v1.0 production flip** (✅ DONE 2026-06-26, ADR-115: migration-caution now in force; the no-Saturday-deploys rule was later reframed to general production-mindfulness, ADR-119).

## ⟢ NOW BUILDING — Meeting Recording (v1a COMPLETE; v1b next, 2026-06-18)

The meeting recording → transcription → AI minutes chunk (`explorations/meeting-recording.md`; Tyler pre-approved incl. the 3 core touches). Transcript-as-pivot, upload/paste-only for v1, minutes via a **Claude-over-MCP workflow, not an in-app LLM call**. Building v1a (paste-first) first, then v1b (audio transcription).

**Decisions settled (Brandon's leanings + the build):** transcript = its own `transcript` item (parent_id + a confirmed `transcript`-role edge to the meeting, so the LLM/MCP path "recent meetings → their transcripts" is one hop, not a title search — Brandon's concern); the "needs minutes" signal = a `minutes` select (none/draft/done) on the transcript; minutes land in the **meeting body**; suggested actions = real **`task`** items (inbox, related to the meeting); AssemblyAI first + 30-day retention for v1b.

- [x] **V1a-1 — Data layer. ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, ADR-087; non-core).** The `transcript` system type (migration 0023 + `seed.mjs`; `is_system`, visible, not in quick-capture; `minutes` select none/draft/done). `src/lib/meetings/transcripts.ts` (`createTranscript` writes the child + the confirmed meeting→transcript edge in one place; `listMeetingTranscripts` with SQL word count). The "Transcripts awaiting minutes" saved view via `scripts/seed-meeting-views.mts` (idempotent, seeded for the owner). `verify-meeting-transcripts.mts` **16/16 on Neon** (create + edge + minutes=none, MCP discovery both directions, word counts, the awaiting filter none-in/draft-done-out, guards). `tsc`/eslint clean.
- [x] **V1a-2 — Transcript panel on the meeting canvas. ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, ADR-087; non-core).** `MeetingTranscripts` (server) lists a meeting's transcripts (minutes badge + word count + open link) + `AddTranscript` (client paste box → `POST /api/meetings/[id]/transcripts` → `createTranscript`). Wired into `MarkdownCanvas` (classic block + a `meetingTranscripts` grid card added to `canvas-layout.ts`). Editing a transcript opens the item (full editor + the Minutes dropdown). **In-browser on Neon:** paste → "No minutes yet · 66 words" → open → editor + Minutes select → awaiting view lists it → set `done` (via the canvas `propertyPatch`) drops it. `next build` clean.
- [x] **V1a-3 — Claude-over-MCP minutes automation + Principle-3 ADR. ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, ADR-087; the CORE element).** `docs/meeting-minutes-automation.md` — the exact prompt (run the awaiting view → draft minutes into the meeting body → file action items as Inbox tasks → set transcript `minutes=draft`), manual + scheduled use, the meeting↔transcript traversal, the no-auto-commit safeguard. Surfaced as an "Automations" section on **Build → AI & MCP** (in-browser verified). **ADR-087 ratifies the Principle-3 reading**: a scheduled external Claude-over-MCP automation producing *staged* suggestions is consistent with "no AI in Ledgr's own cron" because Ledgr's backend stays model-free and nothing auto-commits.

**🎉 v1a (paste-first) is complete — ADR-087.** No new infra, no outbound LLM call: the existing 12 MCP tools express the whole flow.

**v1b — convenience transcription (✅ COMPLETE 2026-06-18, ADR-088/089). Open points settled with Brandon: defer compression, client-poll + cron backstop.**
- [x] **V1b-1 — the `transcription` provider seam + AssemblyAI adapter. ✅ DONE (ADR-088; CORE).** `src/lib/transcription/{types,provider,assemblyai}.ts`: null-safe, env-selected (`ASSEMBLYAI_API_KEY` enables; `TRANSCRIPTION_ADAPTER` selects), submit+poll job model, pure `mapTranscriptResponse` + `transcriptToMarkdown`, no SDK (two fetch calls). `/health` `checks.transcription`. `verify-transcription.mts` ALL PASS (pure fixtures, no key).
- [x] **V1b-2 — audio upload + auto-transcribe. ✅ DONE (ADR-089; non-core plumbing).** `audio/*`/`video/*` allowed with a 2GB cap; `AudioUpload` (presign → PUT → `POST /api/meetings/[id]/transcribe`); `startAudioTranscription`/`advanceTranscription` (`transcription-service.ts`, provider injected for testability) create the transcript child + edge, submit, and fill the diarized body on completion → enters the awaiting-minutes view. **Delivery = client-poll** (`TranscriptionPoller` → `GET /api/transcription/[id]/status`) **+ cron backstop** (`.github/workflows/transcription-poll.yml` → `/api/machine/transcription-poll`, every 15m). **Compression deferred** (knob behind the seam). `verify-transcription-flow.mts` 20/20 on Neon (fake provider).
- [x] **V1b-3 — audio retention. ✅ DONE (ADR-089; CORE schema touch).** `attachments.purge_after` (migration 0024); a completed transcription stamps the audio `now()+30d` (`markAudioForPurge`); the daily purge runs `purgeExpiredAudio` (R2 bytes + row); `deleteAttachment` + `DELETE /api/attachments/[id]` = delete-now. `verify-audio-retention.mts` 10/10 on Neon (spy storage).

**🎉 Meeting recording chunk complete (v1a paste + v1b audio).** In-app recording stays deferred. **Live audio→transcript eyeball is gated on a configured `ASSEMBLYAI_API_KEY` + a real recording (runbook §1i) — the pipeline is proven against a fake provider + fixtures + in-browser (panel renders, upload control correctly hidden without a key, `/health` shows `transcription: none`).**

**Possible follow-ups (not built):** surface audio attachments + their retention status ("kept until <date> · delete now") in the panel; in-app recording (the deferred capture path); a client-side WASM Whisper adapter behind the seam (free/private); compress-on-ingest as a real seam knob if storage bites.

## ⟢ NOW BUILDING — Tasks Polish (finishing touches on Native Tasks, S1–S6, 2026-06-18)

Brandon's finishing-touches pass on the just-shipped Native Tasks chunk (the dual due/scheduled dates, recurrence, statuses, capture). Direction set 2026-06-18; **Tyler greenlit all of it** (including the core items: configurable statuses, the done-checkbox semantics, relative subtasks). ADRs get written per slice as they land.

**Decisions locked (2026-06-18):**
- **Today stays dual-date** — a task lands on Today if it's due *or* scheduled by today (unchanged, Brandon's call). Scheduled is the "when I'll do it" lens everywhere else.
- **Configurable statuses = ClickUp-style, category-backed.** Baked-in categories the plumbing keys off; the user defines labels + colors inside each and marks which count as done. **Add an "In Progress" category** → Not Started / In Progress / Done / Closed. **Per type, with an inherited owner default** (ClickUp's "Inherit from Space" analog). The done-checkbox + recurrence-complete key off the Done category; a check glyph is woven into done-state statuses in the dropdown.
- **Recurrence completions = a month-calendar** (TaskNotes "Completions"): tick whichever specific occurrence dates are done, any order (maps to the per-date completion log).
- **Inverted occurrence edit** (replaces "this & future"): editing a recurring task edits the series; to shape one date, pick that occurrence → a fresh detached copy is carved out (clone machinery), the series skips that date, edits never touch the parent.
- **Relative subtasks** = a stored offset (N days after the parent's scheduled date), recomputed per occurrence — but **set by picking the date on a calendar**, Ledgr back-calculates the offset.
- **NL quick-add** = parse dates + recurrence + urgency from the task title (Todoist-style strip-and-confirm, on save + a manual trigger).
- Dropped: the "N missed" badge.

- [x] **S1 — Both dates in sort / filter / board + subtask rows. ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, branch `tasks-polish-s1-dates`).** `scheduledDate` is now a first-class sort field, date-filter field, board/agenda group bucket, and list/table column across the view engine (`views.ts`: `SORT_FIELDS`/`SORT_COLUMNS`/`DATE_PROPERTIES`/`GROUP_FIELDS`/`COLUMN_FIELDS` + `viewWhere` calendar-day handling), the grouping helpers (`view-grouping.ts` scheduled bucket reuses `dueBucket`), the renderer (`ViewRenderer` dateOf/usesUtc/columnText/labels + `ViewItem`), the board card (`BoardDnd`), and the builder (`ViewBuilder` date/sort/group/column options + labels). The **Tasks list** gained a **Date (due | scheduled)** selector that drives both the window filter and the sort, with the window relabeled **When**; rows show the chosen date (overdue-red on either). **Subtask rows** now show scheduled + due, UTC-formatted (an ADR-008 correctness fix — the old formatter had no `timeZone`). Non-core (UI + view defs). `tsc`/eslint clean; in-browser verified (scheduled sort nulls-last, scheduled+today filter narrows to one, subtask shows "scheduled … due …").
- [x] **S2 — Configurable statuses (category-backed, per-type + inherit) + done-checkbox/glyph. ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, ADR-082; CORE, Tyler greenlit; branch `tasks-polish-s2-statuses`).** `items.status` is now a user-defined key + a denormalized `items.status_category` enum (not_started/in_progress/done/archived) every hot query keys off (migration 0022 — open/done/archived preserved + backfilled). Per-type `types.status_schema` (null = inherit the default 3; the In Progress category ships empty, plumbing ready). Pure engine in `src/lib/status.ts` (+ `status-schema.ts` resolver). A `StatusSchemaEditor` on the type edit page → `PATCH /api/types/[key]/statuses` (validates + re-syncs item categories). The done-checkbox flips via a new `POST /api/items/[id]/complete` (`toggleItemDone`, recurrence-aware) so client checkboxes need no schema; the canvas dropdown shows labels + colors + a `Done ✓` glyph; the **kanban shows every custom status as a colored column in schema order**; `/tasks` filter offers `active` + the type's statuses; `ViewFilter.statusCategory` added. `verify-statuses` 26/26 + in-browser on Neon (editor persists/re-syncs, done-toggle, recurrence-advance, colored board, dropdown glyph); `verify-views`/`-structures`/`-recurrence` green; tsc/build/eslint clean.
- [x] **S3 — Recurrence completions calendar + occurrence carve-out. ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, ADR-083; branch `tasks-polish-s3-recurrence-calendar`).** A `RecurrenceCalendar` month-grid on a recurring (virtual) task: occurrence dates are buttons, **each individually checkable in any order** (toggles `properties.recurrence.completeInstances` via new pure helpers `isOccurrence`/`toggleCompleteInstance`/`addSkippedInstance`/`instanceState`), with the "up next" occurrence highlighted, done days ✓-filled, carved days struck. Plus the **inverted occurrence edit** (replaces ADR-076 §9's "this & future"): editing a recurring task edits the series; **"edit this occurrence" carves out a fresh DETACHED one-off** (`cloneItemSubtree` — fresh subtasks/body, carried relations, no recurrence, **not** linked by the `occurrence` role so the materialized machinery never fires), the series skips the date + advances, you land on the new item. Both go through **`POST /api/items/[id]/occurrence` `{action:"toggle"|"carve", date}`**, which recomputes the series' scheduled (next uncompleted on/after today) + status server-side (`toggleOccurrenceCompletion`/`carveOccurrence` in `recurrence-service.ts`). New `recurrenceCalendar` canvas card (label "Completions"), virtual-only. Non-core (operations over the existing recurrence model; no schema/MCP/seam change). `verify-recurrence-calendar.mts` 39/39 (pure + live-Neon) + `verify-recurrence`/`verify-canvas-layout` green; tsc/build/eslint clean; **in-browser on Neon** (ticked Jun 17 → advanced scheduled to Jun 24, persisted; carved Jun 24 → detached one-off w/ fresh subtask, series skipped + advanced to Jul 1). Flagged to Tyler in COLLAB.
- [x] **S4 — Natural-language quick-add. ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, ADR-084; branch `tasks-polish-s4-nl-quickadd`).** Pure `parseTaskTitle(title, today)` in `nl-date.ts` pulls **date → scheduled** (and **`by <date>` → due**), **recurrence → RRULE** (`every day/week/month/year`, `daily/weekly/…`, `every N units`, `every <weekday>`, `every weekday`), and **urgency** (`p1..p4` / `!1..!4` → critical/high/normal/low) out of a task title, returning the cleaned title + `detections[]`. Wired into **CaptureModal** (type=task): a live **"Detected" preview** row of chips + cleaned title (strip-and-confirm), a **✨ Apply** manual trigger that strips the title + commits the fields (scheduled/recurrence chips, due/urgency into the controls), and **parse-on-save** in `capture()` so an un-Applied title still resolves. Bare dates → scheduled (the plan, ADR-077); only `by <date>` → deadline. 3-letter weekday abbrevs + word-urgency excluded (collision-safe for silent parse-on-save). Non-core. `verify-nl-task.mts` 36/36 + `verify-scheduling` green; tsc/build/eslint clean; **in-browser on Neon** ("Call Bob tomorrow p1 every week" → Detected chips → Apply → Create = task "Call Bob", scheduled Jun 19, critical, weekly; parse-on-save also confirmed). Flagged to Tyler in COLLAB.
- [x] **S5 — Relative subtasks. ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, ADR-085; branch `tasks-polish-s5-relative-subtasks`).** A subtask stores `properties.relativeSchedule = {offsetDays:N}` (offset from the parent's scheduled date); its `scheduled_date` column holds the derived concrete day, so every existing scheduled query works unchanged. Set by **calendar-pick** (the new `SubtaskSchedule` control: pick a date → Ledgr back-calculates the offset; the row shows the date + a `+Nd`/`−Nd` chip). **Recomputed** (pure helpers in `relative-subtask.ts`; `recomputeRelativeChildren` in `relative-subtask-service.ts`) on parent scheduled-date change (`updateItem`), on recurrence advance / S3 calendar toggle-carve (`advanceSeriesRow`/`writeSeriesLogState`), and on **occurrence materialize** (`cloneItemSubtree` derives the date from the new occurrence's date + offset). Offsets chain to the immediate parent; clearing the parent's date clears the derived date (offset kept). Non-core (a `properties` shape + additive hooks in `updateItem`/`cloneItemSubtree`). `verify-relative-subtasks.mts` 26/26 (pure + live-Neon) + `verify-recurrence`/`-recurrence-calendar`/`-subtasks` green; tsc/build/eslint clean; **in-browser on Neon** (parent Jun 25 → pick Jun 27 stored offset 2; reschedule parent to Jul 2 → subtask shifted to Jul 4; chip "Jul 4 +2d"). Flagged to Tyler in COLLAB.
- [x] **S6 — Loose-end follow-ups. ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, ADR-086; branch `tasks-polish-s6-loose-ends`).** Closed the high-value follow-ups from ADR-077/078/079: **ICS EXDATE** (a recurring task's feed drops its completed/skipped occurrence dates — `ics.ts`/`ics-data.ts`); a **per-task reminder picker** (`ReminderControl` writing `properties.reminder.minutesBefore`, which the feed already honored); a **focus-today star on the item canvas** (reused `FocusStar`); a **"planned" marker** on the Tasks list (scheduled-but-no-deadline → `planned <date>`); and a **Top-3 focus dashboard widget** (a new `ViewFilter.focusedToday` index-backed `properties @> {focus:{date:today}}` filter + a "Today's Focus" starter widget — rides the verified dashboards engine). Non-core. `verify-ics`/`verify-views` extended + green, `verify-focus` green; tsc/build/eslint clean; **in-browser on Neon** (canvas star set focus; reminder set 1440; "planned Jun 30" on the list). **Deferred (documented):** focus star on every list/board *row*, drag-reorder of the focus zone, the optional fully-colored status popover; canvas star+reminder are classic-path only. Flagged to Tyler in COLLAB.

**🎉 The Tasks Polish chunk (S1–S6) is complete.** Dual due/scheduled dates, configurable category-backed statuses (ADR-082), a recurrence completions calendar + carve-out (ADR-083), NL quick-add (ADR-084), relative subtasks (ADR-085), and the focus/reminder/ICS/planned loose ends (ADR-086) — all shipped to prod.

- [x] **Time-blocking Stage A — scheduled time + duration on tasks. ✅ DONE (2026-06-18, ADR-091; branch `task-scheduled-time`).** Brandon's "Scheduled should have an optional time, start/end to block time and sync to my calendar" → the time-blocking exploration's **Stage A** (a single scheduled block on the task). An **optional start time + duration refines the scheduled CALENDAR DAY** (`scheduled_date` stays the zone-free UTC-midnight anchor, ADR-008, so Today/recurrence/overdue-roll/relative-subtasks/focus are untouched), stored in **`properties.scheduledTime = {start:"HH:MM", durationMinutes}`** (`properties`-first, **not core**, no migration; column promotion is the later ADR step). **Floating local time** (no zone — a single-user plan, not a UTC instant). **Due stays date-only.** Input = start + a duration dropdown with the **range shown as a hint** ("2:00 PM – 3:30 PM") for the start/end framing. The payoff lands in the **ICS feed (ADR-079)**: a timed task emits a **timed VEVENT** (floating `DTSTART`/`DTEND` = start+duration, default 60m, past-midnight rollover) instead of all-day; default alarm becomes **at-start** vs the all-day 9am; a reminder lead is minutes-before-start; a **timed recurring** series keeps the RRULE + emits **DATE-TIME EXDATE**. The time attaches **only on the scheduled day / recurrence anchor**, never a due-only event. New `src/lib/scheduled-time.ts` (pure) + `ScheduledTimeControl.tsx` (canvas extras row, gated on having a scheduled day); edited `ics.ts`/`ics-data.ts`/`MarkdownCanvas.tsx`. `verify-scheduled-time.mts` **35/35** + `verify-ics` 26/26 (no regression); tsc/eslint/next build clean; **in-browser on Neon** (set 2:00 PM/1.5 hr → `DTSTART:…T140000`/`DTEND:…T153000` + at-start alarm; clear → reverts to all-day). **Follow-ups:** surface the time on Today + Tasks-list rows; grid-canvas path; the **column promotion** (core ADR); **Stage B** (multi-session blocks as event items + the event-model reframe). Non-core, solo.
