## ⟢ Session summary — item-view UX push: inline editing (Feature A, ADR-068) ✅ DONE + arrangeable per-type canvas layout (Feature B, ADR-069) ✅ DONE

**A two-feature item-view UX push Brandon asked for (2026-06-16). Feature A shipped 2026-06-16 (ADR-068); Feature B B1 shipped 2026-06-17 (ADR-069, CORE).** Feature A is non-core; Feature B is core (a `types.canvas_layout` column + the canvas model) — both flagged to Tyler in `COLLAB.md`.

**Feature A — inline editing on the item view (ADR-068), DONE:**
- **A1 — edit a related item's title in place.** New `src/components/relations/InlineTitle.tsx`: a hover-revealed pencil (off to one side, desktop only) on Related-panel rows (`RelatedRow`) and typed relation-field chips (`RelationField`) swaps the title link for an input, optimistic PATCH + revert + `router.refresh()`. The title stays a click-through to the item for anything deeper.
- **A2 — labels-only structure fixes, by right-click ("punch through the line," ADR-063).** New `src/components/build/InlineLabel.tsx`: right-click a type label (Related-panel type-group heading) or a property/field label (field-section heading, Properties/Relations panel `dt`) → a small menu with **Rename** (inline) or **Edit in builder…** (`/build/types/[key]/edit`). Renames move only the display label via a new lightweight `PATCH /api/types/[key]/rename` over `renameTypeLabel`/`renamePropertyLabel` (`src/lib/types.ts`); the key/role is immutable, so values and relation edges are untouched.
- **Ride-along — × to clear a property value** (`canvas-drag-and-drop.md` item 3): a hover × on a filled scalar property in `CustomProperties`, with a remount key so the uncontrolled text/url/number boxes empty visibly.
- **Mobile:** inline edit is desktop-only (no pencil/right-click on touch); tap-through is the fallback (long-press rejected — fights scroll/selection).
- **Verified:** `tsc` (app) + full `next build` + eslint all clean. New `scripts/verify-inline-rename.mts` proves the label-moves-not-the-key invariant (a typed relation edge survives a field rename) **— pending a live Neon run** (no `DATABASE_URL` in this container, same posture as other integration-gated slices). **In-browser eyeball pending:** pencil-edit a related title, right-click-rename a field/type label, × to clear a property.

**Feature B — arrangeable, responsive, per-type item canvas layout (ADR-069, CORE), B1 DONE 2026-06-17.** Build brief: **`explorations/item-canvas-layout.md`**. A 2D free drag/resize grid (reusing the dashboards' `react-grid-layout` v1.5.3) over the default `MarkdownCanvas`, **field-level** (every system field / custom property / relation field is its own card; body + title + Related stay single blocks), **per type** (`types.canvas_layout` jsonb; null = today's stacked render, untouched, so it's never forced), **per breakpoint** (arrange `lg`; `md`/`sm` auto-derived + overridable; sm single-column, view-only), each card **flow** (auto-height) or **fixed** (pinned, scrolls).

**What shipped (B1):**
- **Spike first** (`/scratch/layout`, since deleted): proved the make-or-break mechanic — a flow auto-height body in RGL alongside fixed cards — by measuring the card's *natural* content height (never the RGL box), rAF-debounced, change-guarded. Eyeballed (flow reflow, fixed drag/resize, lg→md→sm, pin, localStorage persist), then graduated into `ItemRglInner`.
- **Data + pure core:** migration **0019** (`types.canvas_layout`), threaded through `schema.ts` + `types.ts` (`setTypeCanvasLayout`, tolerant parse in `rowToDefinition`). Client-safe **`src/lib/canvas-layout.ts`** (vocabulary, `defaultLayout` reproducing today's order, tolerant `parseCanvasLayout`, `reconcile` drop-removed/append-added on every read, `deriveResponsive`). **`scripts/verify-canvas-layout.mts`** 36/36 (pure, no DB).
- **Grid + dispatch:** `ItemRglInner` (graduated engine: flow measure, pin flow⇄fixed, hide/show palette, arrange toolbar, auto-save, Reset) behind `ItemLayoutGrid` (`dynamic ssr:false`). `MarkdownCanvas` dispatches classic (null layout, not arranging) vs grid (saved layout → read-only chrome-free; `?arrange=1` → editable); builds each card's content into a `Record<CardId, ReactNode>` (server panels passed through the RSC boundary). Arrange via full-page `?arrange=1` (hard nav, escapes the modal). **`PATCH /api/types/[key]/layout`** (focused, like the ADR-068 rename route).
- **Supporting:** `ItemEditor` `slot` prop (title/body as independent autosaving cards); **`propertyPatch`** atomic per-key jsonb merge (so per-property cards don't clobber siblings); `.canvas-card-content` CSS neutralizer for the panels' canvas chrome; `CanvasProps.arrange?` threaded from the page.
- **Verified in-browser against live Neon:** classic → arrange (default layout) → pin Body / hide Share → auto-save → Done (grid read-only, persisted) → reload (saved reloaded) → Reset (classic restored). `tsc` + eslint clean (new files); regression verifies green; `propertyPatch` merge round-trip confirmed on Neon.

**Deferred (later slices):** grouping/collapsible container cards (the `CardMeta` shape leaves room); polishing pin-to-fixed for the body; opening bespoke canvases (Chord/Paper) to arrangeability. B2 (top-strip/footer field placement, PRD §4.13) is **subsumed** — field-level placement delivers it.

## ⟢ Session summary — Relations chunk: typed relation properties + create-on-miss ✅ DONE (ADR-067, branch `relations-typed-properties`)

**Built end-to-end this session (R1–R4; Brandon + Tyler already agreed the design, core, ADR-067).** Two things: (1) **un-defer the `relation` property kind** so any type can carry a typed link box the user adds in the builder (a Book's "Author", a Meeting's "Attendees") — fully configurable from Build Mode alongside the other property kinds (Brandon's ask this session); (2) make **inline create-on-miss** the way you add a link to something that does not exist yet, without leaving the item you are in.

**What shipped (by slice):**
- **R1** — `relation` is the 8th `PROPERTY_KINDS`; `PropertyDef` gained `targetType` (null = any) + `cardinality` (single|many), validated shape-only in `parsePropertySchema` (defaults + a guard that a relation key can't be the reserved roles `mention`/`related`). `TypeBuilder` authors it inline per property row — a "Links to" type dropdown (fed by `listTypes()` from both type pages as `availableTypes`) + a "How many" cardinality select. `CustomProperties` skips relation kinds (their value isn't in `items.properties`).
- **R2** — `RelationField` (client) + `RelationProperties` (server) render typed link boxes after the scalar Properties panel: chips, typeahead filtered to `targetType`, cardinality enforced (single replaces), eager typed create-on-miss. New directional read `outgoingRelationsByRole` batches each field's current links; `unrelateItems` + the relations DELETE endpoint gained a `role` param so a field clears only its own edge.
- **R3** — migration **0018** + `seed.mjs` seed the hidden `is_system` `unmarked` type (glyph label `◌`, Brandon's to repick), applied to Neon. Create-on-miss rows in `AddRelation` (+Relate) and `mention-suggestion` (free-text `@`) make an `unmarked` item with `inbox=true` and link in place; an untyped `RelationField` does the same. **Mention edges stay body-owned (ADR-015):** the `@` create row only creates the item + inserts the mention node — the save-time diff-sync writes the edge, so create-on-miss never fights it.
- **R4** — `RelatedPanel` surfaces the host type's relation fields as labeled sections first (schema order, authoritative `outgoingRelationsByRole` set, role-scoped removal threaded through `RelatedRow`/`RelationActions`), then the plain type groups with `unmarked` under its `◌` glyph, sorted last.

**Verified:** `tsc` + full `next build` + eslint all clean; `verify-types.mts` extended (relation kind validation, reserved-key rejection, the unmarked type) ALL PASS; new `verify-relations-typed.mts` ALL PASS (directional role reads, role-scoped removal leaves generic edges intact, create-on-miss server path); migration 0018 applied + the `unmarked` row confirmed on Neon. **Pending:** the in-browser eyeball — add a relation field in Build → Types, open an item, exercise link + create-on-miss + cardinality, confirm the Related panel sections. **Glyph `◌` is Brandon's to repick** (ADR-067 point 4).

**Original design notes (kept for the record):**
**The settled model:**
- **One generic edge table underneath** (`relations`, unchanged). A typed relation field is just edges with `role` = the field's `key`, so typed links also show in the generic Related panel. Relation values are **not** stored in `items.properties`.
- **A relation field's config:** `targetType` (the accepted type, optional) + `cardinality` (`single`|`many`). This is the only place "classification" is declared — once per field, never per link. Hierarchy stays the separate `parent_id` tree; plain links stay unlabeled.
- **One create-on-miss rule: if the box tells us the type, use it; otherwise it's `unmarked` and goes to the Inbox.**
  - Typed field (Author box) → create-on-miss makes the field's `targetType` (eager, no prompt).
  - Free-text `@` on the canvas + generic + Relate → create an `unmarked` item, `inbox = true`, link it, stay in place. (Dropped the earlier "person by default" idea for this uniform rule.)
- **`unmarked`** is a new seeded hidden `is_system` type whose label is a **glyph** (the user never reads the word). Triage out of the Inbox = retype + clear inbox; nothing recreated. (A null `type` and Obsidian-style lazy/unresolved links were both rejected — see ADR-067.)

**Build order (roadmap R1–R4):**
- **R1** — `relation` in `PROPERTY_KINDS`; `PropertyDef` gains `targetType`/`cardinality` (+ `parsePropertySchema` validation); `TypeBuilder` authors it; writes/reads go to `relations` edges (role = key) via `src/lib/relations.ts`, not `items.properties`.
- **R2** — the typed relation input on the canvas (typeahead filtered to `targetType`, chips, cardinality enforced app-side; eager typed create-on-miss).
- **R3** — seed the hidden `unmarked` type (migration + `seed.mjs`); create-on-miss row in `mention-suggestion.ts` (free-text `@`) and `AddRelation.tsx` (+ Relate) → `unmarked` + `inbox`.
- **R4** — `RelatedPanel`: unmarked group under the glyph; role section headings (Attendees/References) where set.

**Watch-fors:** keep `mention` edges body-owned (ADR-015) — don't let create-on-miss in free text fight the diff-sync; the `unmarked` glyph label must not break type-order/label lookups (`compareTypeKeys`, and list tabs already filter hidden); cardinality is app-enforced (no DB constraint). Verification: extend `verify-types.mts` plus a relations-focused script for the new kind and the role-as-key reads.

## ⟢ Customizable dashboards — IN PROGRESS (started 2026-06-15, ADR-064; plan approved by Brandon)

**Building multiple named, freely resizable/draggable dashboards of widgets** (supersedes the single-dashboard ADR-031). A widget is a "chunk" of a saved View with display-only gear settings (item limit, sort, render style); kinds are view / stat-count / action-capture. A dashboard can carry a **focus** item that scopes every widget to one project at once. Dashboards become nav destinations and can be assigned as the **Home** and/or **Today** surface. Full plan: `~/.claude/plans/ok-let-s-quick-build-crispy-neumann.md`. Non-core (views/dashboards); shipped solo, will flag the schema-add to Brandon in `COLLAB.md`.

**Phasing (7 slices):** 1 data model+API · 2 react-grid-layout grid shell (+retire old `/dashboard`) · 3 widget settings + stat cards · 4 focus · 5 action/capture widgets · 6 Views/Widgets distinction · 7 nav + multi-dashboard + Home/Today.

- **Slice 1 — data model + CRUD/API ✓ DONE (2026-06-15).** New **`dashboards` table** (migration 0016, applied to Neon): one row per dashboard, widgets inline as jsonb, `position`, `focus_item_id` (FK items ON DELETE SET NULL). New **`src/lib/dashboards.ts`** (server: tolerant parsers mirroring `parseViewInput`, CRUD `list`/`get`/`create`/`update`/`delete`/`reorderDashboards`, granular `add`/`update`/`removeWidget`, `usedViewIds`) + **`src/lib/dashboard-widgets.ts`** (client-safe: the widget types/const vocabularies + pure `applyFocus`, no db — so the client grid can import shapes without pulling server code). **API** `/api/dashboards{,/[id],/reorder}`; **`?count=1`** added to `/api/items/query` for stat widgets. **`users.settings`** gained nullable `homeDashboardId`/`todayDashboardId` (tolerant → null on a removed dashboard). Exported `parseSort`/`UUID_RE`/`VIEW_LIMIT` from `views.ts` for reuse. **`verify-dashboards.mts` 19/19** (kinds round-trip, parser tolerance, settings-don't-mutate-the-view, layout clamp, focus-merge narrows the count, usedViewIds dedupe, reorder, focus-delete→SET NULL, owner scoping); `tsc` + full `next build` + eslint clean.
- **Slice 2 — react-grid-layout grid shell ✓ DONE (2026-06-15).** New **`/dashboards/[id]`** (server page: batched per-widget fan-out — view kind = focus-merged capped preview + true count, stat = count only, action = none; a deleted backing view degrades to an empty widget, never a crash) → **`DashboardClient`** (owns edit-mode + widget state, debounced ~600ms `PATCH` of the whole `DashboardInput` on drag/resize, ignores RGL's mount-fire in view mode) → **`DashboardGridLayout`** (`next/dynamic` `ssr:false`, mirrors `LazyMarkdownEditor`) → **`RglInner`** (`WidthProvider(Responsive)`, breakpoints lg/md/sm → cols 12/6/1 so mobile stacks single-column, `draggableHandle=".widget-drag-handle"` + `draggableCancel=".cancel-drag"`) → **`WidgetFrame`** (handle/title/count/remove) → **`WidgetBody`** (compact view list ported from the old card; minimal stat number + action placeholder for now). Plus a minimal **`/dashboards`** index + `NewDashboardButton`. **Dependency:** pinned **`react-grid-layout@1.5.3`** (the stable, ubiquitous `WidthProvider(Responsive)` API — npm's `@latest` is the v2 hook-rewrite, deliberately avoided) + `@types/react-grid-layout`; CSS scoped to the dynamic bundle; dark-theme resize-handle/placeholder override in `globals.css`. `tsc` + full `next build` + eslint clean. **Pending: in-browser eyeball** — create a dashboard, add widgets (once Slice 3/5 land), Edit → drag + resize → reload and confirm the layout persisted; mobile single-column.
- **Slice 3 — widget settings + stat cards ✓ DONE (2026-06-15).** `WidgetSettingsPopover` (gear, edit mode) edits display-only overrides — view: itemLimit/sort/renderStyle/title; stat: label; action: config — that never mutate the backing view. **Layout-faithful render reuses `ViewRenderer` verbatim** (the page passes full Date-typed `ViewItem[]` + the backing `ViewDefinition` + board groupOrder/labels). Stat cards render the count. `applySettings` computes the effective view in memory.
- **Slice 5 — action/capture widgets ✓ DONE (2026-06-15).** `ActionWidgetBody`: quick-capture (`POST /api/items`), new-from-template (`POST /api/templates/[id]/apply`), and link — reusing the app's existing create/apply calls + `NavGlyph` for the icon. (Built alongside slice 3 since `WidgetBody` dispatches it.)
- **Slice 4 — dashboard focus ✓ DONE (2026-06-15).** `FocusPicker` (searches items via `/api/items?q=`) sets the dashboard's `focusItemId`; `applyFocus` merges `relatedTo` into every view/stat widget so one focus re-scopes the whole dashboard (confirmed edges only). Header shows a focus pill; setting it persists + `router.refresh()`es.
- **Slice 6 — Views/Widgets distinction ✓ DONE (2026-06-15).** `usedViewIds` powers an "in use as a widget" badge + a Views/Widgets/All filter (server-rendered via a search param) on `/views`, and a badge on `/build/views`.
- **Slice 7 — nav + multi-dashboard + Home/Today + retirement ✓ DONE (2026-06-15).** `/dashboards` index; a **dashboard** nav-destination kind (icon-pickable in Build → Navigation, command-palette entry); **Set as Home / Set as Today** on each dashboard (`users.settings.homeDashboardId`/`todayDashboardId`) with `/` and the new `/today` rendering the assigned dashboard via a shared `DashboardView` loader, falling back to the fixed `TodayHome` layout. **Retired the old single-dashboard model:** dropped `views.dashboard_order` (migration 0017), removed `pinView`/`unpinView`/`setDashboardOrder`/`listDashboardViews`, deleted `/api/dashboard` + `/dashboard` page + `DashboardGrid` + `PinButton` + `verify-dashboard.mts`, rewired every caller (`structure-templates` → `addViewToDefaultDashboard`, `/build`, `/build/surface`, `/build/views`, `/views/[id]`, Today link; `/dashboard`→`/dashboards` in nav-slot-options + command-index).

**🎉 Customizable dashboards is code-complete (slices 1–7, ADR-064/065).** `verify-dashboards` 19/19, `verify-structures`/`verify-views` updated + green, regression suite (`verify-canvas-seam`/`verify-module-registry`/`verify-templates`) green; `tsc` + full `next build` + eslint all clean; migrations 0016/0017 applied to Neon. **Decision deviation from the plan:** retirement happened in slice 7 (not slice 1) so every slice stayed green — `react-grid-layout` pinned to **v1.5.3** (not npm `@latest`, which is the v2 hook-rewrite).

**Pending — in-browser eyeball (the whole feature, scripts can't cover the grid):** create a dashboard, Edit → Add widget (list + count), drag/resize → reload and confirm the layout persisted; open the gear and switch a Tasks widget to 15 items / faithful render; set a focus item and watch all widgets re-scope; Set as Home, reload `/`, confirm it renders the dashboard; add a dashboard as a nav-slot icon; mobile single-column. **Flag to Brandon (COLLAB):** the `views.dashboard_order` drop + `/dashboard`→`/dashboards` move (shared surfaces).

**Post-merge fixes (2026-06-15, eyeball round):** (1) **Build sidebar placement** — Dashboards is now a Build → INTERFACE entry (`build-nav.ts`), and **Navigation got pulled out of Work Surface into its own INTERFACE item** (`/build/navigation`, the `NavSlotsEditor`); Work Surface is now just the dashboards overview. (2) **Render-pattern fix** — `DashboardView` is rendered as JSX (`<DashboardView/>`) everywhere with an internal `notFound()`/`fallback`, not invoked as a function (`await DashboardView(...)`), the unsupported async-server-component call that was the wrong pattern. (Note: the "internal server error" during the eyeball turned out to be a corrupted dev server — `rm -rf .next` had been run under a live `next dev`; a clean restart on :3001 fixed it. POST-create + `/dashboards/[id]` render + DELETE all verified 200 against the clean server.)

**Prebuilt + Text widgets + Build chrome (2026-06-15, eyeball round 2):**
- **Build chrome on dashboards** — `isBuildPath` now treats `/dashboards*` as a Build surface, so the Build sidebar stays when you reach Dashboards from it (a dashboard *assigned* as Home/Today still renders at `/`÷`/today` = Work chrome, the "using it" context). Dashboards + Navigation are both Build → INTERFACE entries (`build-nav.ts`); Navigation was split out of Work Surface into its own `/build/navigation` page.
- **Prebuilt widgets** (`src/lib/starter-widgets.ts`) — Tasks Due Today, Overdue Tasks, Upcoming Tasks (7d), Meetings This Week, Recently Updated. Picking one creates (or reuses by name) its backing saved view and adds the widget — no view builder needed. Reuses the existing `ViewFilter` (due/withinDays/dateField), no engine change.
- **Add-widget menu organized** into **Structure** (Text/Heading), **Prebuilt** (starters), **From Views** (saved views) — each addable as List or Count.
- **Text/Heading widget** — a new `text` widget kind (non-data, like action): a heading + optional note for titling/grouping widgets in the grid. Renders chrome-free (no card), edit controls float in on Edit. The dashboard's own name is still the page `<h1>`; this is in-grid structure (start of `flexible-surfaces.md` Part 2). `verify-dashboards` +1 (text round-trip).

**Eyeball round 3 (2026-06-15):**
- **Removed Work Surface** — Dashboards + Navigation have their own Build → INTERFACE pages now, so `/build/surface` was deleted (the entry's gone from `build-nav.ts`).
- **Compact task widgets are interactive** — task rows show the circular check-off (`SubtaskCheckbox`, optimistic) and an **"associated with" chip** (the person/project/entity each item is confirmed-related to). Powered by a new batched `relatedSummaryFor(ownerId, itemIds)` in `relations.ts` (2 indexed queries, not N+1; computed only for compact view widgets) → `WidgetData.related`.
- **Inline dashboard rename** — in Edit mode the `<h1>` becomes an input; blur/Enter persists (trimmed, non-empty).
- **Edit-mode visibility** — react-grid-layout resize handles now render in the accent color (they only exist in edit mode), every cell gets a faint dashed accent outline (stronger on hover, with a handle glow) via a `dash-edit` class on the grid, and the chrome-free **Text widget gets a dashed border in edit mode** so its bounds show.
- **Text widget defaults** — new text widgets start short (one heading row, compacted to the bottom) and pre-filled with "Sample Header" so they're visible before opening the gear.

**Possible follow-ups (not built):** create-a-view inline from the Add-widget menu (reuse `ViewBuilder` with an `onSaved` prop); ~~richer action-widget pickers (type/template dropdowns instead of text keys)~~ **DONE (2026-06-20, TPL5): action widgets are now addable from the menu + the gear has a template dropdown (`/api/templates`) and a type dropdown (`/api/types`)**; a dashboard switcher/reorder UI on `/dashboards`; markdown rendering in the Text widget body; per-widget "ignore focus" toggle; an "associated with" chip in the faithful (`ViewRenderer`) render too.

## ⟢ Session summary — AI & MCP management page; "Claude" → "AI & MCP" rename; external HTTP API (2026-06-15, ADR-066)

**The `/build/claude` stub became a real management surface for the already-built MCP server (ADR-047), the sidebar label is now "AI & MCP" instead of "Claude & MCP," and a new external HTTP API (`/api/machine/items`) lets app integrations/crons push items in and read them out with a token** (ADR-066 — the trigger was wiring up Savor's journal-push cron). The MCP/page work is **non-core**; the API endpoint **touches core** (the machine/API contract) — additive only, shipped solo, **flagged to Brandon in `COLLAB.md` to ratify.**

- **External HTTP API (ADR-066)** — new `src/app/api/machine/items/route.ts`: `POST` creates one item or a `{items:[...]}` batch (max 100; a bad entry is reported + skipped, not fatal), `GET` lists items body-free. Token-authed under a new **`api` scope**, Clerk-bypassed (same door as the other `/api/machine/*` jobs). Writes reuse `parseItemPayload`/`createItem` so the surface can't drift from `/api/items` or skip owner-scoping. New `src/lib/machine/owner.ts` `resolveMachineOwner()` (optional `LEDGR_API_OWNER_UPN`, same fallbacks as MCP). `.env.example` documents the `api` scope. Type is the integrator's choice (send a `type` key). **Pending:** `verify-machine-items.mts` + curl smoke; **a `journal` type (or use `note`) to receive Savor's entries**; optional `externalId` dedup; the Savor-side cron (its own codebase).

- **Label** — `build-nav.ts` MAINTAIN entry renamed `"Claude & MCP"` → `"AI & MCP"`; route slug stays `/build/claude` (internal, invisible to users). The rename flows automatically to the destination picker's "Build tools" category (same source of truth).
- **Page** (`src/app/build/claude/page.tsx`, now a server component) surfaces the live connection rather than describing a plan: **status** (reuses the `/health` canary's `hasScopedToken("mcp")` + `resolveMcpOwner()` — green when both hold, amber otherwise, and names the acting UPN); **connection details** (endpoint URL derived from request headers so it's correct on prod/preview/localhost without an env var, server name/version from `SERVER_INFO`, supported protocol versions, Bearer-token auth); **how to connect** (the `make-token.mjs … mcp` command, the `LEDGR_API_TOKENS` env step, and a copyable `claude mcp add --transport http … --header "Authorization: Bearer <token>"` for Claude Code, plus a note for claude.ai/Desktop custom connectors); and the **live tool list** straight from `listToolDefs()` (read/write badge per tool) so it can never drift from what the server serves.
- **New** `src/components/build/CopyField.tsx` — a small `"use client"` copy-to-clipboard code field (mirrors `ShareLink`'s copy affordance, no dependency).
- `tsc` + eslint clean. **Pending: in-browser eyeball** — open Build → AI & MCP, confirm the status reflects whether an mcp token is set, copy buttons work, and the six tools list.
- **Possible follow-ups (not built):** a "test connection" button that POSTs an `initialize`/`tools/list` to `/api/mcp` from the page; surfacing scheduled Claude tasks (ADR-052) and briefing config here as the original stub plan imagined; listing configured token names/scopes (read from `LEDGR_API_TOKENS` server-side).

## ⟢ Tyler's lane — Build-mode restructure: left sidebar + System Overview + universal command palette (2026-06-15, ADR-063)

**Build mode went from a flat card grid to a real, scannable left sidebar; `/build` home became a bird's-eye System Overview; and there's now one ⌘K command palette searching everything from both modes.** From `build-mode-restructure-spec.md`. **Mostly non-core** (nav chrome + new Build pages + a search component); one additive Work-nav touch (the destination picker's "Build tools" category). Shipped solo; two small items flagged to Brandon in `COLLAB.md`.

- **Two modes, one rule** (written into CLAUDE.md): *Work is for using the system; Build is for building and maintaining it.* The separation is the **default, not a wall** — any route is a valid nav destination, and a power user can pull a Build tool into their Work nav. Opt-in, invisible by default.
- **Build mode left sidebar** (`src/components/nav/BuildSidebar.tsx`): replaces the Work bar/rail in `/build*`. Three groups under three verbs — **DATA** (Types & Properties, Templates, Workflows & Wikis, Bespoke Tools), **INTERFACE** (Views, Work Surface), **MAINTAIN** (Model Overview, Data Hygiene, Import & Migration, Claude & MCP, User Settings). Dropdowns sparingly — only **Types & Properties** expands (lists the user's types for a quick edit-jump). Desktop-first; mobile = a hamburger drawer (not a focus this phase). Glowing **"Build Mode"** wordmark; **⌘K** box; **Back to Work**. Structure lives in **`src/lib/build-nav.ts`** (single source of truth for the sidebar *and* the picker category *and* the palette's section index).
- **`/build` home → System (Model) Overview** (`src/app/build/page.tsx`): the card grid is retired (the sidebar navigates). Informational v1 — type/item/view/template counts, which views are on Work, and a "Needs attention" seed (zero-item types → the start of Data Hygiene). New `itemCountsByType` in `items.ts`.
- **Producer/consumer view split:** `/views` stays Work-side (browse/open); new **`/build/views`** is the builder/manager. Builder routes (`/views/new`, `/views/[id]/edit`) stay put; the manager links in. **(Brandon to confirm — see COLLAB.)**
- **Universal command palette** (`src/components/search/CommandPalette.tsx`, pure ranking in `src/lib/command-index.ts`): one ⌘K surface in both modes over items (FTS API) + pages + views + types + Build/Maintain sections + named settings; grouped, context-aware ranking (content-first in Work, structure-first in Build). Result model is a **`destination | action` union** (only destinations this phase — the populate-later seam). Replaces `SearchModal`. New `GET /api/command-index` feeds the dynamic (types/views/templates) entries.
- **"Build tools" category** in the Work-nav destination picker (`nav-slot-options.ts` + `NavSlotEditor`), so the cross-the-line capability is discoverable.
- **Stubs** (`/build/hygiene`, `/build/import`, `/build/claude`): real routes + a one-paragraph plan-note each (`BuildStub.tsx`), so the sidebar never dead-links.
- **Chrome/naming:** the Work More-menu door is destination-named "Build"; `/settings` H1 is now "User Settings" (the one both-places entry); `isBuildPath` = `/build*` only (`/views` no longer reads as Build). Deleted the dead `BuildModeButton.tsx` and the superseded `SearchModal.tsx`. No DB migration.

`scripts/verify-build-mode.mts` **31/31** (build-nav taxonomy + `isBuildPath`, command-index match scoring + context-aware ranking + mode-aware hrefs, the "Build tools" category); `tsc` + full `next build` + eslint all clean. **Pending:** in-browser eyeball — enter Build and watch the Work bar crossfade to the glowing sidebar, expand Types & Properties, ⌘K from both Work and Build (note the ranking shift), open a stub, and drop a Build tool into a Work nav slot. Mobile Build (the drawer) is deliberately minimal — revisit later.

**Follow-ups parked (not built):** the action-result side of the palette ("New Sermon", "New Type", "Clean up unused"); the real Data Hygiene / Notion importer / Claude-MCP management behind the stubs; "views that return nothing" + "templates never applied" flags on the Overview (need an apply-counter / per-view count); the polished Work↔Build crossfade; live sub-nav dropdowns for Claude & MCP / Import / User Settings.

## ⟢ Session summary — editor parity: inline image paste (regression) + GFM tables (2026-06-15)

**Two editor-parity items off the carried-from-M3 list (ADR-040): the inline-image-paste regression is fixed, and the editor now does GFM tables.** Non-core (editor internals + two pure helper libs); shipped solo, flagged to Tyler in `COLLAB.md`. The R2 attachment backend was fully intact, so image paste was a re-wire, not a rebuild.

- **Inline image paste/drop — REGRESSION fixed.** The M3 cutover dropped BlockNote's file blocks; the Tiptap canvas had zero image handling. Re-wired the proven presigned-upload handshake (`uploadImage` in `ItemEditor`, byte-for-byte the old `/api/attachments` → R2 PUT → public URL flow) and added Tiptap `handlePaste`/`handleDrop` handlers + a toolbar Image button (hidden file picker) that upload and insert a markdown image at the cursor. Only intercepts when an image file is actually present and an uploader is wired, so text/markdown paste still falls through; controlled hosts with no backing item (scratch route, Changelog notes) pass no `uploadImage`, so image insertion is simply absent there.
- **GFM tables.** Added the first-party `@tiptap/extension-image` + `@tiptap/extension-table` (same `@tiptap/*` vendor already in use; pre-blessed by the roadmap, low resilience risk — markdown is canonical, tables serialize to standard GFM). New `LedgrImage`/`LedgrTable` extensions bind `@tiptap/markdown`'s `renderMarkdown`/`parseMarkdown` hooks: image ↔ `![alt](src)`, table ↔ GFM grid (parse rebuilds the row/cell subtree from marked's one table token; render flattens JSON back to GFM). Toolbar "Table" insert (3×3, header row). Server render was already covered (markdown-it has GFM tables + images on; print/share doc already styles `table`/`img`).
- **Bonus: strikethrough toolbar button** (the trivial sibling on the parity list — Strike already round-tripped via StarterKit, it just had no button).
- **Discipline:** the tricky encode/decode lives in node-testable pure libs (`src/lib/editor/image-markdown.ts`, `table-markdown.ts`), the extensions only bind hooks — same split colors.ts/mention-markdown.ts use. `verify-editor-media.mts` **17/17** (pure helpers + a real `marked` round-trip proving the table grid + image link survive encode→decode); `verify-tiptap-markdown` still green; `tsc` + full `next build` + eslint all clean.

**Pending (same posture as prior editor slices):** the in-browser eyeball — paste a screenshot into a real item (uploads to R2, renders inline, autosaves as `![](url)`), insert/edit a table and confirm it round-trips to GFM and back. The pure logic, the `@tiptap/markdown` dispatch contract (read from source), the marked token shapes (checked against real marked), and the build are all proven; only the live editor mount is unverified here.

**Also recorded this session (no code):** Brandon's decisions on **inline action items** (the next mid-size feature) are now in `explorations/block-linked-action-items.md` — one line ↔ one task, promote *badges* the line and the badge links to the task, the badge/anchors never render on the shared/printed document (a "clean share" acceptance criterion), and the one real spike before building is confirming Tiptap round-trips a trailing `^id` marker.

## ⟢ Tyler's lane — Configurable nav slots + in-context delete standard (2026-06-15, ADR-056/057)

**The middle nav is now owner-configurable from Build, and every delete confirmation is an in-context popover instead of a browser `confirm()`.** Two slices in one session, both UI/chrome (not core), shipped solo; the one core-touch (type-delete taking its items) is flagged to Brandon to ratify.

- **Nav slot customization (ADR-056, from `nav-customization-spec.md`).** The hardcoded `NAV_SLOTS` is gone; the middle slots live in `users.settings` (`navSlots` max 5 + `mobileNavSlots` null=mirror / max 4), each a `destination` (builtin / saved view / type list) or a `tools` group (popover of up to 8 child destinations, no nesting). Home/New/More stay locked (prepended/appended at render). `parseSettings` is tolerant (unknown icon → `items`, malformed slot dropped, `/`-Home stripped, nested tools-child flattened, counts capped). New shared icon library `src/lib/nav-icons.ts` (~45 hand-rolled glyphs + `navIconPaths`/`isNavIcon`) replaces NavShell's `switch(slot)`; `NavGlyph` renders one icon. The `/build/surface` "Navigation" section is now a live editor (`NavSlotsEditor`/`NavSlotEditor`/`NavIconPicker`): live preview bar, native HTML5 drag-reorder (no DnD dep), destination/tools forms with a searchable icon picker, Desktop/Mobile split, persists the whole array to `PATCH /api/settings` per change. The four v6 layouts are untouched apart from the icon lookup + a new tools-popover path; a `/search` destination still opens the palette. **`src/lib/nav.ts` deleted.**
- **In-context delete is the new standard (ADR-057).** New `src/components/ui/ConfirmButton.tsx` — a trigger that opens a confirm popover anchored to itself (title/description, optional extra body, Confirm/Cancel; closes on outside-click/Esc; `onConfirm` may throw to show an API error inline). Replaced all five `window.confirm()` sites (TypeBuilder, ViewBuilder, TemplateBuilder, CollabNotes, + the new nav-slot remove). **No `window.confirm` remains in the app.**
- **Delete a type *and* its items — to Trash, recoverable (ADR-057 confirm + ADR-058 soft-delete, core-touch).** When a type is in use, the delete popover offers "Also move the N items to Trash" → `DELETE /api/types/[key]?withItems=1` → `softDeleteTypeWithItems` soft-deletes the type and its live items (+ descendants, recursive on `parent_id`) with one shared `deleted_at`, so they restore as a unit. **Types now have a `deleted_at` column (migration 0014)** and join the soft-delete model: hidden from `listTypes`/pickers, still resolvable via `getType` for labels, blocked from new-item creation; the **Trash page got a "Types" section** (`restoreType` + `POST /api/types/[key]/restore` + `TypeRestoreButton`), restoring an item auto-revives its type, and `purgeExpiredTrash` hard-purges expired soft-deleted types after their items. This **supersedes the original hard-delete** (it broke the "soft-delete only" convention — Tyler's catch).

- **Hide a built-in (or any) type you don't use (ADR-059, core-touch).** New `types.hidden` column (migration 0015): a hidden type still exists/works but drops out of quick capture, +New menus, list tabs, and nav options (`listTypes()` excludes hidden by default; `{includeHidden:true}` on the Build → Types page only). The `/build/types` page now reads as a small table: a **Quick Capture column** (circular checkbox per row → `setTypeQuickCapture` / `POST /api/types/[key]/quick-capture`) beside a **Show (visibility eye)** column, dimmed hidden rows, a subtle dashed "Create new type" footer button, and a card linking to the bespoke-tool catalog ("Check our bespoke data types" — names live capabilities Chord Chart / Paper Workspace) so a user building a plain type knows richer behavior exists to borrow.

`verify-nav-and-deletes.mts` **45/45** (pure nav-icons + `parseSettings` slot validation + dest options; live-Neon: hidden show/hide via listTypes, in-use block, soft-delete to Trash, hide-from-registry, relation survives, `listDeletedTypes`, `restoreType`, restore-revives-type, create-under-deleted-type blocked). `tsc` + full `next build` + eslint all clean (migrations 0014 + 0015 applied to Neon; also fixed a pre-existing ref-in-render lint in TypeBuilder). **Pending:** in-browser eyeball — build a tools group + reorder slots + a custom-mobile list and watch the real nav update; run a type-delete-with-items then restore it from Trash; hide Link and confirm it leaves capture/tabs/nav.

**Brandon-ratify (core-touch):** ADR-058 (soft-delete types: `deleted_at`, restore, purge) + ADR-059 (`hidden` flag). Built at Tyler's direction under the usual solo-core waiver, but they touch `items`/`types` semantics — see ADR-057/058/059 + the COLLAB heads-up. **Also for-discussion (Brandon):** Tyler proposes dropping **Link** as a built-in (built-ins → Meeting/Note/Person/Task; Link becomes a bespoke capability) — parked in COLLAB.

**Idea parked (for-discussion / next):** a **"Create a type for each bespoke model" button** (one click seeds a sample type per available capability — Chord Chart, Paper Workspace, …) so a user can test-drive the custom modules and delete them after. Low effort on top of the catalog + `createType`; good onboarding. Decide placement (Build home or the catalog page) with Brandon.

**Top-bar spacing modes (follow-up, same session).** The top bar now mirrors the rails: **Spread / Compact (left) / Compact (center) / Compact (right)**, anchoring the grouped cluster via the stored `railAnchor` (top/center/bottom read as left/center/right on the horizontal axis). This **supersedes** the old top-bar compact = centered ~40rem band described in the nav-v6 follow-up below; the bar still spans the full top edge, only the content cluster moves.

## ⟢ Session summary — nav v6 (docked rail / top bar) + circular checkboxes (2026-06-15)

**The nav chrome got a real desktop redesign and every checkbox in the app became a dark-mode circle.** UI/UX polish (not core), shipped solo. Two builders share the file, so the change is presentational only: the slot list, settings shape contract, and API are untouched apart from one additive setting.

- **Circular checkboxes everywhere.** The editor already had the canonical dark circle (gray ring on dark fill, accent fill + white check when ticked); promoted that look to a global `.ledgr-check` / `.ledgr-check-sm` utility in `globals.css` and applied it to every native `<input type="checkbox">`: task done-toggles (`SubtaskCheckbox`, `RelatedRow`) and all Build/config forms (`CustomProperties`, `TypeBuilder`, `TemplateBuilder`, `StructureBuilder`, `ViewBuilder`, `DashboardGrid` including multi-select option lists, Brandon's call). No more white boxes in dark mode. `SubtaskCheckbox` now shows the white check too.
- **Nav v6 (`NavShell.tsx` rewrite).** Four layouts off the existing `navPosition`: **bottom** keeps the floating pill (also the mobile default on every position); **top** is now a full-width docked menu bar (icons + labels, inline badges); **left/right** are full-height docked side rails (no longer a floating pill). The rail has three sizes the collapse arrow cycles, **fat** (icons + names) → **thin** (icons only, tooltips) → **hidden** (a reopen tab at the screen edge), with the More kebab switching to horizontal dots and items aligned to the top.
- **New setting `railSize` (`fat`|`thin`|`hidden`, default `fat`).** Added to `lib/settings.ts` (type + default + `parseSettings` validation); persisted through the existing `PATCH /api/settings`. The collapse arrow flips local state instantly and persists in the background (no refresh). Body padding moved off hardcoded Tailwind classes onto CSS vars (`--nav-pt/pb/pl/pr`) computed by the shared `lib/nav-layout.ts` (`navPadVars` + `RAIL_PX`), set server-side in `layout.tsx` for first paint and nudged client-side by `NavShell` on collapse so the rail width and content padding stay locked together (smoothed by a `body` padding transition in `globals.css`).
- **Glowing Build entry.** The More menu's Build link is now a highlighted button: accent border, accent text, a wrench icon, and the same `shadow-[0_0_…_var(--accent)]` glow as the Bespoke-tools card on `/build`.

`tsc` clean; eslint clean on the changed files; `/` returns 200 with no compile errors against the changed files. **Pending:** in-browser eyeball of each of the four positions + the fat/thin/hidden cycle (the local dev environment had several stray servers; worth a clean look). Possible follow-ups for `next_steps`: animate the rail width itself (not just body padding), and remember whether the rail was last hidden per-position.

  **Follow-up round (same day, Brandon's tweaks):**
  - **Spacing setting** added (`navDensity: spread|compact`, default spread; `railAnchor: top|center|bottom`, default top, in `lib/settings.ts`). New "Spacing" control in the More menu below "Move menu": rails get **Spread / Compact (top) / Compact (center) / Compact (bottom)**, the top bar gets **Compact / Spread**, the bottom bar gets nothing (it is always compact). Spread pins slots and the New/More utilities to opposite ends; compact groups them, anchored to the chosen rail position (top/center/bottom via `flex-1` spacers around the cluster). On the **top bar**, the bar itself always spans the full top edge (background + border line all the way across, like the rails span the full height); compact **centers the content** within a ~40rem band (`mx-auto max-w-[40rem]`), spread lets it use the full width. Logo + items left, New/More far-right in both. Flips local state instantly, persists in the background (same pattern as the rail-size cycle).
  - **Logo:** a **"Ledgr" wordmark** set in **Bricolage Grotesque** (`next/font/google`, exposed as `--font-logo` on `<html>`, only the logo opts in; final "r" in the accent color). Shown on the **top bar** (left) and on the **fat left/right rail** (full wordmark, paired with the collapse arrow). The **thin rail shows just the collapse arrow** (no logo, arrow enlarged to 22px). Font is easy to swap, the variable is the only knob.
  - **Rail "More" menu** now opens away from the kebab so it can't run off-screen (cluster at top → opens down; bottom → up; center → centered on the kebab), and is height-capped with scroll (`max-h-[calc(100vh-1rem)] overflow-y-auto`). The rolled-up reopen tab's arrow is also enlarged (22px).
  - **Top bar:** drop shadow removed (border only now).
  - **Bottom bar:** the desktop floating pill now fills ~40rem (`max-w-[calc(100vw-2rem)]`) with items spread (`justify-between`) so it carries the same presence as the other layouts; its icons are scaled up (`[&_svg]:h-6/w-6`) since the smaller ones felt thin. Mobile keeps the compact pill.
  - **Rail collapse affordances:** the in-rail collapse arrow uses the accent color; the fully-rolled-up reopen tab gets the glowing accent treatment (accent border + `0_0_16px var(--accent)` glow, 24px wide, body padding kept in sync via `RAIL_PX.hidden`) so the hidden menu is easy to find.
  - **More menu:** "Settings" → "User Settings"; the "Back to Work" entry now uses a distinct return-arrow icon instead of reusing Build's wrench.

## ⟢ Tyler's lane — Changelog + shared collab notes (2026-06-14, ADR-054)

**A Changelog page in the kebab "More" menu so Brandon and Tyler can see what each other shipped, plus a shared notes scratchpad beside it.** The page is 3/4 commit list + 1/4 markdown notes. Not core (a new page + a per-instance integration); shipped solo, flagged to Brandon in `COLLAB.md`.

- **The cross-deploy problem + the call:** separate single-tenant deploys (separate Vercel + Neon) mean a DB row is never shared. **GitHub is the shared medium** (git already is for `COLLAB.md`/`decisions.md`): the Changelog **reads commit history live** via the REST API; the notes live in a **committed file** (`COLLAB_NOTES.md`) read/written via the Contents API. One PAT, plain `fetch`, no Octokit (Principle 5).
- **`src/lib/github/client.ts`** — config (null = not configured), `getChangelog` (cached list + per-commit immutable detail → file/line counts), `readNotes`/`writeNotes` (sha-guarded so a stale Save 409s instead of clobbering; auto-creates a non-deploy notes branch if configured), `checkGithub` canary. Pure `toChangelogEntry`/`decodeContent` seams.
- **`/changelog`** (server component, 3/4 + 1/4 grid) with **`CollabNotes`** client panel (reuses `LazyMarkdownEditor` controlled; Save/Clear; 409→reload prompt). **`GET`/`PUT /api/collab/notes`**, owner-guarded. Kebab gained a Changelog link; `checks.github` added to `gatherHealth`.
- **`GITHUB_NOTES_BRANCH`** opts notes onto a non-deployed branch so a note Save doesn't trigger a Vercel rebuild (default: deploy branch, fine during alpha).

`verify-changelog.mts` **21/21**; `verify-health-check` still green (synthetic updated for the new canary); `tsc` + full `next build` + eslint clean. **Brandon-step (to use it live):** set `GITHUB_TOKEN` per runbook **§1g**; until then both surfaces show a clean "not connected" state. **In-browser live check is pending** the token (same posture as the other integration-blocked slices) — the not-configured render, the build, and the pure logic are proven.

## ⟢ Session summary — the MCP server (2026-06-13, ADR-047, slice 36)

**Phase 3, Tier 3 opens: Claude is now a first-class client.** A thin MCP server exposes Ledgr's items to Claude over a personal API token (PRD §5.5) — "what's open with Roger," "file this as a task due Friday," "prep tomorrow's 1:1." **CORE** (the machine/MCP API contract); shipped solo per Brandon's Tier direction, flagged to Tyler in `COLLAB.md`.

- **Hand-rolled MCP over Streamable HTTP, no SDK** (Principle 5; the ADR-034 web-push precedent). In-app endpoint at **`POST /api/mcp`**, stateless, verified against the 2025-06-18 spec: request → one `application/json` response, notification → 202, GET/DELETE → 405, version negotiation. Clerk-bypassed (`proxy.ts` public set); gated by the **existing machine-token scheme with a new `mcp` scope** (ADR-004 unchanged). Owner resolved by UPN like the other machine jobs (`resolveMcpOwner`).
- **Six tools, each a thin wrapper over the libs the REST API already uses** (search/views/items/relations/types) — `search_items`, `list_items` (by type/status/due-window/entity), `get_item` (fields + markdown body + relations), `create_item` (markdown body, `relateTo`, `inbox:false`), `update_item`, `list_types`. Create/update **reuse `parseItemPayload`**, so MCP writes validate like `/api/items` writes; can't skip owner-scoping.
- **Pure/impure split (M5/M6 discipline):** `src/lib/mcp/{protocol,server,tools,owner}.ts`, a thin route shell. **`parseItemPayload`/`asUuid` extracted to a node-safe `src/lib/item-input.ts`** (api.ts re-exports → 17 call sites untouched), since api.ts's `next/server`/Clerk chain can't load in a verify script. `/health` `checks.mcp` canary.
- `verify-mcp.mts` **50/50** against Neon (pure protocol + dispatcher + all six tools end-to-end + validation + owner scoping); `tsc` + full `next build` clean. **In-browser HTTP smoke of the real route (dev stand-in):** GET→405, no-token→401, `initialize`→200 (serverName "ledgr", negotiated 2025-06-18), notification→202, `tools/list`→6 tools, `create_item`→`get_item` round-tripped the body, bad version→400, unknown method→-32601; smoke item + temp dev token cleaned up.

**Brandon-step (§1f, to use it live):** `node scripts/make-token.mjs claude-mcp mcp` → append the entry to `LEDGR_API_TOKENS` in Vercel + `.env.local`, redeploy → add a custom MCP connector with URL `https://ledgr-teal.vercel.app/api/mcp` + `Authorization: Bearer <raw token>`. Owner defaults to `ONEDRIVE_EXPORT_UPN` (already set). Verify `/health` `checks.mcp.configured:true`.

**Next: the rest of Tier 3 — scheduled Claude tasks** (morning briefing / weekly health check over the same API/token), then the **selective Notion migration**. Then Tier 4 (planning rhythms + parked items). The editor-parity track (inline image paste regression first) still slots against priority; the §1c-blocked matcher UIs whenever Azure lands.

**Phase 3, Tier 2 is complete: a guided "New Workflow" / "New Wiki" generates a type + properties + starter views and surfaces it on Work.** Second half of Tier 2 (item templates were Tier 2a, ADR-045). The view-definition shape widened (a board can group by a custom property) — views are non-core, so no new core surface beyond ADR-045's table; noted to Tyler.

- **A structure template is a *generator*, not a stored thing** — its output is ordinary `types` + `views`, so "retire it" is just unpinning and the data always stays. No new table.
- **`src/lib/structure-templates.ts`** — pure `planWorkflow`/`planWiki` (+ presets: Hiring/Content pipelines, Trip/Reading wikis) reusing `parseTypeInput`/`parseViewInput`; impure `applyStructurePlan` over `createType`/`createView`/`pinView`. A workflow prepends a `stage` select and makes a board-grouped-by-stage + a table; a wiki makes a title-sorted table.
- **`POST /api/build/structures`**; **`/build/new`** chooser + guided `StructureBuilder` (stages reorder = board column order, fields editor, add-to-dashboard); **`/build/surface`** pin/unpin page (Work-surface wiring, reuses `PinButton`). Both `/build` "soon" cards are now real.
- **View-engine extension (the enabler):** board grouping by a custom **select** property. Pure helpers extracted to **`src/lib/view-grouping.ts`** (`groupValueFor`/`orderedGroups`/`dueBucket`, node-testable); `ViewGrouping` widened to `{propertyKey}`; column order resolves live from the type's option list; `ViewBuilder` offers + preserves property grouping.
- **"Wire into Work" = the dashboard-widget pin** (built, offered inline). **Nav-slot config is deferred** to its own slice (the nav is still the `NAV_SLOTS` stand-in).

`verify-structures.mts` **38/38**, `verify-views` extended + green; `tsc` + full `next build` clean. **In-browser (dev stand-in):** the chooser rendered both sections + presets; generating a workflow created the type + board + table, pinned the board, and the board showed stage columns **in option order** with items in their stages — zero console errors; verification data cleaned up.

**Next: Phase 3, Tier 3 — the Claude layer** (the MCP server: search/read/create/update items over a personal API token), then scheduled Claude tasks and the selective Notion migration. The §1c-blocked matcher UIs still slot in whenever the Azure step lands.

## ⟢ Tyler's lane — Songs module: the chord chart builder ✅ DONE (2026-06-13, PR #3 `tyler/chord-chart-builder`)

The first real workflow module, built end-to-end on the M5/M6 foundation (ADR-042). A `song` type with a **ChordPro canonical body**, a bespoke **chord-chart canvas**, a **click-to-place WYSIWYG editor**, and **live transpose/capo** — designed against two real PraiseCharts exports ("Light on the Hill", "Sharpen My Sword"). All in `src/lib/chordpro/` + `src/components/chord-editor/` + `src/components/canvas/Chord*`.

- **S1 — pure ChordPro core** (`src/lib/chordpro/`): parser/serializer, chart→HTML renderer (chords over syllables, words never split, leading + trailing chords, two columns, column/page breaks, arrangement line), lyrics-only FTS text, transpose/capo math. Import-pure → one renderer for editor preview, print/share, and the verify script.
- **S2 — module + render surfaces**: `src/lib/modules/songs.ts` + the registration boot site (`src/lib/modules/register.ts` — the first live `registerModule` caller, imported by `module-wiring.tsx`); `print-html.ts`/`body-text.ts` branch on `body.format === "chordpro"`; song types-row seed.
- **S3 — WYSIWYG editor + Edit ⇄ Preview toggle**: click a character → chord picker (no bracket typing); leading/trailing slots; per-line ✎ raw fallback; section add/label/reorder/delete/repeat; autosave (`useItemAutosave`).
- **S4 — transpose/capo** + **lock-shapes** (🔒 keep shapes, capo drives the key).
- **S5 — mid-syllable placement** (chord above the exact clicked character).
- **S6 — mid-song key change**: TRANSPOSE/REDEFINE KEY markers (parse PCO tokens + directives; renderer threads a running transpose; PCO export emits them).
- **Copy for Planning Center**: button copies PCO Lyrics & Chords format exactly (ALL-CAPS headings, inline `[chord]`, bare `COLUMN_BREAK`/`PAGE_BREAK`, `{ note }`, `{{ … }}` metadata note, plain `| G | G |` bars). Registered as the song module's `ExporterDef`.

**Verification:** `verify-chordpro.mts` 91/91, `verify-songs.mts` 13/13; `verify-module-registry` 38 + `verify-canvas-seam` 16 still green; `tsc` clean. Two of Tyler's songs seeded locally and confirmed canvas/print/FTS/copy end to end.

**Shared-file touches (flagged for Brandon in the PR):** (1) module-registration boot site — first `registerModule` caller (possible ADR — where modules register); (2) `print-html.ts` + `body-text.ts` branch on `body.format` (markdown default byte-for-byte unchanged); (3) `Modal.tsx` gained a `wide` prop (songs preview two-column; note/task modals unchanged). No behavior change for the five core types.

**Future (logged, not built):**
- **Bespoke-module catalog — attach a module's behavior to any type the user names** *(**DONE 2026-06-14, ADR-051** — Tyler's `spike/bespoke-tool-catalog`, reviewed + approved by Brandon and merged; `types.capability` column via migration 0011, capability-aware registry resolvers, `/build/tools` catalog).* A "Bespoke modules" browser in the Build surface (`/build`) that lists the bespoke capabilities we've built (Chord Chart today; Papers/etc. later) with a short description/preview. A user who wants it picks it and **attaches it to a type they create under their own name** (key/label/icon) — so the chord-chart behavior isn't locked to the `song` key: someone could make a **"Music"** or **"Worship Set"** type and still get the chord canvas + ChordPro + transpose + PCO export on top. **The architectural change:** decouple a module's *behavior* (canvas/format/exporters) from a fixed type key. Today a module owns its key (`songModule.types[0].key === "song"`); instead a user-created type would declare a `capability`/module id that the registry resolves for canvas/format/exporters — generalizing M6 (ADR-043) + the type builder (ADR-044) + the per-user `isModuleEnabled` seam. **Why:** the tool shouldn't impose naming. Most users never see these (opt-in, invisible by default), but a user who wants the power keeps ownership of their own data model — they shape the type, our bespoke behavior rides underneath.
- **S7 — inline performance instructions** ("Band builds in") that render *above* the lyric line like a chord but styled as a direction; export as a bracketed `[…]` token (PCO convention). Currently imported as a `{note}` comment line.
- **Real Planning Center API push** (one-click) — investigated (PCO Services API + personal access token); deferred in favor of copy/paste. The `IntegrationDef` slot + provider-seam pattern are mapped.
- **Chord-diagram grids** and **style tags** (`<b>`/`<i>`) / **∆ ♭ ° ø** glyphs in export.
- **Song import: PDF / chord chart → ChordPro** — bulk-add the existing chord-chart library to the archive. Scoped 2026-06-14, **build is the next Songs-lane slice**: deterministic text-layer parse (no AI), reviewed in the chord canvas before save. Full build spec in `explorations/song-import.md` (pipeline, reuse map, the one new dep `unpdf`, risks). Decided over AI-assisted (Principle 3/5; ~$0 target); AI fallback for scanned PDFs deferred.

## ⟢ Tyler's lane — Papers module: the seminary paper workspace ✅ DONE (2026-06-14, ADR-048)

The second workflow module on the M5/M6 foundation. A `paper` type with a **markdown-canonical body**, a bespoke **tabbed canvas (Quote Bank · Outline · Draft)**, a **deterministic MSM citation engine**, click-to-cite footnotes, and a one-click **MSM `.docx` export** (the proven `ty-docs/msm-render.js` ported in). Replaces the hand-run HTML-viewer + Word-doc flow (`ty-docs/Paper_Outline_Viewer_Build_Spec.md`).

- **P1 — pure core** (`src/lib/papers/`): `types.ts` (`Source`/`QuoteEntry`/`PaperMeta`/`PaperScaffold`, `PAPER_STAGES`) + `citation.ts` (MSM 4th-ed Full/Short/Ibid for book + video sources). Import-pure → one engine for the quote-bank UI, the docx renderer, and the verify script.
- **P2 — MSM docx** (`src/lib/papers/msm-docx.ts` + `app/api/items/[id]/render-docx/route.ts`): faithful port of the CLI renderer, meta from item title + properties (not YAML); positional footnotes (no unpack/pack fix). Binary → a dedicated route, **not** the `ExporterDef` slot (which returns a string), so the core module contract is untouched. New runtime dep: `docx`.
- **P3 — tabbed canvas** (`PaperCanvas` → `PaperCanvasClient` + `PaperMarkdownArea`/`QuoteBank`/`PaperMeta`): Draft = canonical body; Outline + Quote Bank = scaffold in `properties`. **`PaperCanvasClient` is the single properties writer** (no `CustomProperties` race). Cite from the quote bank mints a unique `[^id]`, splices it at the Draft caret, and appends the definition. Draft/Outline use a raw-markdown textarea (footnotes are a raw-markdown feature Tiptap doesn't support); CodeMirror later.
- **Module + seed**: `src/lib/modules/papers.ts` (+`register.ts`, `module-wiring.tsx`), `scripts/seed-papers.mjs` (type row + title-page/stage `property_schema`, idempotent; seeded to dev DB).

**Verification:** `verify-papers.mts` **16/16** (registration, citation book+video forms, a real 10KB docx render with 3 footnotes); `verify-module-registry` 38 + `verify-canvas-seam` 16 + `verify-songs` still green; `tsc` + `next build` + eslint clean. **Left for Tyler's morning check:** the in-browser canvas walkthrough (create a paper → add a quote → Cite drops a footnote → edit Outline persists → fill title page → Export MSM .docx opens correctly; compare title-page spacing to the 2 Timothy 100/100 benchmark, the renderer's `TP` constants).

**Shared-file touches (flag for Brandon):** `register.ts` (+`paperModule`) and `module-wiring.tsx` (+`paper` canvas) — both additive; the five core types unchanged. Binary docx via a route, so no change to the `ExporterDef` core contract.

**Future (logged, not built):**
- **Promote the scaffold to a child item** if `properties` bloats (outline → child `note` for its own revisions/FTS) — the logged caveat in ADR-048.
- **Outline-as-shape generator** — generate the Draft heading skeleton + gray cue paragraphs from the outline structure (the build spec's "Paper Shape → built markdown"), if the side-by-side scaffold proves too manual.
- **CodeMirror** for the Draft/Outline (syntax highlight, better caret UX) over the v1 textarea.

## ⟢ Session summary — the Build surface + custom type & property builder (2026-06-13, ADR-044, slice 33)

**Phase 3, Tier 1 shipped end-to-end: Ledgr now has a Build surface and a working custom type & property builder.** First Phase-3 slice; touches core (a `types` column + the type model), shipped solo per Brandon ("build the full shell and full type/property builder and then merge; no Tyler sign-off needed") — flagged to Tyler in `COLLAB.md`.

- **The Work/Build switch is a standalone floating button** (`BuildModeButton`, top-right, separate from the nav — Brandon's design). On/off state is **path-derived** (`/build*`), so it can't desync: click from Work → `/build`, from Build → `/`. Shows "Building" (amber) vs "Build".
- **`/build` home** surfaces the building blocks: Types & properties, Views (existing), and "soon" placeholders for Templates/Workflows + Work-surface wiring.
- **`src/lib/types.ts` registry store** + `/api/types{,/[key]}`: CRUD + hand-rolled validation, `property_schema` as an ordered `PropertyDef[]` (kinds text/number/date/checkbox/url/select/multi_select; `relation` deferred — relations already cover links). Types are **instance-global** (no `owner_id`); key is immutable; system types are extendable but not deletable; in-use delete is guarded (FK + counted message).
- **`show_in_quick_capture` column** (migration **0008**, default true): quick capture is now **data-driven + opt-in**.
- **`TypeBuilder` over `/build/types{,/new,/[key]/edit}`** (label, auto-slugged key, icon, quick-capture toggle, property editor with select options + reorder).
- **Custom properties render + edit on the canvas** (`CustomProperties`; `MarkdownCanvas` is now async, loads the schema via `getType`). It merges over the full `properties` object so a PATCH never wipes the namespaced system keys (email/todoist/calendar/match/notify). A user type needs **no `modules.ts` change** — it falls back to the default markdown canvas (ADR-043 split holds).
- **The parked type/kind UX landed** (`explorations/type-and-kind-ux.md` §1–3): entity **Kind** is a dropdown of existing kinds + "New kind…" (in FieldStrip + capture); the capture relate-picker is renamed **"Relate to…"** with an inline entity-kind picker so entities don't land kind-less. **Q6 working resolution:** types are first-class/creatable, entity kinds stay a reused vocabulary (not auto-graduated).

`tsc` + full `next build` clean (all new routes); **`verify-types.mts` 37/37**; regression smoke green (`verify-canvas-seam` 16/16, `verify-module-registry` 38/38, `verify-items`). **In-browser (dev stand-in):** built "Hiring Candidate" with a `Stage` select through the real form, captured an item of it, its `Stage` select rendered + persisted (`stage="Interview"`); quick capture showed the custom type; the entity kind picker + "Relate to…" rename both confirmed — zero console errors. Verification data was cleaned up (registry back to the five system types).

**Next: Phase 3, Tier 2 — templates** (workflow/wiki guided creation, then per-type item templates), which build on this type/property machinery. The §1c-blocked matcher UIs still slot in whenever Brandon completes the Azure step.

## ⟢ Session summary — M6, the module-registration boundary; Phase M complete (2026-06-13, ADR-043)

**A module can now contribute `{type, canvas, exporters, integration}` onto core; core itself is the first module, so the whole app resolves type behavior through one boundary.** This is the last Phase-M slice — the foundation rework is done. (Core change; Brandon directed shipping it solo without waiting on Tyler's sign-off, designed against Tyler's PR #1 specs and flagged to him in `COLLAB.md`.)

- **`src/lib/modules.ts` — the pure policy/contract half.** The contract (`ModuleManifest` = `{id, label, enabledByDefault, types, exporters, integration?}`; `ModuleTypeDef` = `{key, label, canonicalFormat, canvasId, icon?}`; `ExporterDef` with a deterministic `render`; `IntegrationDef`), the registry (`registerModule`/`allModules`/`isModuleEnabled`), and the resolvers core dispatches through (`canvasIdForType`, `canonicalFormatForType`, `exportersForType`, `moduleForType`, `typeDefFor`, `registeredTypeKeys`). **Import-pure** (only `react` types + `@/lib/body` + `import type` getItem), so it resolves the same on the server and in a node verify script — the discipline M5 set, generalized to all four slots, halves linked by string ids. The canvas-contract types moved here; **`canvas-registry.ts` was folded in and deleted.**
- **Core is the first module** (`coreModule` — the 5 system types, all markdown, only `link` bespoke). The default markdown experience is byte-for-byte unchanged; the dogfood is the proof the boundary is real.
- **`src/lib/module-wiring.tsx` — the impure half.** `canvasId → React component` (the one thing that can't be pure) with a `?? MarkdownCanvas` fallback. `ItemCanvas` now resolves `canvasComponentFor(canvasIdForType(type, owner.id))` — owner-aware so the per-user enable flip needs no call-site change.
- **Two slots become real capabilities without behavior change:** canonical-body-format-per-type (`canonicalFormatForType`, answers Tyler decision #1 — Songs can declare `chordpro`) and the per-user enable seam (`isModuleEnabled`, default-on, owner-threaded — the "later config flip").
- **Proof, not a module (ADR-042):** `referenceModule` is exported as executable documentation touching all four slots but is **not** registered live (no DB type row, zero app footprint); the verify script registers it (+ a ChordPro fixture + a disabled fixture) to prove resolution + the enable seam, and core's live `link`→`LinkCanvas` proves the canvas-wiring path end to end.

`tsc --noEmit` + full `next build` clean. **`verify-module-registry.mts` 38/38** (core dogfooding, a module slotting in with all four slots, format-per-type, the enable/disable seam, duplicate-id rejection); **`verify-canvas-seam.mts` still 16/16** (repointed; M5 + ADR-018 invariants intact). **In-browser (dev stand-in):** the `link` item shows the URL chip over the full default canvas (toolbar, URL strip, Related, Save Offline, Share, Fields); a `note` renders the unchanged default markdown canvas (no chip) — both routed through the new boundary, zero console/server errors. No schema/DB change, no new dependency.

**Phase M is complete** (M1–M3 markdown cutover ADR-040, M5 canvas seam ADR-041, M6 module boundary ADR-043). **Next: Phase 3, Tier 1 — the Build surface UX** (Work/Build toggle, then the custom type & property builder + the type/kind authoring UX from `explorations/type-and-kind-ux.md`). The deferred matcher setup-wizard + learn-by-confirmation UIs still slot in whenever §1c lands.

## ⟢ Session summary — M5, the per-type canvas seam (2026-06-13, ADR-041)

**A type can now declare its own canvas; everything else keeps the markdown canvas, unchanged.** This is the platform hook Tyler's Papers/Songs modules need (PR #1 decision #2), implementing the per-type-canvas direction ADR-037 already agreed.

- **Shell / canvas split.** `src/components/canvas/ItemCanvas.tsx` is now a thin **shell** — owner check, item load, trash/`notFound` guards, ancestor breadcrumb — that resolves the type's canvas and renders `<Canvas item ownerId variant />`. The whole type-specific surface moved into **`MarkdownCanvas.tsx`** (the default: markdown editor + per-type panels + RelatedPanel + Save Offline + Share + the collapsed Fields), a near-verbatim extraction so the default experience is identical.
- **Policy split from wiring (testability).** `src/lib/canvas-registry.ts` is the **pure policy** — `canvasIdForType(type)` → a canvas id, default `"markdown"`, *no component imports* (`import type` only), so it resolves the same on the server and in a plain-node verify script. The **wiring** (id → component) is a small `Record` in the shell with a `?? MarkdownCanvas` fallback. This mirrors how a module will declare `{type → canvas}` (policy) and provide the component (wiring) — M6-shaped, not throwaway.
- **The proof: `link` → `LinkCanvas`.** Foregrounds the URL as a click-through chip, then composes `<MarkdownCanvas/>` underneath — the exact compose-pattern a real module canvas follows. Drop the registry entry and links fall back to the default; nothing else depends on it.
- **`footerFieldsFor` extracted pure** into `canvas-fields.ts` (beside `topStripFields`), so the Fields-section logic is one testable place and the ADR-018 "due/urgency stay task-only, even on legacy rows" invariant is asserted by a test.

`tsc --noEmit` + full `next build` clean. **16/16** in `scripts/verify-canvas-seam.mts` (resolution + the footer invariant). **In-browser (dev stand-in):** the `link` item shows the URL chip above the title with the full default canvas beneath (toolbar, URL strip, Related, Save Offline, Share, Fields); a `note` renders through the unchanged default canvas (no chip, markdown intact) — zero console errors. No schema/DB change, no new dependency. (Housekeeping: cleared two orphan `next dev` servers that were 500ing and blocking a clean build; added `autoPort: true` to the dev `launch.json` so the preview server coexists with a manual dev server.)

**Next: M6 — the module-registration boundary**, built alongside the first real module (Papers): turn the hardcoded wiring `Record` into a module contributing its `{type, canvas, exporters, integration}` onto core, per-user enable as a later config flip.

## ⟢ Session summary — M3, the Markdown cutover (2026-06-13, ADR-040)

**BlockNote is gone; Markdown is the only body path.** Shipped and verified:
- **`{format,text}` body** via new `src/lib/body.ts` (`makeMarkdownBody`/`isItemBody`/tolerant `bodyMarkdown`); `items.body`/`revisions.body` stay jsonb (no column migration). `parseItemPayload` now accepts the object and rejects the old block array.
- **Server render via markdown-it** (new `src/lib/markdown-render.ts`): `markdownToHtml` (print/share) + `markdownToText` (FTS), one token-rewrite rule shifts body headings under the doc `<h1>` and renders `ledgr://item/<id>` links as flat `.mention` spans; `html:true` passes the color/highlight inline HTML through. markdown-it is the one vetted markdown dep (Principle 5, server-only).
- **Readers/writers switched:** `print-html.ts` (shell+CSS kept, body via markdown-it), `export/engine.ts` (`bodyMarkdown` — body *is* the markdown), `body-text.ts` (markdown strip), `mentions.ts` (`collectMentionIdsFromMarkdown`), email-in (`emailToMarkdown`). `ItemEditor` moved to `src/components/markdown-editor/` and wired to the Tiptap `LazyMarkdownEditor`.
- **Data: wiped, not migrated** (Brandon's call — alpha, start fresh). 30 dev items deleted (cascades cleaned the rest). The BlockNote→markdown serializer (`src/lib/markdown.ts`) + the migration script were **deleted** (no data to migrate, so no tool to keep).
- **BlockNote retired:** `@blocknote/*` uninstalled, `src/components/editor/` deleted, stale globals.css rules dropped.
- **Side fix:** a pre-existing export-idempotency flake (export's `updatedAt` $onUpdate landing just after `exportedAt` → spurious re-export) — pinned both stamps to one instant; `verify-export` 5/5.

`tsc` + full `next build` clean; **24/24 verify scripts** (rewrote `verify-print` for markdown, added `verify-body-contract`, migrated the BlockNote-body fixtures in 7 scripts to markdown). **In-browser (dev stand-in):** share page renders the heading-shift/colors/highlight/mention/code/table with no console errors; the real canvas mounts the Tiptap editor over a `{format,text}` body and a live edit autosaved back as markdown with `body_text` reindexed.

**Next: M5 — the per-type canvas seam** (`type → canvas` registry, default = markdown canvas), the platform hook the Papers/Songs modules need. Then M6 (module registration boundary), built alongside the first real module.

## Known mobile bugs & UX gaps

Captured 2026-06-14 from real mobile use. Fix against priority, not all at once.

**Bugs:**
- **Saving a link from Android creates 2 copies.** The share-target → link creation produces a duplicate item.

**UX fixes (hover-dependent UI doesn't work on mobile):**
- **Delete button is hover-only.** The Trash affordance appears on hover, which has no equivalent on touch. Needs a persistent or long-press-triggered control on mobile.
- **Build button overlaps content on mobile.** The floating `BuildModeButton` is positioned for desktop hover interaction and covers page content or other controls at small viewport sizes.
- **Canvas too narrow on mobile.** Excess margin on the item canvas; should be full-width or near-full-width.
- **Close button is small and at the top of the canvas.** It exists but is easy to miss on mobile; worth considering a more prominent or thumb-reachable dismiss control.
- **Text wrapping on mobile.** Long lines don't wrap well; consider forcing word-wrap by default or adding a view-settings toggle.

**Missing features / gaps:**
- **Kanban drag-and-drop not implemented.** Board views have no DnD on any platform; items can't be moved between columns by dragging.
- ✅ **`@`-mention links are now tappable in read/share view** (fixed 2026-06-15). `markdown-render.ts` rewrites a mention's `ledgr://item/<id>` href to an in-app `<a href="/items/<id>" class="mention">`, so it's a real link on the print view and share page (was a hover-dead `.mention` span). A malformed/empty-id mention still flattens to a span so no broken anchor leaks. FTS text and the existing `.mention` styling are unchanged. `verify-print` updated + green; tsc + `next build` + eslint clean. *(Note: on a public share page the link targets the auth-gated `/items/<id>`, so an anonymous viewer hits the sign-in wall — the owner viewing their own print/items gets the intended jump. Revisit if cross-item navigation from a public share is ever wanted.)*
- **No undo button on the canvas toolbar.** Tiptap history is active (Ctrl+Z works on desktop) but there's no undo button in the toolbar itself — not accessible on mobile at all.

**Explorations opened (2026-06-14):** `explorations/mobile-swipe-navigation.md`, `explorations/notes-organization.md`, `explorations/meeting-recording.md`, `explorations/canvas-drag-and-drop.md`, `explorations/rich-export-and-theming.md`.

**Build surface follow-up (2026-06-14) — custom properties on system types:**
The infrastructure for this already exists: `updateType` allows editing system types (adding properties is explicitly permitted in the code comment), and the `TypeBuilder` already shows "System type — can be extended, not deleted" when you open one via `/build/types/[key]/edit`. What's missing is **discoverability and framing**:
- The types list in `/build/types` should make it clearer that system types are editable (an "Add property" shortcut or a more inviting edit link, not just a greyed-out system badge).
- In the property editor for a system type, distinguish between **built-in properties** (connected to plumbing — Todoist sync, calendar fields, etc.) and **custom properties** (organizational only — sorting, grouping, filtering). A visual divider or a label like "Custom properties" below the system ones would set the right expectation.
- No schema change needed — custom properties on system types ride `properties` jsonb the same way they do on user types.
